/* design:
 - single state object holds cfg/env/abort/log/items/seen/groupBest/ui/lb/crawl
 - core pipeline: collect -> dedup -> render queue; UI is createShell/render/bind
 - abort/cleanup/finalize centralized; finalize is idempotent; UI cleanup separate
 - crawl is bounded, same-site, abortable; HTML/script/bg scans only when needed
*/

 * rev3.0.3 remains in the archived source. Preserve all active features,
 * dormant acquisition paths, Safari fallbacks and acceptance tests.

 */

const ROOT_ID = '__fmp_root__';
const FMP_ACTIVE_ATTR = 'data-nf-img-view-active';
const FMP_RUNTIME_KEY = '__FMP_RUNTIME__';
const FMP_STATUS_KEY = '__FMP_STATUS__';
const DEBUG_GLOBAL_KEYS = [
  '__FMP_RUNTIME__',
  '__FMP_STATUS__',
  '__FMP_ABORT__',
  '__FMP_ABORT_CONTROLLERS__',
  '__FMP_ITEMS__',
  '__FMP_SEEN__',
  '__FMP_SEEN_ORDER__',
  '__FMP_GROUP_BEST__',
  '__FMP_FILTER__',
  '__FMP_STOP_CRAWL__',
  '__FMP_SET_FILTER__',
  '__FMP_APPLY_FILTER_SORT__',
  '__FMP_APPLY_ERROR_VIS__',
  '__FMP_PERF__',
  '__FMP_LB_OPEN__'
];

function clearDebugGlobals() {
  DEBUG_GLOBAL_KEYS.forEach(function(key) {
    try { window[key] = null; } catch (e) {}
    try { delete window[key]; } catch (e) {}
  });
}

function perfEnabled() {
  return !!(state && state.perf && state.perf.enabled && state.cfg && state.cfg.perfEnabled);
}

function perfNow() {
  try {
    if (typeof performance !== 'undefined' && performance && typeof performance.now === 'function') {
      return performance.now();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return Date.now();
}

function perfCount(name, amount) {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return;
  const key = String(name || '');
  if (!key) return;
  const n = Number(amount);
  const delta = typeof n === 'number' && isFinite(n) ? n : 1;
  state.perf.counts[key] = (state.perf.counts[key] || 0) + delta;
}

function perfMeasureSourceEl(item) {
  if (!perfEnabled() || !item || !item.__sourceEl) return;
  if (!item.__sourceElMetricRetained) {
    item.__sourceElMetricRetained = true;
    perfCount('sourceElRetained');
  }
  let status = 'connected';
  try {
    if (item.__sourceEl.isConnected === false) status = 'detached';
  } catch (e) {}
  if (item.__sourceElMetricStatus === status) return;
  item.__sourceElMetricStatus = status;
  perfCount(status === 'connected' ? 'sourceElConnected' : 'sourceElDetached');
}

function releaseSourceEl(item, reason) {
  if (!state.cfg.releaseSourceEl || !item) return;
  item.__thumbSourceFacts = null;
  if (!item.__sourceEl) return;
  item.__sourceEl = null;
  item.__sourceElMetricStatus = '';
  perfCount('sourceElReleased');
}

function perfMark(name) {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return;
  const key = String(name || '');
  if (!key) return;
  state.perf.marks[key] = perfNow();
}

function perfMarkOnce(name) {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return;
  const key = String(name || '');
  if (!key || Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(state.perf.marks, key)) return;
  state.perf.marks[key] = perfNow();
}

function perfDuration(name, start) {
  if (!perfEnabled() || typeof start !== 'number') return;
  const key = String(name || '');
  if (!key) return;
  const elapsed = Math.max(0, perfNow() - start);
  state.perf.durations[key] = (state.perf.durations[key] || 0) + elapsed;
}

function perfStart(name) {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return null;
  perfCount(name + '.start');
  perfMark(name + '.start');
  return perfNow();
}

function perfEnd(name, start) {
  if (!perfEnabled() || typeof start !== 'number') return;
  perfCount(name + '.end');
  perfMark(name + '.end');
  perfDuration(name, start);
}

function perfGauge(name, value) {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return;
  const key = String(name || '');
  if (!key) return;
  state.perf.gauges[key] = value;
}

function getPerfSnapshot() {
  if (!perfEnabled()) return null;
  const gauges = Object.assign({}, state.perf.gauges);
  gauges.activeCtx = {
    thumb: state.thumb && state.thumb.inFlight ? state.thumb.inFlight : 0,
    crawl: state.crawl && state.crawl.inFlight ? state.crawl.inFlight : 0,
    lightbox: state.lb && state.lb.__ctx ? 1 : 0
  };
  gauges.queue = {
    thumb: typeof thumbQueueLength === 'function' ? thumbQueueLength() : 0,
    crawl: typeof crawlQueueLength === 'function' ? crawlQueueLength() : 0
  };
  gauges.inFlight = {
    thumb: state.thumb && state.thumb.inFlight ? state.thumb.inFlight : 0,
    crawl: state.crawl && state.crawl.inFlight ? state.crawl.inFlight : 0
  };
  return {
    enabled: true,
    counts: Object.assign({}, state.perf.counts),
    marks: Object.assign({}, state.perf.marks),
    durations: Object.assign({}, state.perf.durations),
    gauges: gauges
  };
}

function setActiveMarker(active) {
  try {
    const html = document.documentElement;
    if (!html) return;
    if (active) html.setAttribute(FMP_ACTIVE_ATTR, '1');
    else html.removeAttribute(FMP_ACTIVE_ATTR);
  } catch (e) {}
}

function getRuntimeStats() {
  const crawl = state && state.crawl ? state.crawl : {};
  const items = state && state.items ? state.items : {};
  const thumb = state && state.thumb ? state.thumb : {};
  const lb = state && state.lb ? state.lb : {};
  const stats = {
    name: SCRIPT_NAME,
    rev: SCRIPT_REV,
    active: !!document.getElementById(ROOT_ID),
    running: !!window.__FMP_RUNNING__,
    aborted: !!(state && state.abort && state.abort.aborted),
    items: items.list ? items.list.length : 0,
    errors: items.errorCount || 0,
    noise: items.noiseCount || 0,
    crawlStarted: !!crawl.started,
    crawlDone: !!crawl.done,
    crawlVisited: crawl.visited ? crawl.visited.size : 0,
    crawlQueue: typeof crawlQueueLength === 'function' ? crawlQueueLength() : 0,
    crawlInFlight: crawl.inFlight || 0,
    thumbQueue: typeof thumbQueueLength === 'function' ? thumbQueueLength() : 0,
    thumbInFlight: thumb.inFlight || 0,
    lightboxOpen: !!lb.open
  };
  if (perfEnabled()) stats.perf = getPerfSnapshot();
  return stats;
}

function isRunCurrent() {
  if (!state || !state.runToken || (state.abort && state.abort.aborted)) return false;
  try {
    const runtime = window[FMP_RUNTIME_KEY];
    if (!runtime || runtime.runToken !== state.runToken) return false;
    const root = document.getElementById(ROOT_ID);
    return !!root && (!state.ui || !state.ui.root || state.ui.root === root);
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function publishRuntime() {
  try {
    const runtime = state.runtime || {};
    runtime.name = SCRIPT_NAME;
    runtime.rev = SCRIPT_REV;
    runtime.runToken = state.runToken;
    runtime.isActive = function() {
      return !!document.getElementById(ROOT_ID);
    };
    runtime.isRunning = function() {
      return !!window.__FMP_RUNNING__;
    };
    runtime.getStats = getRuntimeStats;
    runtime.dispose = function() {
      try { abortAll('runtime-dispose'); } catch (e) {}
      try { cleanupUi(); } catch (e) {}
      try { finalize('ok', { reason: 'runtime-dispose' }); } catch (e) {}
    };
    state.runtime = runtime;
    window[FMP_RUNTIME_KEY] = runtime;
    window[FMP_STATUS_KEY] = getRuntimeStats();
  } catch (e) {}
}

function publishDebugGlobals() {
  window.__FMP_ABORT__ = state.abort;
  window.__FMP_ABORT_CONTROLLERS__ = state.abort.controllers;
  window.__FMP_ITEMS__ = state.items.list;
  window.__FMP_SEEN__ = state.seen.map;
  window.__FMP_SEEN_ORDER__ = state.seen.order;
  window.__FMP_GROUP_BEST__ = state.groupBest;
  window.__FMP_FILTER__ = state.filter;
  window.__FMP_STOP_CRAWL__ = abortAll;
  window.__FMP_SET_FILTER__ = setFilter;
  window.__FMP_APPLY_FILTER_SORT__ = applyFilterSort;
  window.__FMP_APPLY_ERROR_VIS__ = applyErrorVis;
  if (perfEnabled()) window.__FMP_PERF__ = state.perf;
  window.__FMP_LB_OPEN__ = function(idOrIndex) {
    if (typeof idOrIndex === 'number') {
      buildLightboxList();
      if (idOrIndex >= 0 && idOrIndex < state.lb.list.length) {
        state.lb.index = idOrIndex;
        renderLightboxItem();
        state.lb.overlay.classList.add('open');
        state.lb.open = true;
        if (state.thumb) state.thumb.paused = true;
      }
    } else if (typeof idOrIndex === 'string') {
      openLightboxById(idOrIndex);
    }
  };
}

function disconnectThumbObserver() {
  if (!state.thumb) return;
  if (state.thumb.observer) {
    try { state.thumb.observer.disconnect(); } catch (e) {}
    state.thumb.observer = null;
  }
}

function cleanupActiveThumbLoads(reason) {
  const why = reason || 'cleanup';
  try {
    if (state.items && state.items.list && state.items.list.length) {
      state.items.list.forEach(function(item) {
        if (!item) return;

        try { clearOverlayShowTimer(item); } catch (e) {}

        try {
          if (item.__thumbCtx && typeof item.__thumbCtx.abort === 'function') {
            item.__thumbCtx.abort(why);
          }
        } catch (e) {}

        try {
          const img = item.__thumbImg || (item.card && item.card.querySelector ? item.card.querySelector('img') : null);
          if (img) {
            img.onload = null;
            img.onerror = null;
            if (img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpRevealQueued = '0';
            if (img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpFitQueued = '0';
            if (img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpNudged = '0';
            try { img.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (e2) {}
          }
        } catch (e) {}

        try {
          if (item.card) hideThumbOverlay(item.card);
        } catch (e) {}

        item.__thumbCtx = null;
        item.__thumbLoading = false;
        item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
        releaseSourceEl(item, why);
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    if (state.thumb) {
      if (state.thumb.queued && typeof state.thumb.queued.clear === 'function') state.thumb.queued.clear();
      if (state.thumb.queuePri) state.thumb.queuePri.length = 0;
      if (state.thumb.queue) state.thumb.queue.length = 0;
      state.thumb.queuePriHead = 0;
      state.thumb.queueHead = 0;
      state.thumb.inFlight = 0;
      state.thumb.pumpScheduled = false;
      state.thumb.paused = false;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function cleanupLightboxLoad(reason) {
  try {
    if (typeof resetLightboxLoad === 'function') resetLightboxLoad(reason || 'cleanup');
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    if (state.lb) {
      state.lb.open = false;
      state.lb.index = -1;
      state.lb.list = [];
      state.lb.currentId = null;
      state.lb.__ctx = null;
      state.lb.__loading = false;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function cleanupExternalThumbItems(list, reason) {
  const why = reason || 'external-cleanup';
  if (!list || !list.length) return;
  try {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(list, function(item) {
      if (!item) return;

      try {
        if (item.__overlayShowTimer) {
          clearTimeout(item.__overlayShowTimer);
          item.__overlayShowTimer = 0;
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        if (item.__thumbCtx && typeof item.__thumbCtx.abort === 'function') {
          item.__thumbCtx.abort(why);
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        const card = item.card || null;
        const img = item.__thumbImg || (card && card.querySelector ? card.querySelector('img') : null);
        if (img) {
          img.onload = null;
          img.onerror = null;
          try { img.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (e2) {}
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      try {
        const card = item.card || null;
        if (card && card.querySelector) {
          const overlay = card.querySelector('.ph-load');
          if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      item.__thumbCtx = null;
      item.__thumbLoading = false;
      item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
    });
  } catch (e) {}
}

function cleanupExternalRootDom(root, reason) {
  if (!root || !root.querySelectorAll) return;
  try {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('.ph-load'), function(overlay) {
      if (!overlay) return;
      try {
        if (overlay.__hideTimer) {
          clearTimeout(overlay.__hideTimer);
          overlay.__hideTimer = 0;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
      } catch (e) {}
    });
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('img'), function(img) {
      if (!img) return;
      try { img.onload = null; } catch (e) {}
      try { img.onerror = null; } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (img.__fmpGpuRaf1) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(img.__fmpGpuRaf1);
          img.__fmpGpuRaf1 = 0;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (img.__fmpGpuRaf2) {
          cancelAnimationFrame(img.__fmpGpuRaf2);
          img.__fmpGpuRaf2 = 0;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      try { img.__fmpGpuActive = false; } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (img.classList) img.classList.remove('fmp-gpu');
      } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (img.dataset) {
          img.dataset.fmpRevealQueued = '0';
          img.dataset.fmpFitQueued = '0';
          img.dataset.fmpNudged = '0';
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      try { img.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (e) {}
    });
  } catch (e) {}

  try {
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll('video, iframe'), function(node) {
      if (!node) return;
      try { node.onload = null; } catch (e) {}
      try { node.onerror = null; } catch (e) {}
      try { node.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (e) {}
      try { node.removeAttribute('srcdoc'); } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (node.pause && typeof node.pause === 'function') node.pause();
      } catch (e) {}
      try {
        if (node.load && typeof node.load === 'function') node.load();
      } catch (e) {}
    });
  } catch (e) {}
}

function cleanupUi() {
  if (state.ui.disposed) return;
  state.ui.disposed = true;
  state.items.visualGroups.length=0;
  state.items.visualCount=0;
  state.items.visualCanvas=null;
  state.items.liveImageVisibility.clear();
  state.crawl.responsiveCss.clear();
  for (const it of state.items.list) {it.__visualGroup=null;it.__visualOrder=null;}
  if (state.items.renderRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(state.items.renderRaf);
  state.items.renderRaf = 0;
  if (state.thumb.pumpRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(state.thumb.pumpRaf);
  state.thumb.pumpRaf = 0;
  if (state.thumb.uiFlushRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(state.thumb.uiFlushRaf);
  state.thumb.uiFlushRaf = 0;
  state.thumb.uiFlushScheduled = false;
  const cleanupStartedAt = perfStart('cleanup');
  perfCount('cleanup.begin');
  disconnectMutationObserver();
  stopWatchdog();
  resetLightboxZoom();
  cleanupLightboxLoad('cleanup-ui');
  cleanupActiveThumbLoads('cleanup-ui');
  disconnectThumbObserver();
  if (state.thumb) {
    const grid = state.ui ? state.ui.grid : null;
    if (grid && state.thumb.onScroll) {
      try { grid.removeEventListener('scroll', state.thumb.onScroll); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (state.thumb.onResize) {
      try { window.removeEventListener('resize', state.thumb.onResize); } catch (e) {}
    }
    state.thumb.onScroll = null;
    state.thumb.onResize = null;
    if (state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer);
      state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer = 0;
    }
    if (state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer);
      state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer = 0;
    }
    state.thumb.pausedByScroll = false;
    state.thumb.scrollPauseUntil = 0;
    state.thumb.scrollPriorityRescueUsed = false;
    if (state.thumb.repaintRaf) {
      cancelAnimationFrame(state.thumb.repaintRaf);
      state.thumb.repaintRaf = 0;
    }
    if (state.thumb.repaintSet) state.thumb.repaintSet.clear();
    state.thumb.repaintSet = null;
    if (state.thumb.uiQueue) state.thumb.uiQueue.length = 0;
    state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
    if (state.thumb.queued && typeof state.thumb.queued.clear === 'function') state.thumb.queued.clear();
    if (state.thumb.queuePri) state.thumb.queuePri.length = 0;
    if (state.thumb.queue) state.thumb.queue.length = 0;
    state.thumb.queuePriHead = 0;
    state.thumb.queueHead = 0;
    state.thumb.inFlight = 0;
    state.thumb.scanNow = null;
    state.thumb.__inited = false;
  }
  if (state.items && state.items.renderQueue) {
    state.items.renderQueue.length = 0;
    state.items.renderQueueHead = 0;
    state.items.renderScheduled = false;
  }
  if (state.items && state.items.filterSortTimer) {
    clearTimeout(state.items.filterSortTimer);
    state.items.filterSortTimer = 0;
  }
  if (state.ui.progressThrottleTimer) {
    clearTimeout(state.ui.progressThrottleTimer);
    state.ui.progressThrottleTimer = 0;
  }
  if (state.ui.infoThrottleTimer) {
    clearTimeout(state.ui.infoThrottleTimer);
    state.ui.infoThrottleTimer = 0;
  }
  if (state.ui.progressTimer) {
    clearTimeout(state.ui.progressTimer);
    state.ui.progressTimer = 0;
  }
  if (state.ui.infoTimer) { clearTimeout(state.ui.infoTimer); state.ui.infoTimer = 0; }
  if (state.ui.logFlashTimer) { clearTimeout(state.ui.logFlashTimer); state.ui.logFlashTimer = 0; }
  if (state.ui.boundVis) {
    if (state.ui.onVisibilityChange) {
      try { document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', state.ui.onVisibilityChange); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (state.ui.onPageShow) {
      try { window.removeEventListener('pageshow', state.ui.onPageShow); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (state.ui.onFocus) {
      try { window.removeEventListener('focus', state.ui.onFocus); } catch (e) {}
    }
    state.ui.boundVis = false;
  }
  state.ui.onVisibilityChange = null;
  state.ui.onPageShow = null;
  state.ui.onFocus = null;
  unlockPage(state);
  if (state.ui.root && state.ui.root.parentNode) {
    state.ui.root.parentNode.removeChild(state.ui.root);
  }
  state.ui.root = null;
  setActiveMarker(false);
  perfMarkOnce('cleanup.end');
  perfEnd('cleanup', cleanupStartedAt);
  clearDebugGlobals();
}

function guardAndCleanup() {
  try {
    const previousRuntime = window[FMP_RUNTIME_KEY];
    if (previousRuntime && typeof previousRuntime.dispose === 'function') {
      previousRuntime.dispose();
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    const root = document.getElementById(ROOT_ID);
    const htmlLocked = document.documentElement && document.documentElement.style.overflow === 'hidden';
    const bodyLocked = document.body && document.body.style.overflow === 'hidden';
    const htmlMarked = document.documentElement && document.documentElement.dataset && document.documentElement.dataset.fmpLocked === '1';
    const bodyMarked = document.body && document.body.dataset && document.body.dataset.fmpLocked === '1';
    if (!root && (htmlLocked || bodyLocked) && (htmlMarked || bodyMarked)) {
      unlockPage(state);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (window.__FMP_ITEMS__) {
      cleanupExternalThumbItems(window.__FMP_ITEMS__, 'restart-external');
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (typeof window.__FMP_STOP_CRAWL__ === 'function') {
      window.__FMP_STOP_CRAWL__('restart');
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    cleanupLightboxLoad('restart');
    cleanupActiveThumbLoads('restart');
  } catch (e) {}
  const old = document.getElementById(ROOT_ID);
  if (old) {
    try {
      cleanupExternalRootDom(old, 'restart-root');
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (old && old.parentNode) old.parentNode.removeChild(old);
  window.__FMP_SCRIPT_NAME__ = SCRIPT_NAME;
  window.__FMP_SCRIPT_REV__ = SCRIPT_REV;
  window.__FMP_RUNNING__ = true;
  setActiveMarker(true);
  publishRuntime();
}

let finalizeOnce = false;
function finalize(status, detail) {
  if (finalizeOnce) return;
  finalizeOnce = true;
  window.__FMP_RUNNING__ = false;
  try {
    if (window[FMP_RUNTIME_KEY]) publishRuntime();
  } catch (e) {}
  const reason = detail && detail.reason ? String(detail.reason) : '';
  const terminalCleanup = status === 'error' || reason === 'closed' || reason === 'runtime-dispose';
  if (terminalCleanup) {
    try { abortAll(reason || status); } catch (e) {}
    disconnectMutationObserver();
    stopWatchdog();
  }
  updateProgress('idle', status);
  publishPayloadCompletion(status, detail || {});
}

/* [01] env/device判定 */
function detectHighHzOnce() {
  try {
    if (!state.env) return;
    state.env.isHighHz = false;
    if (typeof requestAnimationFrame !== 'function') return;

    const samples = [];
    const targetSamples = 24;
    let prevTs = 0;
    let done = false;

    const finish = function() {
      if (done) return;
      done = true;
      try {
        if (!state.env) return;
        if (!samples.length) {
          state.env.isHighHz = false;
          return;
        }
        const sorted = samples.slice().sort(function(a, b) { return a - b; });
        const mid = Math.floor(sorted.length / 2);
        const median = (sorted.length % 2)
          ? sorted[mid]
          : ((sorted[mid - 1] + sorted[mid]) / 2);
        state.env.isHighHz = median < 11;
      } catch (e) {
        try { if (state.env) state.env.isHighHz = false; } catch (e2) {}
      }
      try {
        if (state.thumb && !state.thumb.paused) thumbPump();
      } catch (e) {}
    };

    const step = function(ts) {
      try {
        if (prevTs > 0) {
          const dt = ts - prevTs;
          if (dt > 0 && dt < 1000) samples.push(dt);
        }
        prevTs = ts;
        if (samples.length >= targetSamples) {
          finish();
          return;
        }
        requestAnimationFrame(step);
      } catch (e) {
        finish();
      }
    };

    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  } catch (e) {
    try { if (state.env) state.env.isHighHz = false; } catch (e2) {}
  }
}

function initEnv() {
  const ua = navigator.userAgent || '';
  const isIOSLike = /iP(hone|ad|od)/.test(ua) || (navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && navigator.maxTouchPoints > 1);
  const minSide = Math.min(screen.width || 0, screen.height || 0);
  const isIPad = isIOSLike && (/(iPad)/.test(ua) || minSide >= 768);
  const isIPhone = isIOSLike && !isIPad;
  const vv = window.visualViewport || null;
  const vw = vv ? vv.width : (window.innerWidth || minSide);
  const isWide = vw >= 900;
  state.env = {
    ua: ua,
    isIOS: isIOSLike,
    isIPad: isIPad,
    isIPhone: isIPhone,
    vv: vv,
    isWide: isWide,
    isHighHz: false,
    thumbBoxPx: isWide ? 180 : 140,
    dpr: window.devicePixelRatio || 1,
    href: location.href,
    host: location.hostname,
    origin: location.origin
  };
  if (isIPhone) detectHighHzOnce();
}

/* [03] logger */
function initLogger() {
  state.log.push = function(level, msg) {
    const time = new Date().toISOString();
    const line = `[${time}] ${level}: ${String(msg)}`;
    state.log.ring.push(line);
    if (state.log.ring.length > state.log.max) state.log.ring.shift();
    if (state.cfg.debug) console.log(line);
  };
  state.log.copy = async function() {
    const ts = new Date().toISOString();
    const stats = collectVisibilityStats(80);
    const visible = stats.visible;
    const hidden = Math.max(0, state.items.list.length - visible);
    const reasonSummary = formatHideReasonCounts(stats.counts, 8);
    const summary = [
      '=== img view b64 log ===',
      'time: ' + ts,
      'href: ' + location.href,
      'host: ' + location.hostname,
      'items: ' + state.items.list.length,
      'visible: ' + visible,
      'hidden: ' + hidden,
      'error: ' + state.items.errorCount,
      'filter: ' + (state.filter && state.filter.key ? state.filter.key : ''),
      'hidden_reason: ' + (reasonSummary || '(none)')
    ].join('\n');
    const ring = state.log.ring.length ? state.log.ring.join('\n') : '(none)';
    const list = getVisibleItems(60);
    const listText = list.length ? list.map(function(u, i) { return (i + 1) + '. ' + u; }).join('\n') : '(none)';
    const reasonLines = getHideReasonCountLines(stats.counts, 20);
    const reasonText = reasonLines.length ? reasonLines.join('\n') : '(none)';
    const hiddenItems = getHiddenItemsWithReason(60);
    const hiddenText = hiddenItems.length ? hiddenItems.join('\n') : '(none)';
    const failed = getFailedItems(20);
    const failedText = failed.length ? failed.map(function(u, i) { return (i + 1) + '. ' + u; }).join('\n') : '(none)';
    const text = [
      summary,
      '',
      '[log]',
      ring,
      '',
      '[hidden reason counts]',
      reasonText,
      '',
      '[visible items]',
      listText,
      '',
      '[hidden items]',
      hiddenText,
      '',
      '[failed items]',
      failedText
    ].join('\n');
    let ok = false;
    try {
      ok = await copyText(text);
    } catch (e) {
      ok = false;
    }
    if (ok) {
      flashLogButton();
      setInfoTemp('コピー成功', 900);
    } else {
      setInfoTemp('共有/モーダルを使用', 1400);
    }
  };
}

/* [04] abort */
function makeController() {
  const c = new AbortController();
  state.abort.controllers.add(c);
  return c;
}

function abortAll(reason) {
  if (state.abort.aborted) return;
  state.abort.aborted = true;
  state.abort.reason = reason || 'abort';
  perfCount('abort');
  disconnectMutationObserver();
  stopWatchdog();
  state.crawl.stop = true;
  if (state.crawl.timer) {
    clearTimeout(state.crawl.timer);
    state.crawl.timer = 0;
  }
  state.log.push('abort', 'reason=' + state.abort.reason + ' visited=' + state.crawl.visited.size + ' inFlight=' + state.crawl.inFlight + ' errors=' + state.items.errorCount);
  state.abort.controllers.forEach(function(c) {
    try { c.abort(); } catch (e) {}
  });
  state.abort.controllers.clear();
  updateProgress('idle', 'abort');
}

/* [05] url/canon */
const IMG_EXT = /\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp)(\?|#|$)/i;
const VID_EXT = /\.(mp4|webm|mov|m4v|ogv)(\?|#|$)/i;

function hasExcludedWord(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  let path = String(u);
  try { path = new URL(path, location.href).pathname; } catch (e) {}
  try { path = decodeURIComponent(path); } catch (e) {}
  const tokens = path.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1 $2').toLowerCase().split(/[^a-z0-9]+/);
  const excluded = /^(?:favicon|sprite|icon|logo|avatar|emoji|badge|spinner|loading|pixel|spacer)s?\d*$/;
  return tokens.some(function(token) { return excluded.test(token); }) ||
    /(?:^|[\/_-])apple-touch-icon(?:[\/_\-.]|$)/i.test(path);
}

function isImageResourceLink(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  try {
    const p = new URL(String(u), location.href);
    return /\.(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp|svg|ico)$/i.test(p.pathname) || isPbsMediaImageUrl(u);
  } catch (e) { return false; }
}

function isImageUrl(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  const s = String(u).trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  if (/^data:/i.test(s)) return false;
  if (hasExcludedWord(s) && !getImgurDirectMedia(s)) return false;
  if (/\.svg(\?|#|$)/i.test(s)) return false;
  return IMG_EXT.test(s);
}

function isPbsMediaImageUrl(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  let x;
  try { x = new URL(String(u), location.href); } catch (e) { return false; }
  if ((x.hostname || '').toLowerCase() !== 'pbs.twimg.com') return false;
  if (!/^\/media\/[^/?#]+/i.test(x.pathname || '')) return false;
  const fmt = (x.searchParams.get('format') || '').toLowerCase();
  return /^(jpe?g|png|gif|webp|avif|bmp)$/.test(fmt);
}

function isDirectImageUrl(u) {
  return isImageUrl(u) || isPbsMediaImageUrl(u);
}

function isImgTagCandidateUrl(u) {
  if (!u) return false;
  const s = String(u).trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  if (/^data:/i.test(s)) return false;
  if (hasExcludedWord(s) && !getImgurDirectMedia(s)) return false;
  if (/\.svg(\?|#|$)/i.test(s)) return false;
  return /^https?:/i.test(s) || /^blob:/i.test(s);
}

function isVideoUrl(u) {
  return /^data:video\//i.test(u) || VID_EXT.test(u) || (/\.gifv(?:[?#]|$)/i.test(u) && !!getImgurDirectMedia(u));
}

function stripSizeSuffix(path) {
  let p = path;
  p = p.replace(/-\d{2,4}x\d{2,4}(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i, '');
  p = p.replace(/-(s|m|t)(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i, '');
  p = p.replace(/@2x(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i, '');
  p = p.replace(/\._[A-Z0-9,-]+_(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i, '');
  return p;
}

function resolveMediaReference(raw, base) {
  const value = String(raw || '').trim();
  if (!value) return '';
  if (/^(blob:|data:)/i.test(value)) return value;
  try {
    const u = new URL(value, base || location.href);
    if (!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)) return '';
    u.hash = '';
    return u.href;
  } catch (e) { return ''; }
}

function getMediaDocumentBase(doc, fallback) {
  const base = fallback || location.href;
  const owner = doc && (doc.nodeType === 9 ? doc : doc.ownerDocument);
  if (!owner) return base;
  const tag = owner.querySelector('base[href]');
  return tag ? (resolveMediaReference(tag.getAttribute('href'), base) || base) : base;
}

function normalizeUrl(raw, base) {
  if (!raw) return null;
  const s = String(raw).trim();
  if (!s) return null;
  if (s.startsWith('data:') || s.startsWith('blob:')) {
    const key = s.slice(0, 64) + ':' + s.length;
    return { abs: s, canon: s, groupKey: key, host: '' };
  }
  let u;
  try {
    u = new URL(s, base || location.href);
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
  const unwrapKeys = ['url', 'u', 'img', 'image', 'media'];
  let unwrapped = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < unwrapKeys.length; i += 1) {
    const key = unwrapKeys[i];
    const rawVal = u.searchParams.get(key);
    if (!rawVal) continue;
    const direct = rawVal.trim();
    if (/^https?:/i.test(direct)) { unwrapped = direct; break; }
    try {
      const decoded = decodeURIComponent(direct);
      if (/^https?:/i.test(decoded)) { unwrapped = decoded; break; }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (!unwrapped) {
    const hostNow = (u.hostname || '').toLowerCase();
    if (/^resize\.blogsys\.jp$/.test(hostNow)) {
      const joined = String((u.pathname || '') + (u.search || ''));
      let m = /(https?:\/\/.+)$/i.exec(joined);
      if (!m) {
        try {
          const decodedJoined = decodeURIComponent(joined);
          m = /(https?:\/\/.+)$/i.exec(decodedJoined);
        } catch (e) {}
      }
      if (m && m[1]) unwrapped = m[1];
    }
  }
  if (unwrapped) {
    try {
      u = new URL(unwrapped, base || location.href);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)) return null;
  u.hash = '';
  const sourceAbs = u.href;
  let host = u.hostname.toLowerCase();
  if (host === 'livedoor.sp.blogimg.jp') {
    host = 'livedoor.blogimg.jp';
    u.hostname = host;
  } else if (host.startsWith('sp.')) {
    host = host.replace(/^sp\./, '');
    u.hostname = host;
  }
  let path = u.pathname;
  if (/blogimg/.test(host)) {
    path = path.replace(/\/_thumbs?\//, '/');
    path = path.replace(/\/s\//, '/');
    path = path.replace(/\/resize\/\d+x\d+\//i, '/');
    path = path.replace(/\/thumbs?\/\d+x\d+\//i, '/');
  }
  path = stripSizeSuffix(path);
  u.pathname = path;

  const params = new URLSearchParams(u.search);
  const trackKeys = ['utm_source', 'utm_medium', 'utm_campaign', 'utm_term', 'utm_content', 'fbclid', 'gclid', 'igshid', 'ref', 'spm', '_t'];
  trackKeys.forEach(function(k) { params.delete(k); });

  const sizeKeys = ['w', 'h', 'width', 'height', 'resize', 'fit', 'quality', 'q', 'auto', 'crop', 'dpr'];
  const canonParams = new URLSearchParams();
  params.forEach(function(v, k) {
    if (sizeKeys.indexOf(k) === -1) canonParams.set(k, v);
  });

  const groupParams = new URLSearchParams();
  params.forEach(function(v, k) {
    if (['id', 'photo', 'photo_id', 'image', 'img', 'media', 'v'].indexOf(k) !== -1) {
      groupParams.set(k, v);
    }
  });

  u.search = canonParams.toString() ? '?' + canonParams.toString() : '';
  const canon = u.toString();
  const hasExt = /\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp|mp4|webm|mov|m4v)$/i.test(path);
  const groupKey = host + path + (hasExt ? '' : (groupParams.toString() ? '?' + groupParams.toString() : ''));
  return { abs: u.toString(), canon: canon, groupKey: groupKey, host: host, sourceAbs: sourceAbs };
}

function isLivedoorArticle(url) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, location.href);
    if (!/\/archives\/\d+\.html$/i.test(u.pathname || '')) return false;
    const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase();
    if (host === 'blog.livedoor.jp') return true;
    return host === location.hostname.toLowerCase();
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function computePageLabel(url) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, location.href);
    const p = u.searchParams.get('p') || u.searchParams.get('page');
    const num = p ? parseInt(p, 10) : 1;
    if (isLivedoorArticle(u.toString())) return 'P' + (num > 0 ? num : 1);
    if (p && num > 0) return 'P' + num;
  } catch (e) {}
  return 'P1';
}

function legacyCanonicalMediaKey(absUrl, kind) {
  if (!absUrl) return '';
  if (absUrl.startsWith('data:') || absUrl.startsWith('blob:')) return absUrl;
  let u;
  try {
    u = new URL(absUrl, location.href);
  } catch (e) {
    return absUrl;
  }
  u.hash = '';
  let host = u.hostname.toLowerCase();
  if (/^livedoor\.\d+\.blogimg\.jp$/.test(host) || host === 'livedoor.sp.blogimg.jp') {
    host = 'livedoor.blogimg.jp';
    u.hostname = host;
  }
  if (host === 'pbs.twimg.com') {
    return u.origin + u.pathname;
  }
  if (host === 'i.imgur.com') {
    // Five/seven-character full IDs must never lose their final letter.
    u.pathname = u.pathname.replace(/^(\/(?:[A-Za-z0-9]{5}|[A-Za-z0-9]{7}))[sbtmlh](\.(?:jpe?g|png|gif|webp|avif|bmp))$/i, '$1$2');
  }
  if (host === 'imgs.minkch.com') {
    u.pathname = (u.pathname || '').replace(/^(\/imgs\/(?:d)?\d{8,14}\/\d+)([smt])(\.[a-z0-9]+)$/i, '$1$3');
  }
  if (/blogimg\.jp$/.test(host)) {
    let p = u.pathname || '';
    p = p.replace(/\/_thumbs?\//, '/');
    p = p.replace(/\/s\//, '/');
    p = p.replace(/\/resize\/\d+x\d+\//i, '/');
    p = p.replace(/\/thumbs?\/\d+x\d+\//i, '/');
    p = stripSizeSuffix(p);
    u.pathname = p;
  }
  const params = new URLSearchParams(u.search);
  const dropExact = ['fbclid', 'gclid', 'ref', 'spm', 'mc_cid', 'mc_eid'];
  const dropSize = ['w', 'h', 'width', 'height', 'resize', 'fit', 'quality', 'q', 'dpr', 'auto', 'crop', 'name', 'sz'];
  const next = new URLSearchParams();
  params.forEach(function(v, k) {
    const key = k.toLowerCase();
    if (key.startsWith('utm_')) return;
    if (dropExact.indexOf(key) !== -1) return;
    if (dropSize.indexOf(key) !== -1 && !(key === 'name' && !/\.[a-z0-9]+$/i.test(u.pathname))) return;
    next.set(k, v);
  });
  u.search = next.toString() ? '?' + next.toString() : '';
  return u.origin + u.pathname + u.search;
}

function isKnownImageVariantHost(host) {
  return /(?:^|\.)blogimg\.jp$/i.test(host) || host === 'resize.blogsys.jp' ||
    host === 'pbs.twimg.com' || host === 'i.imgur.com' || host === 'imgs.minkch.com';
}

function canonicalMediaKey(absUrl, kind) {
  if (kind !== 'img' && kind !== 'video') return legacyCanonicalMediaKey(absUrl, kind);
  if (!absUrl || /^(?:data:|blob:)/i.test(absUrl)) return absUrl || '';
  try {
    const u = new URL(absUrl, location.href);
    u.hash = '';
    if (kind === 'img' && isKnownImageVariantHost(u.hostname.toLowerCase())) {
      const norm = normalizeUrl(u.href, location.href);
      return legacyCanonicalMediaKey(norm ? norm.abs : u.href, kind);
    }
    // URL suffixes, arbitrary query keys and parameter order can encode
    // different content. Without known host or same-slot evidence, keep them.
    return u.href;
  } catch (e) { return absUrl; }
}

function sameDeclaredRendition(a,b) {
  if (!a || !b || a===b) return false;
  try {
    const u=new URL(a,location.href),v=new URL(b,location.href);
    if (u.origin!==v.origin || !/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)) return false;
    const shape = p=>p.replace(/-\d{2,4}x\d{2,4}(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i,'').replace(/@2x(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i,'');
    if (shape(u.pathname)!==shape(v.pathname)) return false;
    const sizeOnly = x => {
      const p=new URLSearchParams(x.search);
      for (const k of ['w','h','width','height','dpr']) {
        const values=p.getAll(k);
        if (values.length===1 && /^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(values[0]) && Number(values[0])>0) p.delete(k);
      }
      return p.toString();
    };
    return sizeOnly(u)===sizeOnly(v) && (u.pathname!==v.pathname || u.search!==v.search);
  } catch(e) { return false; }
}

function getDeclaredOriginalIdentity(img, url, base) {
  if (!img || !img.closest) return url;
  const link = img.closest('a[href]');
  if (!link || link.querySelector('img') !== img || link.querySelectorAll('img').length !== 1) return url;
  const original = resolveMediaReference(link.getAttribute('href'), base);
  if (!original || original === url || !isDirectImageUrl(original)) return url;
  if (sameDeclaredRendition(url,original)) return original;
  const a = normalizeUrl(url, base), b = normalizeUrl(original, base);
  // Only link-related candidates that matched the existing variant rule.
  // Never infer equivalence between independent images or arbitrary links.
  if (a && b && legacyCanonicalMediaKey(a.abs,'img') === legacyCanonicalMediaKey(b.abs,'img')) {
    try {
      const ua = new URL(url, base), ub = new URL(original, base);
      if (isKnownImageVariantHost(ua.hostname.toLowerCase()) && isKnownImageVariantHost(ub.hostname.toLowerCase()) &&
          canonicalMediaKey(ua.href,'img') === canonicalMediaKey(ub.href,'img')) return original;
      if (ua.origin === ub.origin && ua.search === ub.search && stripSizeSuffix(ua.pathname) === stripSizeSuffix(ub.pathname)) return original;
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return url;
}

function pickBestFromSrcset(srcset) {
  if (!srcset) return '';
  const parts = srcset.split(',');
  let bestUrl = '';
  let bestScore = -1;
  parts.forEach(function(part) {
    const seg = part.trim().split(/\s+/);
    if (!seg[0]) return;
    let score = 1;
    if (seg[1]) {
      if (seg[1].endsWith('w')) score = parseInt(seg[1], 10) || 1;
      if (seg[1].endsWith('x')) score = (parseFloat(seg[1]) || 1) * 1000;
    }
    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestUrl = seg[0];
    }
  });
  return bestUrl;
}

/* [06] score/filter */
function scoreItem(item) {
  const area = item.width && item.height ? item.width * item.height : 0;
  let score = Math.log(area + 1);
  if (item.kind === 'video') score += 2;
  if (item.scope === 'body') score += 1;
  if (item.source === 'crawl') score += 0.5;
  if (/\.avif$/i.test(item.url)) score += 0.2;
  item.area = area;
  return score;
}

function imageVariantQuality(url) {
  if (!url) return 0;
  let score = 0;
  try {
    const u = new URL(String(url), location.href);
    const host = (u.hostname || '').toLowerCase();
    const path = (u.pathname || '').toLowerCase();
    if (/^resize\.blogsys\.jp$/.test(host)) score -= 30;
    const sizeTag = /-([smt])(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i.exec(path);
    if (!sizeTag) score += 30;
    else if (sizeTag[1].toLowerCase() === 'm') score += 20;
    else if (sizeTag[1].toLowerCase() === 's') score += 10;
    else score += 5;
    const dimsInPath = /-(\d{2,4})x(\d{2,4})(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i.exec(path);
    if (dimsInPath) {
      const dw = parseInt(dimsInPath[1] || '0', 10) || 0;
      const dh = parseInt(dimsInPath[2] || '0', 10) || 0;
      if (dw && dh) score += Math.min((dw * dh) / 50000, 20);
    }
    const params = new URLSearchParams(u.search);
    const qw = parseInt(params.get('w') || params.get('width') || '0', 10) || 0;
    const qh = parseInt(params.get('h') || params.get('height') || '0', 10) || 0;
    if (qw && qh) score += Math.min((qw * qh) / 50000, 20);
    if (qw || qh) score -= 6;
  } catch (e) {
    const s = String(url).toLowerCase();
    const sizeTag = /-([smt])(?=\.[a-z0-9]+($|[?#]))/i.exec(s);
    if (!sizeTag) score += 30;
    else if (sizeTag[1].toLowerCase() === 'm') score += 20;
    else if (sizeTag[1].toLowerCase() === 's') score += 10;
    else score += 5;
  }
  return score;
}

function imageQualityScore(item) {
  if (!item || item.kind !== 'img') return -999999;
  let best = -999999;
  const refs = [item.openUrl, item.rawUrl, item.abs, item.url];
  refs.forEach(function(u) {
    const q = imageVariantQuality(u);
    if (q > best) best = q;
  });
  if (item.width && item.height) {
    best += Math.min((item.width * item.height) / 80000, 10);
  }
  return best;
}

function isHigherQualityImageCandidate(candidate, existing) {
  if (!candidate || !existing) return false;
  if (candidate.kind !== 'img' || existing.kind !== 'img') return false;
  const q1 = imageQualityScore(candidate);
  const q2 = imageQualityScore(existing);
  if (q1 !== q2) return q1 > q2;
  return String(candidate.openUrl || '').length > String(existing.openUrl || '').length;
}

function passesSize(item) {
  if (!item.width || !item.height) return true;
  if (item.width < state.cfg.minWidth || item.height < state.cfg.minHeight) return false;
  if (item.width * item.height < state.cfg.minArea) return false;
  return true;
}

function matchesFilterKey(item, key) {
  const k = key || 'ALL';
  if (k === 'ALL') return true;
  if (k === 'BODY') return item.scope === 'body';
  if (k === 'IMG') return item.kind === 'img';
  if (k === 'VIDEO') return item.kind === 'video';
  if (k === 'X') return item.kind === 'embed' && item.embedType === 'X';
  if (k === 'INSTA') return item.embedType === 'INSTA';
  if (k === 'IMGUR') return isImgurFilterItem(item);
  if (k === 'YT') return item.kind === 'embed' && item.embedType === 'YT';
  if (k === 'TT') return item.kind === 'embed' && item.embedType === 'TT';
  if (k === 'EXTVIDEO') return isAdditionalVideoItem(item);
  return true;
}

function passesFilter(item) {
  return matchesFilterKey(item, state.filter.key || 'ALL');
}

function createFilterCounts() {
  return {
    ALL: 0,
    BODY: 0,
    IMG: 0,
    VIDEO: 0,
    X: 0,
    INSTA: 0,
    IMGUR: 0,
    YT: 0,
    TT: 0,
    EXTVIDEO: 0
  };
}

function countFilterItem(counts, item) {
  if (!item || item.__hashDup || item.__failed || isSourceSuppressed(item)) return;
  if (state.cfg.noiseHide && item.__isNoise) return;
  if (state.cfg.hiddenHide && (item.__allHidden || item.__autoHidden)) return;
  counts.ALL += 1;
  if (item.scope === 'body') counts.BODY += 1;
  if (item.kind === 'img') counts.IMG += 1;
  if (item.kind === 'video') counts.VIDEO += 1;
  if (item.embedType === 'INSTA') counts.INSTA += 1;
  if (isImgurFilterItem(item)) counts.IMGUR += 1;
  if (item.kind === 'embed') {
    if (item.embedType === 'X') counts.X += 1;
    if (item.embedType === 'YT') counts.YT += 1;
    if (item.embedType === 'TT') counts.TT += 1;
    if (isAdditionalVideoItem(item)) counts.EXTVIDEO += 1;
  }
}

function collectFilterCounts() {
  const counts = createFilterCounts();
  state.items.list.forEach(function(item) { countFilterItem(counts, item); });
  return counts;
}

function applyFilterSort() {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.ui.grid) return;
  for (const group of state.items.visualGroups) if (group.length>1) refreshVisualGroup(group);
  syncMediaOrder();
  invalidateVisibilityStats('filter-sort');
  const counts = createFilterCounts();
  const reasons = Object.create(null);
  let visible = 0;
  state.items.list.forEach(function(it) {
    countFilterItem(counts, it);
    const reason = getHideReasonCode(it, true);
    const show = !reason;
    const card = it.card;
    if (card) {
      if (it.__failed) {
        if (card.parentNode) card.parentNode.removeChild(card);
      } else {
        if (card.classList.contains('is-hidden') === show) card.classList.toggle('is-hidden', !show);
      }
    }
    it.__hideReason = reason;
    if (reason) reasons[reason] = (reasons[reason] || 0) + 1;
    else visible++;
  });
  state.ui.visibilityStats = {visible:visible, counts:reasons, hidden:[]};
  state.ui.visibilityStatsDirty = false;
  state.ui.visibilityStatsLimit = 0;
  applyErrorVis();
  updateChipUI(counts);
  if (state.thumb && !state.thumb.observer && typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function') state.thumb.scanNow();
}

function setTextIfChanged(el, value) {
  if (el && el.textContent !== value) el.textContent = value;
}
function setAttrIfChanged(el, key, value) {
  if (el && el.getAttribute(key) !== value) el.setAttribute(key, value);
}
function setPressed(btn, active) {
  if (!btn) return;
  if (btn.classList.contains('active') !== active) btn.classList.toggle('active', active);
  setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-pressed', String(active));
}

function updateChipUI(precomputedCounts) {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.ui.filterBar) return;
  const f = state.filter.key;
  const counts = precomputedCounts || collectFilterCounts();
  (state.ui.filterButtons || Array.from(state.ui.filterBar.querySelectorAll('[data-filter]'))).forEach(function(btn) {
    const key = btn.getAttribute('data-filter') || 'ALL';
    const label = (btn.dataset.baseLabel || key) + ' ' + (counts[key] || 0);
    setPressed(btn, key === f);
    setTextIfChanged(btn, label);
    setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-label', label);
  });
  const boostBtn = state.ui.boostChip || state.ui.filterBar.querySelector('[data-action="boost-crawl"]');
  const hiddenBtn = state.ui.hiddenChip || state.ui.filterBar.querySelector('[data-action="toggle-hidden"]');
  const noiseBtn = state.ui.noiseChip;
  setPressed(boostBtn, !!state.crawl.allowUserBoost);
  setPressed(hiddenBtn, !state.cfg.hiddenHide);
  setPressed(noiseBtn, !state.cfg.noiseHide);
  setTextIfChanged(hiddenBtn, state.cfg.hiddenHide ? '非表示も表示' : '非表示を隠す');
  setTextIfChanged(noiseBtn, state.cfg.noiseHide ? 'ノイズも表示' : 'ノイズを隠す');
}

function setFilter(key, opts) {
  state.filter = { key: key, opts: opts || {} };
  window.__FMP_FILTER__ = state.filter;
  applyFilterSort();
}

/* [07] collect */
function inBodyScope(el) {
  if (!el || !el.closest) return false;
  const sel = state.cfg.__articleSelector || '';
  if (!sel) return false;
  return !!el.closest(sel);
}

function inAvoidScope(el) {
  if (!el || !el.closest) return false;
  const sel = state.cfg.__avoidSelector || '';
  if (!sel) return false;
  return !!el.closest(sel);
}

function getCollectScopeInfo(el, strictBody) {
  const inBody = inBodyScope(el);
  return {
    skip: !!((strictBody && !inBody) || inAvoidScope(el)),
    scope: inBody ? 'body' : ''
  };
}

function getImgSize(img) {
  const w = img.naturalWidth || parseInt(img.getAttribute('width') || 0, 10) || 0;
  const h = img.naturalHeight || parseInt(img.getAttribute('height') || 0, 10) || 0;
  return { w: w, h: h };
}

function hasInlineHiddenStyle(styleText) {
  if (!styleText) return false;
  const s = String(styleText).toLowerCase();
  if (!s) return false;
  if (/(^|;)\s*display\s*:\s*none(\s*!important)?\s*(;|$)/.test(s)) return true;
  if (/(^|;)\s*visibility\s*:\s*(hidden|collapse)(\s*!important)?\s*(;|$)/.test(s)) return true;
  return false;
}

function setHiddenCache(cache, nodes, value) {
  if (!cache || !nodes) return;
  nodes.forEach(function(node) {
    if (node && node.nodeType === 1) {
      try { cache.set(node, value); } catch (e) {}
    }
  });
}

function isOriginallyHidden(el, hiddenCache) {
  if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return false;
  if (hiddenCache && hiddenCache.has(el)) return !!hiddenCache.get(el);
  const useComputed = !!el.isConnected && el.ownerDocument === document;
  let cur = el;
  let depth = 0;
  const walked = [];
  while (cur && cur.nodeType === 1 && depth < 18) {
    if (hiddenCache && hiddenCache.has(cur)) {
      const cached = !!hiddenCache.get(cur);
      setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, cached);
      return cached;
    }
    walked.push(cur);
    if (cur.hasAttribute && cur.hasAttribute('hidden')) {
      setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, true);
      return true;
    }
    if (cur.getAttribute) {
      const aria = (cur.getAttribute('aria-hidden') || '').toLowerCase();
      if (aria === 'true') {
        setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, true);
        return true;
      }
      if (hasInlineHiddenStyle(cur.getAttribute('style'))) {
        setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, true);
        return true;
      }
    }
    if (cur.tagName && cur.tagName.toUpperCase() === 'DETAILS' && !(cur.hasAttribute && cur.hasAttribute('open'))) {
      setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, true);
      return true;
    }
    if (useComputed) {
      try {
        const style = getComputedStyle(cur);
        if (style && (style.display === 'none' || style.visibility === 'hidden' || style.visibility === 'collapse')) {
          setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, true);
          return true;
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    cur = cur.parentElement;
    depth += 1;
  }
  setHiddenCache(hiddenCache, walked, false);
  return false;
}

function isLiveCssHidden(el, cache) {
  if (!el || !el.isConnected || el.ownerDocument !== document) return false;
  if (cache && cache.has(el)) return cache.get(el);
  let hidden=false;
  try {
    const own=getComputedStyle(el);
    hidden=own.display==='none' || own.visibility==='hidden' || own.visibility==='collapse';
    for(let n=el.parentElement;!hidden && n && n.nodeType===1;n=n.parentElement) {
      if(getComputedStyle(n).display==='none')hidden=true;
    }
  }catch(e){}
  if(cache)cache.set(el,hidden);
  return hidden;
}

function liveImageSourceElement(el,cache) {
  if(!el || el.ownerDocument!==document || !el.isConnected)return null;
  if(el.tagName==='A' && !String(el.textContent || '').trim()){
    const imgs=el.querySelectorAll('img');
    if(imgs.length===1)return imgs[0];
  }
  return el;
}

function noteLiveImageVisibility(url,el,hidden) {
  if(!url || !el)return;
  const map=state.items.liveImageVisibility;
  let rec=map.get(url);
  if(!rec){
    if(map.size>=4096)return;
    rec={sources:[],uncertain:false};map.set(url,rec);
  }
  if(rec.uncertain)return;
  const existing=rec.sources.find(v=>v.ref && v.ref.deref()===el);
  if(existing){
    if(existing.hidden!==hidden){existing.hidden=hidden;state.items.liveVisibilityDirty=true;}
  }else if(typeof WeakRef==='function' && rec.sources.length<8){
    rec.sources.push({ref:new WeakRef(el),hidden});state.items.liveVisibilityDirty=true;
  }else{
    rec.uncertain=true;state.items.liveVisibilityDirty=true;
  }
}

function isExcludedLiveImage(url) {
  const rec=state.items.liveImageVisibility.get(url);
  return !!(rec && !rec.uncertain && rec.sources.length && rec.sources.every(v=>v.hidden));
}

function isSourceSuppressed(item) {
  return !!(item && item.kind==='img' && item.__sourceSuppressed);
}

function reconcileLiveImageVisibility() {
  if(!state.items.liveVisibilityDirty)return;
  state.items.liveVisibilityDirty=false;
  let changed=false;
  for(const item of state.items.list){
    if(item.kind!=='img')continue;
    const hidden=isExcludedLiveImage(item.requestUrl);
    if(hidden===!!item.__sourceSuppressed)continue;
    item.__sourceSuppressed=hidden;changed=true;
    if(item.card && hidden)item.card.classList.add('is-hidden');
    if(hidden && item.__thumbLoading && item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.abort){
      item.__thumbCtx.abort('source-css-hidden');
      if(item.__thumbImg)item.__thumbImg.src=BLANK_GIF;
    }
  }
  if(changed){
    invalidateVisibilityStats('source-css-hidden');
    scheduleApplyFilterSort('source-css-hidden');
  }
}

function getDocumentCompareKey(url, baseUrl) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(String(url), baseUrl || location.href);
    const path = (u.pathname || '/').replace(/\/+$/, '') || '/';
    return u.origin + path + (u.search || '');
  } catch (e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function getThumbSourceFacts(el, meta) {
  if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return null;
  const pageUrl = (meta && meta.pageUrl) || location.href;
  const mediaBase = (meta && meta.mediaBase) || pageUrl;
  const cls = String((el.className || '') + ' ' + (el.id || '')).toLowerCase();
  const tag = el.tagName ? el.tagName.toUpperCase() : '';
  const anchor = tag === 'A' ? el : (el.closest ? el.closest('a[href]') : null);
  const href = anchor ? (anchor.getAttribute('href') || '').trim() : '';
  const anchorHref = resolveMediaReference(href, mediaBase);
  const nonMedia = !!href && !isDirectImageUrl(anchorHref) && !isVideoUrl(anchorHref);
  let sameSitePageLink = false;
  if (anchorHref && nonMedia) {
    try {
      const linkUrl = new URL(anchorHref, mediaBase);
      sameSitePageLink = linkUrl.origin === new URL(pageUrl).origin &&
        getDocumentCompareKey(linkUrl.href, pageUrl) !== getDocumentCompareKey(pageUrl, pageUrl);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return {
    outsideBody: !inBodyScope(el),
    avoid: inAvoidScope(el),
    classHint: /(thumb|thumbnail|related|ranking|recommend|popular|pickup|sidebar|widget|pager|list)/.test(cls),
    relatedHint: /(related|recommend|ranking|pickup|popular|list)/.test(cls),
    nonMedia: nonMedia,
    articleHint: /\/archives\/\d+|\.html(?:$|[?#])|\/(entry|article|post|news)\//i.test(href),
    sameSitePageLink: sameSitePageLink,
    hasImgContext: tag === 'IMG' || !!(anchor && anchor.querySelector && anchor.querySelector('img'))
  };
}

function scoreThumbnailLike(el, meta, absUrl, sourceFacts) {
  const facts = sourceFacts || getThumbSourceFacts(el, meta);
  if (!facts) return 0;
  const w = (meta && meta.width) ? meta.width : 0;
  const h = (meta && meta.height) ? meta.height : 0;
  let score = 0;

  if (w && h) {
    const sw = state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxW || 0;
    const sh = state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxH || 0;
    if (sw > 0 && sh > 0 && w <= sw && h <= sh) {
      score += (state.cfg.thumbLikeMinScore || 5);
    }
    const area = w * h;
    const aspect = Math.max(w / h, h / w);
    if (area <= 140 * 140) score += 2;
    else if (area <= 320 * 240) score += 1;
    if (aspect >= 1.2 && aspect <= 2.2) score += 1;
  } else {
    score += 1;
  }

  if (facts.outsideBody) score += 2;
  if (facts.avoid) score += 2;
  if (facts.classHint) score += 1;
  if (facts.nonMedia) score += 1;
  if (facts.articleHint) score += 2;
  const relatedArticleSquare = !!(w && h && w <= 340 && h <= 340 && Math.abs(w-h) <= 24 && facts.sameSitePageLink);
  if (relatedArticleSquare) {
    score += 4;
    if (facts.relatedHint) score += 1;
  }

  const u = String(absUrl || '').toLowerCase();
  const sw = state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxW || 0;
  const sh = state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxH || 0;
  let hintedW = 0;
  let hintedH = 0;
  const cropHint = /\/(?:crop\d*|resize|thumbs?)\/(\d{2,4})x(\d{2,4})(?:\/|$)/i.exec(u);
  if (cropHint) {
    hintedW = parseInt(cropHint[1] || '0', 10) || 0;
    hintedH = parseInt(cropHint[2] || '0', 10) || 0;
  } else {
    try {
      const pu = new URL(String(absUrl || ''), location.href);
      hintedW = parseInt(pu.searchParams.get('w') || pu.searchParams.get('width') || '0', 10) || 0;
      hintedH = parseInt(pu.searchParams.get('h') || pu.searchParams.get('height') || '0', 10) || 0;
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (sw > 0 && sh > 0 && hintedW && hintedH && hintedW <= sw && hintedH <= sh) {
    score += (state.cfg.thumbLikeMinScore || 5);
  }
  const hasSquare320PathHint = /(?:^|[\/_-])(?:3[01]\d|320)x(?:3[01]\d|320)(?:$|[\/_.\-?&#])/.test(u);
  const hasSquare320QueryHint = /[?&](?:w|width)=(?:3[01]\d|320)(?:[&#]|$)/.test(u) &&
    /[?&](?:h|height)=(?:3[01]\d|320)(?:[&#]|$)/.test(u);
  if (relatedArticleSquare && (hasSquare320PathHint || hasSquare320QueryHint)) score += 1;
  if (/(thumb|thumbnail|small|\/s\/|_thumb|\/resize\/\d+x\d+|[?&](w|h|width|height)=\d+)/.test(u)) score += 1;

  return score;
}

function pickAutoHiddenReason(a, b) {
  const ra = String(a || '');
  const rb = String(b || '');
  if (ra === 'pageLinkThumb' || rb === 'pageLinkThumb') return 'pageLinkThumb';
  return rb || ra || '';
}

function getAutoHiddenReason(el, meta, absUrl, thumbScore, sourceFacts) {
  const facts = sourceFacts || getThumbSourceFacts(el, meta);
  if (!facts || !facts.hasImgContext) return '';
  const score = Number.isFinite(thumbScore) ? thumbScore : scoreThumbnailLike(el, meta, absUrl, facts);
  if (facts.sameSitePageLink && score >= (state.cfg.thumbLikeMinScore || 0)) return 'pageLinkThumb';
  if (score >= (state.cfg.thumbLikeMinScore || 0)) return 'thumbScore';
  return '';
}

function collectImageUrls(img, baseUrl) {
  const urls = [];
  const picture = img.parentElement && img.parentElement.tagName === 'PICTURE' ? img.parentElement : null;
  const seenCanon = new Set();
  function add(u) {
    if (!u) return;
    const s = resolveMediaReference(u, baseUrl);
    if (!s || !isImgTagCandidateUrl(s)) return;
    const canon = picture ? s : (canonicalMediaKey(s, 'img') || s);
    if (seenCanon.has(canon)) return;
    seenCanon.add(canon);
    urls.push(s);
  }
  // Explicit originals and lazy srcsets may be present before the live
  // element has selected a real source. Never use a detached DOM's .src.
  add(img.getAttribute('data-original'));
  if (picture && img.ownerDocument === document && img.currentSrc) add(img.currentSrc);
  if (img.ownerDocument !== document || !img.currentSrc) {
    if (picture) for (const source of picture.children) {
      if (source === img) break;
      if (source.tagName !== 'SOURCE') continue;
      const type = source.getAttribute('type') || '';
      if (type && !/^image\/(?:jpeg|png|gif|webp|avif|bmp)$/i.test(type)) continue;
      const media = source.getAttribute('media');
      if (media && (!window.matchMedia || !window.matchMedia(media).matches)) continue;
      const candidate = pickBestFromSrcset(source.getAttribute('data-srcset') || source.getAttribute('srcset'));
      if (candidate && isImgTagCandidateUrl(resolveMediaReference(candidate, baseUrl))) { add(candidate); break; }
    }
  }
  add(pickBestFromSrcset(img.getAttribute('data-srcset')));
  add(pickBestFromSrcset(img.getAttribute('srcset')));
  add(img.getAttribute('data-src'));
  add(img.getAttribute('data-lazy'));
  if (img.ownerDocument === document) add(img.currentSrc);
  add(img.getAttribute('src'));
  return urls;
}

function findFirstHttpUrlInText(text) {
  if (!text) return '';
  const m = /https?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/i.exec(String(text));
  return m ? m[0] : '';
}

function normalizePreviewText(raw) {
  return String(raw || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}

function compactPreviewText(raw, maxLen) {
  const s = normalizePreviewText(raw);
  const limit = Math.max(0, maxLen || 0);
  if (!s) return '';
  if (!limit || s.length <= limit) return s;
  return s.slice(0, Math.max(1, limit - 1)).trimEnd() + '…';
}

function sanitizeEmbedPreviewText(raw, skipUrls) {
  let s = normalizePreviewText(raw);
  if (!s) return '';
  const skips = Array.isArray(skipUrls) ? skipUrls : [];
  skips.forEach(function(u) {
    const exact = normalizePreviewText(u);
    if (!exact || !s) return;
    if (s === exact) {
      s = '';
      return;
    }
    s = s.split(exact).join(' ');
  });
  s = s.replace(/\bhttps?:\/\/[^\s"'<>]+/ig, ' ');
  s = normalizePreviewText(s);
  if (!s) return '';
  if (/^(?:https?:\/\/|www\.)/i.test(s)) return '';
  return compactPreviewText(s, 72);
}

function pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(list, raw, skipUrls) {
  const s = sanitizeEmbedPreviewText(raw, skipUrls);
  if (!s) return;
  if (list.indexOf(s) >= 0) return;
  list.push(s);
}

function getEmbedPreviewTextFromElement(el, skipUrls) {
  if (!el) return '';
  const out = [];
  if (el.getAttribute) {
    pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, el.getAttribute('aria-label'), skipUrls);
    pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, el.getAttribute('title'), skipUrls);
  }
  if ((el.tagName || '').toUpperCase() === 'A') {
    pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, el.textContent || '', skipUrls);
  }
  if (el.querySelector) {
    const media = el.querySelector('img[alt], img[title]');
    if (media && media.getAttribute) {
      pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, media.getAttribute('alt'), skipUrls);
      pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, media.getAttribute('title'), skipUrls);
    }
  }
  const fig = el.closest ? el.closest('figure') : null;
  if (fig) {
    if (fig.getAttribute) {
      pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, fig.getAttribute('aria-label'), skipUrls);
      pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, fig.getAttribute('title'), skipUrls);
    }
    const cap = fig.querySelector ? fig.querySelector('figcaption') : null;
    if (cap) pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, cap.textContent || '', skipUrls);
  }
  const parent = el.parentElement;
  if (parent && parent.children) {
    Array.from(parent.children).forEach(function(child) {
      if (!child || child === el) return;
      const tag = (child.tagName || '').toUpperCase();
      const cls = String(child.className || '');
      if (tag === 'FIGCAPTION' || /(caption|title|summary|desc|description)/i.test(cls)) {
        pushEmbedPreviewCandidate(out, child.textContent || '', skipUrls);
      }
    });
  }
  return out[0] || '';
}

function queryAllIncludingRoot(root, selector) {
  const out = [];
  if (!root || !selector) return out;
  try {
    if (root.matches && root.matches(selector)) out.push(root);
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (root.querySelectorAll) {
      const list = root.querySelectorAll(selector);
      for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += 1) out.push(list[i]);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return out;
}

// Use element paths rather than layout reads or retaining parsed documents.
function mediaOrderPath(el, cache) {
  if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return [Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER];
  const path = [];
  for (let node = el; node && node.parentElement; node = node.parentElement) {
    const parent = node.parentElement;
    let positions = cache.get(parent);
    if (!positions) {
      positions = new WeakMap();
      let i = 0;
      for (const child of parent.children) positions.set(child, i++);
      cache.set(parent, positions);
    }
    path.push(positions.get(node) || 0);
  }
  return path.reverse();
}

function compareOrderPath(a,b) {
  for (let i=0;i<Math.min(a.length,b.length);i++) {
    if (a[i] !== b[i]) return a[i]-b[i];
  }
  return a.length-b.length;
}

function pageOrderRank(url) {
  const key = normalizeCrawlUrl(url);
  if (key === normalizeCrawlUrl(location.href)) return [0];
  try {
    const a = new URL(url,location.href), b = new URL(location.href);
    const p = a.searchParams.get('p') || a.searchParams.get('page');
    a.searchParams.delete('p'); a.searchParams.delete('page');
    b.searchParams.delete('p'); b.searchParams.delete('page');
    if (p && /^\d+$/.test(p) && a.origin+a.pathname+a.search===b.origin+b.pathname+b.search) return [1,0,Number(p)];
  } catch(e) {}
  return state.crawl.pageOrder.get(key) || [3];
}

function recordCrawlPageOrder(url,parent,priority,index) {
  if (state.crawl.pageOrder.has(url)) return;
  const rank = parent ? pageOrderRank(parent) : [0];
  const path = rank[0] === 0 ? (priority ? [1,1,index] : [2,index]) : rank.concat(priority ? 0 : 1,index);
  state.crawl.pageOrder.set(url,path);
}

function refreshMediaOrder(item,cache) {
  const el = item.__orderRef && item.__orderRef.deref();
  if (el && el.isConnected && el.ownerDocument === document) item.__orderPath = mediaOrderPath(el,cache);
}

function compareMediaOrder(a,b) {
  return compareOrderPath(a.__pageRank || pageOrderRank(a.__orderPage || a.pageUrl),b.__pageRank || pageOrderRank(b.__orderPage || b.pageUrl)) ||
    (a.__orderPage === b.__orderPage ? 0 : String(a.__orderPage).localeCompare(String(b.__orderPage))) ||
    compareOrderPath(a.__orderPath || [],b.__orderPath || []) || (a.index ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER)-(b.index ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER);
}

function keepEarlierMediaOrder(existing,item) {
  if (!existing.__orderPath || !item.__orderPath) return;
  if (existing.__orderRef && existing.__orderRef.deref() === (item.__orderRef && item.__orderRef.deref())) {
    existing.__orderPath=item.__orderPath;
    return;
  }
  if (compareMediaOrder(item,existing) < 0) {
    existing.__orderPage=item.__orderPage;
    existing.__orderPath=item.__orderPath;
    existing.__orderRef=item.__orderRef;
    existing.pageLabel=item.pageLabel;
    state.items.orderDirty=true;
    if (existing.card) setTextIfChanged(existing.card.querySelector('.chip-page'),existing.pageLabel);
  }
}

function syncMediaOrder() {
  if (!state.items.orderDirty || !state.ui.grid || state.ui.disposed) return;
  state.items.orderDirty=false;
  const cache=new WeakMap();
  const cards=state.items.list.filter(it=>it.card && it.card.parentNode===state.ui.grid);
  for (const it of cards) {
    refreshMediaOrder(it,cache);
    it.__pageRank=pageOrderRank(it.__orderPage || it.pageUrl);
  }
  cards.sort((a,b)=>compareMediaOrder(a.__visualOrder || a,b.__visualOrder || b) || a.index-b.index);
  let cursor=state.ui.grid.firstElementChild;
  for (const it of cards) {
    if (it.card===cursor) cursor=cursor.nextElementSibling;
    else state.ui.grid.insertBefore(it.card,cursor);
    delete it.__pageRank;
  }
}

function extractFromDocument(doc, source, baseUrl, responsiveHidden) {
  const items = [];
  const orderCache = new WeakMap();
  const cssHiddenCache=new WeakMap();
  const makeOrderedItem = (kind,url,meta,base) => {
    if(kind==='img'){
      const el=liveImageSourceElement(meta.el,cssHiddenCache);
      if(el){
        const hidden=(meta.origHidden || el!==meta.el) && (isLiveCssHidden(el,cssHiddenCache) || (el!==meta.el && isLiveCssHidden(meta.el,cssHiddenCache)));
        const observed=resolveMediaReference(url,meta.mediaBase || base);
        for(const v of [observed,meta.identityUrl].concat(meta.pictureFallbackUrls || [])){
          if(v)noteLiveImageVisibility(v,el,!!hidden);
        }
        if(hidden)return null;
      }
    }
    meta.orderPath=mediaOrderPath(meta.el,orderCache);
    return makeItem(kind,url,meta,base);
  };
  const strictBody = !!state.cfg.strictBodyOnly;
  const standaloneMedia = doc && doc.nodeType === 9 && isMediaDocument(doc);
  const mediaBase = getMediaDocumentBase(doc, baseUrl);
  const hiddenCache = typeof WeakMap === 'function' ? new WeakMap() : null;
  const bodyScopeCache = typeof WeakMap === 'function' ? new WeakMap() : null;
  const avoidScopeCache = typeof WeakMap === 'function' ? new WeakMap() : null;
  const getScopedInfo = function(el) {
    if (!el || el.nodeType !== 1) return { skip: true, scope: '' };
    let inBody = false;
    let inAvoid = false;
    if (bodyScopeCache && bodyScopeCache.has(el)) {
      inBody = !!bodyScopeCache.get(el);
    } else {
      inBody = inBodyScope(el);
      if (bodyScopeCache) bodyScopeCache.set(el, inBody);
    }
    if (avoidScopeCache && avoidScopeCache.has(el)) {
      inAvoid = !!avoidScopeCache.get(el);
    } else {
      inAvoid = inAvoidScope(el);
      if (avoidScopeCache) avoidScopeCache.set(el, inAvoid);
    }
    return {
      skip: !!((strictBody && !inBody && !(standaloneMedia && el.parentElement === doc.body && /^(IMG|VIDEO)$/.test(el.tagName))) || inAvoid),
      scope: inBody || (standaloneMedia && el.parentElement === doc.body && /^(IMG|VIDEO)$/.test(el.tagName)) ? 'body' : ''
    };
  };
  const imgs = queryAllIncludingRoot(doc, 'img');
  imgs.forEach(function(img) {
    if (responsiveHidden && responsiveHidden.has(img)) return;
    const scopeInfo = getScopedInfo(img);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) return;
    const urls = collectImageUrls(img, mediaBase);
    if (!urls.length) return;
    const size = getImgSize(img);
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(img, hiddenCache);
    const loadedUrl = resolveMediaReference(img.ownerDocument === document ? (img.currentSrc || img.getAttribute('src')) : img.getAttribute('src'), mediaBase);
    const picture = img.parentElement && img.parentElement.tagName === 'PICTURE';
    const consumed = new Set();
    for (const u of urls) {
      if (consumed.has(u)) continue;
      if (!isImgTagCandidateUrl(u)) continue;
      const sameSource = u === loadedUrl;
      // A picture describes alternative renditions of one displayed image.
      // Keep fallback candidates rather than a visible card per source.
      if (picture && sameSource && !passesSize({width:size.w,height:size.h})) continue;
      const related = picture ? urls.filter(v=>v!==u) : urls.filter(v=>v!==u && !consumed.has(v) && sameDeclaredRendition(u,v));
      related.forEach(v=>consumed.add(v));
      const imDirect = getImgurDirectMedia(u);
      items.push(makeOrderedItem(imDirect && imDirect.video ? 'video' : 'img', u, {
        width: sameSource ? size.w : 0,
        height: sameSource ? size.h : 0,
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        source: source,
        openUrl: u,
        mediaBase: mediaBase,
        identityUrl: getDeclaredOriginalIdentity(img, u, mediaBase),
        pictureFallbackUrls: related.length ? related.slice(0,4) : null,
        renditionAliases: !picture ? related.slice(0,4) : null,
        el: img
      }, baseUrl));
      if (picture) break;
    }
  });

  const videos = queryAllIncludingRoot(doc, 'video');
  videos.forEach(function(video) {
    const scopeInfo = getScopedInfo(video);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) return;
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(video, hiddenCache);
    const vsrcs = [];
    if (video.ownerDocument === document && video.currentSrc) vsrcs.push(video.currentSrc);
    vsrcs.push(video.getAttribute('src'));
    Array.from(video.querySelectorAll('source')).forEach(function(s) {
      vsrcs.push(s.getAttribute('src'));
    });
    const current = video.ownerDocument === document ? resolveMediaReference(video.currentSrc, mediaBase) : '';
    const captured = current && !video.getAttribute('poster') ? captureSmallVideoFrame(video) : '';
    uniq(vsrcs.map(function(u) { return resolveMediaReference(u, mediaBase); })).forEach(function(u) {
      if (!isVideoUrl(u)) return;
      items.push(makeOrderedItem('video', u, {
        width: parseInt(video.getAttribute('width') || 0, 10) || 0,
        height: parseInt(video.getAttribute('height') || 0, 10) || 0,
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        poster: resolveMediaReference(video.getAttribute('poster'), mediaBase),
        capturedPoster: u === current ? captured : '',
        source: source,
        openUrl: u,
        el: video
      }, baseUrl));
    });
  });

  const links = queryAllIncludingRoot(doc, 'a[href]');
  links.forEach(function(a) {
    const scopeInfo = getScopedInfo(a);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) return;
    const href = a.getAttribute('href');
    if (!href || href.startsWith('#')) return;
    const concreteHref = resolveMediaReference(href, mediaBase);
    const abs = normalizeUrl(concreteHref, mediaBase);
    if (!abs) return;
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(a, hiddenCache);
    const linkImg = a.querySelector ? a.querySelector('img') : null;
    if (responsiveHidden && linkImg && !String(a.textContent || '').trim() &&
        Array.from(a.querySelectorAll('img')).every(img=>responsiveHidden.has(img))) return;
    const linkSize = linkImg ? getImgSize(linkImg) : { w: 0, h: 0 };
    const imPost = getImgurPost(concreteHref);
    const embedFromHref = imPost ? {type:'IMGUR',url:imPost.embedUrl,openUrl:imPost.openUrl} : (getImgurDirectMedia(concreteHref) ? null : (getAdditionalVideoInfo(concreteHref) || getEmbedInfo(abs.abs)));
    const hrefIsDirectImage = isDirectImageUrl(concreteHref) || isDirectImageUrl(abs.abs);
    const hrefIsDirectVideo = isVideoUrl(concreteHref) || isVideoUrl(abs.abs);
    const hrefIsDirectMedia = hrefIsDirectImage || hrefIsDirectVideo;
    const textUrl = findFirstHttpUrlInText(a.textContent || '');
    let textAbs = null;
    let textEmbed = null;
    let textIsDirectImage = false;
    let textIsDirectVideo = false;
    if (textUrl) {
      textAbs = normalizeUrl(textUrl, baseUrl || location.href);
      if (textAbs) {
        textEmbed = getAdditionalVideoInfo(textUrl) || getEmbedInfo(textAbs.abs);
        textIsDirectImage = isDirectImageUrl(textAbs.abs);
        textIsDirectVideo = isVideoUrl(textAbs.abs);
      }
    }
    let embedFromText = null;
    if (!embedFromHref && !hrefIsDirectMedia && textEmbed) {
      embedFromText = textEmbed;
      if (embedFromText && !embedFromText.openUrl && textAbs) {
        embedFromText = Object.assign({}, embedFromText, { openUrl: textAbs.abs });
      }
    }
    const textIsDirectMedia = textIsDirectImage || textIsDirectVideo;
    const directVideoFromText = !hrefIsDirectMedia && textIsDirectVideo;
    const directImageFromText = !hrefIsDirectMedia && textIsDirectImage;
    const mediaHintType = (!embedFromHref && (hrefIsDirectMedia || textIsDirectMedia) && textEmbed && textEmbed.type === 'INSTA') ? 'INSTA' : '';
    const embed = embedFromHref || embedFromText;
    if (embed) {
      const previewText = getEmbedPreviewTextFromElement(a, [abs.abs, embed.openUrl || '', embed.url || '', textAbs ? textAbs.abs : '']);
      items.push(makeOrderedItem('embed', abs.abs, {
        width: 0,
        height: 0,
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        source: source,
        openUrl: embed.openUrl || abs.abs,
        embedType: embed.type,
        embedUrl: embed.url,
        previewText: previewText,
        el: a
      }, baseUrl));
    }
    if (!embedFromHref && (hrefIsDirectVideo || directVideoFromText)) {
      const mediaUrl = directVideoFromText && textAbs ? (isVideoUrl(textUrl) ? resolveMediaReference(textUrl, mediaBase) : (textAbs.sourceAbs || textAbs.abs)) : (isVideoUrl(concreteHref) ? concreteHref : (abs.sourceAbs || abs.abs));
      items.push(makeOrderedItem('video', mediaUrl, {
        width: 0,
        height: 0,
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        poster: '',
        source: source,
        openUrl: mediaUrl,
        embedType: mediaHintType,
        el: a
      }, baseUrl));
    } else if (!embedFromHref && (hrefIsDirectImage || directImageFromText)) {
      const mediaUrl = directImageFromText && textAbs ? (isDirectImageUrl(textUrl) ? resolveMediaReference(textUrl, mediaBase) : (textAbs.sourceAbs || textAbs.abs)) : (isDirectImageUrl(concreteHref) ? concreteHref : (abs.sourceAbs || abs.abs));
      const thumbUrl = linkImg ? resolveMediaReference(linkImg.ownerDocument === document ? (linkImg.currentSrc || linkImg.getAttribute('src')) : linkImg.getAttribute('src'), mediaBase) : '';
      const sameSource = !!thumbUrl && thumbUrl === mediaUrl;
      items.push(makeOrderedItem('img', mediaUrl, {
        width: sameSource ? linkSize.w : 0,
        height: sameSource ? linkSize.h : 0,
        directImageLink: true,
        mediaBase: mediaBase,
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        source: source,
        openUrl: mediaUrl,
        embedType: mediaHintType,
        el: a
      }, baseUrl));
    }
  });

  const iframes = queryAllIncludingRoot(doc, 'iframe[src],iframe[data-src],iframe[data-lazy-src],script[src*="embed.nicovideo.jp/watch/"]');
  iframes.forEach(function(fr) {
    const scopeInfo = getScopedInfo(fr);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) return;
    let abs = null, info = null;
    for (const attr of ['src','data-src','data-lazy-src']) {
      const src = fr.getAttribute(attr);
      if (!src) continue;
      const candidate = normalizeUrl(src, mediaBase);
      const parsed = candidate && (getEmbedInfoFromIframe(resolveMediaReference(src, mediaBase) || candidate.abs) || getEmbedInfoFromIframe(candidate.abs));
      if (parsed && (fr.tagName !== 'SCRIPT' || parsed.type === 'NICO')) { abs = candidate; info = parsed; break; }
    }
    if (!info) return;
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(fr.tagName === 'SCRIPT' ? fr.parentElement : fr, hiddenCache);
    const previewText = getEmbedPreviewTextFromElement(fr, [abs.abs, info.openUrl || '', info.embedUrl || '']);
    items.push(makeOrderedItem('embed', info.openUrl, {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      scope: scope,
      origHidden: origHidden,
      source: source,
      openUrl: info.openUrl,
      embedType: info.type,
      embedUrl: info.embedUrl,
      previewText: previewText,
      el: fr
    }, baseUrl));
  });

  const bqs = queryAllIncludingRoot(doc, 'blockquote.imgur-embed-pub[data-id], blockquote[data-id][class*=\"imgur-embed\"]');
  bqs.forEach(function(el) {
    const scopeInfo = getScopedInfo(el);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) return;
    const info = getImgurEmbedFromBlockquote(el);
    if (!info) return;
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(el, hiddenCache);
    const previewText = getEmbedPreviewTextFromElement(el, [info.openUrl || '', info.embedUrl || '']);
    items.push(makeOrderedItem('embed', info.openUrl, {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      scope: scope,
      origHidden: origHidden,
      source: source,
      openUrl: info.openUrl,
      embedType: 'IMGUR',
      embedUrl: info.embedUrl,
      previewText: previewText,
      el: el
    }, baseUrl));
  });

  return items;
}

async function unlockLazy() {
  if (!document.body) return;
  const startY = window.scrollY || 0;
  const maxY = Math.max((document.documentElement.scrollHeight || 0) - window.innerHeight, 0);
  const steps = state.cfg.lazyScrollSteps;
  for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i += 1) {
    if (state.abort.aborted) return;
    window.scrollTo(0, Math.round(maxY * (i / steps)));
    await sleep(state.cfg.lazyDelayMs);
  }
  window.scrollTo(0, startY);
  await sleep(state.cfg.lazyDelayMs);
}

function parseCssUrls(value) {
  const out = [];
  if (!value || value === 'none') return out;
  const re = /url\(("|')?(.*?)("|')?\)/g;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(value))) {
    if (m[2]) out.push(m[2]);
  }
  return out;
}

function extractBackgrounds() {
  const items = [];
  const orderCache = new WeakMap();
  const selector = '[style*="background"], [data-bg], [data-background], [data-bgurl]';
  const hiddenCache = typeof WeakMap === 'function' ? new WeakMap() : null;
  const nodes = document.querySelectorAll(selector);
  let checked = 0;
  for (const node of nodes) {
    if (checked >= state.cfg.maxBgChecks) break;
    const scopeInfo = getCollectScopeInfo(node, !!state.cfg.strictBodyOnly);
    if (scopeInfo.skip) continue;
    checked++;
    const rect = node.getBoundingClientRect ? node.getBoundingClientRect() : { width: 0, height: 0 };
    if (rect.width * rect.height < state.cfg.minArea) continue;
    const urls = [];
    if (node.dataset) {
      if (node.dataset.bg) urls.push(node.dataset.bg);
      if (node.dataset.background) urls.push(node.dataset.background);
      if (node.dataset.bgurl) urls.push(node.dataset.bgurl);
    }
    try {
      const style = getComputedStyle(node);
      urls.push.apply(urls, parseCssUrls(style.backgroundImage));
    } catch (e) {}
    const scope = scopeInfo.scope;
    const origHidden = isOriginallyHidden(node, hiddenCache);
    uniq(urls).forEach(function(u) {
      const resolved=resolveMediaReference(u,location.href);
      const hidden=origHidden && isLiveCssHidden(node);
      noteLiveImageVisibility(resolved,node,!!hidden);
      if(hidden)return;
      if (!isImageUrl(u)) return;
      items.push(makeItem('img', u, {
        width: Math.round(rect.width),
        height: Math.round(rect.height),
        scope: scope,
        origHidden: origHidden,
        source: 'bg',
        openUrl: u,
        orderPath: mediaOrderPath(node,orderCache),
        el: node
      }));
    });
  }
  return items;
}

function extractFromScripts() {
  // DISABLED in rev1.21: strict body-only mode; keep for future toggle
  const items = [];
  const scripts = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('script'))
    .filter(function(s) { return !s.src && s.textContent; })
    .slice(0, state.cfg.maxScriptCount);
  let buf = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < scripts.length; i += 1) {
    if (buf.length >= state.cfg.maxScriptChars) break;
    buf += scripts[i].textContent.slice(0, state.cfg.maxScriptChars - buf.length);
  }
  if (!buf) return items;
  const re = /(https?:\/\/[^\s'"\\>]+?\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp|mp4|webm|mov|m4v))(\?|#|\b)/ig;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(buf))) {
    items.push(makeItem(isVideoUrl(m[1]) ? 'video' : 'img', m[1], {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      scope: '',
      source: 'script',
      openUrl: m[1]
    }));
  }
  return items;
}

function extractFromHtmlString() {
  // DISABLED in rev1.21: strict body-only mode; keep for future toggle
  const items = [];
  let html = '';
  try {
