    html = (document.documentElement && document.documentElement.outerHTML) || '';
  } catch (e) {}
  if (!html) return items;
  html = html.slice(0, state.cfg.maxHtmlChars).replace(/&amp;/g, '&');
  const re = /(https?:\/\/[^\s'"\\>]+?\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp|mp4|webm|mov|m4v))(\?|#|\b)/ig;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(html))) {
    items.push(makeItem(isVideoUrl(m[1]) ? 'video' : 'img', m[1], {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      scope: '',
      source: 'html',
      openUrl: m[1]
    }));
  }
  return items;
}

async function collectInitial() {
  const collectStartedAt = perfStart('collect');
  perfCount('collect');
  updateProgress('scan', 'dom');
  const beforeDom = state.items.list.length;
  addItems(extractFromDocument(document, 'dom', location.href));
  perfCount('collect.dom.items', state.items.list.length - beforeDom);
  state.log.push('collect', 'dom +' + (state.items.list.length - beforeDom) + ' total=' + state.items.list.length);

  if (state.items.list.length < state.cfg.bgMinItems) {
    await unlockLazy();
    if (!isRunCurrent()) return;
    updateProgress('scan', 'lazy');
    const beforeLazy = state.items.list.length;
    addItems(extractFromDocument(document, 'lazy', location.href));
    perfCount('collect.lazy.items', state.items.list.length - beforeLazy);
    state.log.push('collect', 'lazy +' + (state.items.list.length - beforeLazy) + ' total=' + state.items.list.length);
  }

  if (state.items.list.length < state.cfg.bgMinItems) {
    updateProgress('scan', 'bg');
    const beforeBg = state.items.list.length;
    addItems(extractBackgrounds());
    perfCount('collect.bg.items', state.items.list.length - beforeBg);
    state.log.push('collect', 'bg +' + (state.items.list.length - beforeBg) + ' total=' + state.items.list.length);
  }
  state.log.push('collect', 'script/html scans DISABLED (body-only strict)');
  perfEnd('collect', collectStartedAt);
}

/* [08] dedup/groupBest */
function collectDeclaredPreviewUrls(el, base) {
  if (!el || !el.getAttribute) return [];
  const out = [];
  const add = function(raw) {
    const url = resolveMediaReference(raw, base);
    if (url && isImgTagCandidateUrl(url) && !out.includes(url)) out.push(url);
  };
  for (const key of ['poster','data-poster','data-thumbnail','data-thumb','data-preview-image']) add(el.getAttribute(key));
  if (el.tagName !== 'VIDEO' && el.querySelector) {
    const img = el.querySelector('img');
    if (img) {
      const size = getImgSize(img);
      if (!size.w || !size.h || (size.w >= 100 && size.h >= 80)) {
        for (const url of collectImageUrls(img, base)) add(url);
      }
    }
  }
  return out.slice(0,4);
}

function makeItem(kind, url, meta, baseUrl) {
  const resolved = resolveMediaReference(url, baseUrl || location.href);
  const directImgur = (kind === 'video' || kind === 'img') ? getImgurDirectMedia(resolved) : null;
  const concrete = directImgur ? directImgur.url : resolved;
  const norm = normalizeUrl(concrete, baseUrl || location.href);
  if (!norm) return null;
  const media = kind === 'img' || kind === 'video';
  let knownImageHost = false;
  if (kind === 'img') { try { knownImageHost = isKnownImageVariantHost(new URL(concrete).hostname.toLowerCase()); } catch (e) {} }
  let requestUrl = media ? concrete : norm.canon;
  let fallbackRequestUrl = '';
  const pageUrl = baseUrl || location.href;
  if (kind === 'img' && !meta.directImageLink) {
    // Keep legacy original-image inference for the explicitly recognized
    // livedoor image CDN, but retain the observed URL if inference fails.
    try {
      const rawHost = new URL(concrete).hostname;
      const originalHost = new URL(norm.canon).hostname;
      if ((/(^|\.)blogimg\.jp$/i.test(rawHost) || rawHost === 'resize.blogsys.jp') &&
          /(^|\.)blogimg\.jp$/i.test(originalHost) && norm.canon !== concrete) {
        requestUrl = norm.canon;
        fallbackRequestUrl = concrete;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  const item = {
    id: 'f' + state.items.nextId++,
    kind: kind,
    abs: media && !knownImageHost ? concrete : norm.abs,
    url: media && !knownImageHost ? requestUrl : norm.canon,
    rawUrl: url,
    requestUrl: requestUrl,
    fallbackRequestUrl: fallbackRequestUrl,
    pictureFallbackUrls: meta.pictureFallbackUrls || null,
    renditionAliases: meta.renditionAliases || null,
    imgurFallbackUrls: null,
    mediaBase: meta.mediaBase || pageUrl,
    __directImageLink: !!meta.directImageLink,
    groupKey: norm.groupKey,
    host: norm.host,
    width: meta.width || 0,
    height: meta.height || 0,
    scope: meta.scope || '',
    source: meta.source || 'dom',
    openUrl: media ? requestUrl : (meta.openUrl || norm.canon),
    poster: meta.poster || '',
    previewUrls: kind === 'img' ? [] : collectDeclaredPreviewUrls(meta.el, meta.mediaBase || getMediaDocumentBase(meta.el, pageUrl)),
    __capturedPoster: meta.capturedPoster || '',
    previewText: meta.previewText || '',
    embedType: meta.embedType || '',
    embedUrl: meta.embedUrl || '',
    pageUrl: pageUrl,
    pageLabel: computePageLabel(pageUrl),
    __orderPage: normalizeCrawlUrl(pageUrl) || pageUrl,
    __orderPath: meta.orderPath || mediaOrderPath(meta.el,new WeakMap()),
    __orderRef: meta.el && meta.el.ownerDocument === document && typeof WeakRef === 'function' ? new WeakRef(meta.el) : null,
    index: state.items.list.length,
    card: null,
    __sourceEl: meta.el || null,
    __thumbSourceFacts: null,
    __origHidden: !!meta.origHidden,
    __allHidden: !!meta.origHidden,
    __autoHidden: false,
    __autoHiddenReason: '',
    __noiseReason: '',
    __dupClusterUrls: []
  };
  const extraIdentity = kind === 'embed' && getAdditionalVideoInfo(item.openUrl);
  const imgurIdentity = kind === 'embed' && item.embedType === 'IMGUR' && getImgurPost(item.openUrl);
  const declaredDirect = directImgur && getImgurDirectMedia(meta.identityUrl);
  item.dedupKey = imgurIdentity ? imgurIdentity.openUrl :
    (extraIdentity ? 'provider:'+extraIdentity.type+':'+extraIdentity.identity :
    canonicalMediaKey(media ? (declaredDirect ? declaredDirect.url : (directImgur ? concrete : (meta.identityUrl || concrete))) : item.abs, kind));
  item.__thumbScore = 0;
  if (kind === 'img') {
    const thumbMeta = Object.assign({}, meta, {pageUrl:pageUrl, mediaBase:item.mediaBase});
    const sourceFacts = getThumbSourceFacts(meta.el || null, thumbMeta);
    const thumbScore = scoreThumbnailLike(meta.el || null, thumbMeta, item.requestUrl, sourceFacts);
    const autoHiddenReason = getAutoHiddenReason(meta.el || null, thumbMeta, item.requestUrl, thumbScore, sourceFacts);
    item.__thumbScore = thumbScore;
    item.__autoHidden = !!autoHiddenReason;
    item.__autoHiddenReason = autoHiddenReason;
    if (state.cfg.releaseSourceEl && item.source === 'crawl' && item.__sourceEl && item.__sourceEl.ownerDocument !== document) item.__thumbSourceFacts = sourceFacts;
  }
  if (item.url) item.__dupClusterUrls.push(item.url);
  item.dupCount = 1;
  item.score = scoreItem(item);
  perfMeasureSourceEl(item);
  // Parsed crawl documents are immutable here. Keep only the facts
  // required by later dimension-based classification.
  if (state.cfg.releaseSourceEl && item.kind !== 'img' && item.source === 'crawl' && item.__sourceEl && item.__sourceEl.ownerDocument !== document) {
    releaseSourceEl(item, 'crawl-media-facts');
  }
  if (item.__thumbSourceFacts) {
    const facts = item.__thumbSourceFacts;
    releaseSourceEl(item, 'crawl-facts');
    item.__thumbSourceFacts = facts;
  }
  return item;
}

function registerSeen(key) {
  if (state.seen.map.has(key)) return;
  state.seen.map.set(key, true);
  state.seen.order.push(key);
}

function isSameDupClusterUrl(a, b, kind) {
  if (!a || !b) return false;
  if (a === b) return true;
  if (kind === 'img') return isSameImageVariantUrl(a, b);
  return false;
}

function addDupClusterUrl(item, url) {
  if (!item || !url) return;
  if (!Array.isArray(item.__dupClusterUrls)) item.__dupClusterUrls = [];
  const u = String(url);
  if (!u) return;
  for (let i = 0; i < item.__dupClusterUrls.length; i += 1) {
    if (isSameDupClusterUrl(item.__dupClusterUrls[i], u, item.kind)) return;
  }
  item.__dupClusterUrls.push(u);
}

function mergeDupCluster(target, candidate) {
  if (!target) return;
  addDupClusterUrl(target, target.url || target.abs || '');
  if (!candidate) return;
  addDupClusterUrl(target, candidate.url || candidate.abs || candidate.openUrl || '');
  if (Array.isArray(candidate.__dupClusterUrls)) {
    candidate.__dupClusterUrls.forEach(function(u) {
      addDupClusterUrl(target, u);
    });
  }
}

function getDupClusterHostCounts(item) {
  const counts = Object.create(null);
  if (!item || !Array.isArray(item.__dupClusterUrls)) return counts;
  item.__dupClusterUrls.forEach(function(u) {
    if (!u) return;
    try {
      const host = new URL(String(u), location.href).hostname.toLowerCase();
      if (!host) return;
      counts[host] = (counts[host] || 0) + 1;
    } catch (e) {}
  });
  return counts;
}

function buildDupClusterDetail(item, maxUrls) {
  if (!item || !item.dupCount || item.dupCount < 2) return '';
  const urls = Array.isArray(item.__dupClusterUrls) ? item.__dupClusterUrls.slice() : [];
  const hosts = getDupClusterHostCounts(item);
  const hostEntries = Object.keys(hosts).sort(function(a, b) { return hosts[b] - hosts[a]; });
  const limit = Math.max(1, maxUrls || 6);
  const lines = [];
  lines.push('dup=' + item.dupCount + ' host=' + hostEntries.length + ' url=' + urls.length);
  if (hostEntries.length) {
    const hostLine = hostEntries.map(function(h) {
      return h + ':' + hosts[h];
    }).join(', ');
    lines.push('hosts: ' + hostLine);
  }
  const shown = urls.slice(0, limit);
  shown.forEach(function(u, i) {
    lines.push((i + 1) + '. ' + u);
  });
  if (urls.length > shown.length) {
    lines.push('... +' + (urls.length - shown.length));
  }
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function mergeBest(target, candidate) {
  const keepId = target.id;
  const keepIndex = target.index;
  const keepOrder = [target.__orderPage,target.__orderPath,target.__orderRef,target.pageLabel];
  const keepCard = target.card;
  const keepDup = target.dupCount || 1;
  const keepUrl = target.url;
  const keepRequest = target.requestUrl || keepUrl;
  const keepEmbedType = target.embedType || '';
  const keepPreviewText = target.previewText || '';
  const keepPoster = target.poster || '';
  const keepCaptured = target.__capturedPoster || '';
  const keepPreviewUrls = target.previewUrls || [];
  const keepThumbLoaded = target.__thumbLoaded;
  const keepThumbLoading = target.__thumbLoading;
  const keepThumbRetry = target.__thumbRetry;
  const keepThumbCtx = target.__thumbCtx;
  const keepThumbToken = target.__thumbToken;
  const keepIsNoise = target.__isNoise;
  const keepNoiseReason = target.__noiseReason;
  const keepAutoHidden = !!target.__autoHidden;
  const keepAutoHiddenReason = target.__autoHiddenReason || '';
  if (keepRequest !== (candidate.requestUrl || candidate.url)) forgetVisualFingerprint(target);
  const keepHashDup = target.__hashDup;
  const keepFailed = target.__failed;
  const keepRenderUrl = target.__renderUrl;
  const keepAllHidden = !!target.__allHidden;
  const keepDupClusterUrls = Array.isArray(target.__dupClusterUrls) ? target.__dupClusterUrls.slice() : [];
  Object.assign(target, candidate);
  target.id = keepId;
  target.index = keepIndex;
  [target.__orderPage,target.__orderPath,target.__orderRef,target.pageLabel] = keepOrder;
  target.card = keepCard;
  target.dupCount = keepDup;
  if (!target.embedType && keepEmbedType) target.embedType = keepEmbedType;
  if (!target.previewText && keepPreviewText) target.previewText = keepPreviewText;
  if (!target.poster && keepPoster) target.poster = keepPoster;
  if (!target.__capturedPoster && keepRequest === (target.requestUrl || target.url)) target.__capturedPoster = keepCaptured;
  target.previewUrls = uniqueThumbUrls(keepPreviewUrls.concat(target.previewUrls || [])).slice(0,4);
  target.__thumbLoaded = keepThumbLoaded;
  target.__thumbLoading = keepThumbLoading;
  target.__thumbRetry = keepThumbRetry;
  target.__thumbCtx = keepThumbCtx;
  target.__thumbToken = keepThumbToken;
  target.__renderUrl = keepRenderUrl;
  target.__dupClusterUrls = keepDupClusterUrls;
  target.__allHidden = keepAllHidden && !!candidate.__allHidden;
  target.__origHidden = target.__allHidden;
  target.__autoHidden = keepAutoHidden && !!candidate.__autoHidden;
  target.__autoHiddenReason = target.__autoHidden ? pickAutoHiddenReason(keepAutoHiddenReason, candidate.__autoHiddenReason || '') : '';
  if (keepRequest === (target.requestUrl || target.url)) {
    target.__isNoise = keepIsNoise;
    target.__noiseReason = keepNoiseReason;
    target.__hashDup = keepHashDup;
    target.__failed = keepFailed;
  } else {
    if (keepIsNoise) state.items.noiseCount = Math.max(0, state.items.noiseCount - 1);
    target.__isNoise = false;
    target.__noiseReason = '';
    target.__failed = false;
    target.__hashDup = keepHashDup;
    target.__resolvedRequestFor = '';
    target.__resolvedRequestUrl = '';
    invalidateVisibilityStats('media-source-change');
    scheduleApplyFilterSort('media-source-change');
  }
  mergeDupCluster(target, candidate);
  target.score = scoreItem(target);
  return target;
}

function adoptItem(item) {
  if (!item) return null;
  if (item.kind==='img' && isExcludedLiveImage(item.requestUrl)) return null;
  if (!passesSize(item) && item.kind !== 'embed') return null;
  const key = item.dedupKey || item.url;
  const existing = state.groupBest.get(key) || (item.kind === 'img' && (item.renditionAliases || []).map(u=>canonicalMediaKey(u,'img')).map(k=>state.groupBest.get(k)).find(it=>it && it.kind==='img'));
  if (existing && existing.kind === 'img' && item.kind === 'img') {
    for (const k of [key].concat((item.renditionAliases || []).map(u=>canonicalMediaKey(u,'img')))) {
      if (k && !state.groupBest.has(k)) state.groupBest.set(k,existing);
    }
  }
  if (existing) {
    keepEarlierMediaOrder(existing,item);
    const betterByScore = (item.score - existing.score) > 1e-6;
    const betterByQuality = isHigherQualityImageCandidate(item, existing);
    const bothImages = item.kind === 'img' && existing.kind === 'img';
    const distinctRequest = bothImages && item.requestUrl !== existing.requestUrl;
    if (bothImages) {
      const related = distinctRequest && sameDeclaredRendition(existing.requestUrl,item.requestUrl);
      const variants = uniqueThumbUrls((existing.pictureFallbackUrls || []).concat(item.pictureFallbackUrls || [], related ? [existing.requestUrl,item.requestUrl] : []));
      if (variants.length) {
        existing.pictureFallbackUrls=variants.filter(u=>u!==existing.requestUrl).slice(0,4);
        item.pictureFallbackUrls=variants.filter(u=>u!==item.requestUrl).slice(0,4);
      }
    }
    const preferExplicit = distinctRequest && item.__directImageLink && !existing.__directImageLink;
    const keepExplicit = distinctRequest && existing.__directImageLink && !item.__directImageLink;
    const a = bothImages && getImgurDirectMedia(item.requestUrl);
    const b = bothImages && getImgurDirectMedia(existing.requestUrl);
    const rendition = x => x && /^(?:[A-Za-z0-9]{5}|[A-Za-z0-9]{7})[sbtmlh]$/.test(x.id);
    const preferOriginal = a && b && !rendition(a) && rendition(b) && a.ext === b.ext && b.id.slice(0,-1) === a.id;
    const keepOriginal = a && b && rendition(a) && !rendition(b) && a.ext === b.ext && a.id.slice(0,-1) === b.id;
    const newImgurAlias = a && b && a.ext === b.ext && item.requestUrl !== existing.requestUrl &&
      !(existing.imgurFallbackUrls || []).includes(item.requestUrl);
    if (a && b && a.ext === b.ext && item.requestUrl !== existing.requestUrl) {
      const observed = uniqueThumbUrls([existing.requestUrl,item.requestUrl].concat(existing.imgurFallbackUrls || [],item.imgurFallbackUrls || []));
      existing.imgurFallbackUrls = observed.filter(u => u !== existing.requestUrl).slice(0,3);
      item.imgurFallbackUrls = observed.filter(u => u !== item.requestUrl).slice(0,3);
    } else if (a && b) {
      item.imgurFallbackUrls = existing.imgurFallbackUrls;
    }
    if (newImgurAlias && existing.__failed && !existing.card) {
      // A newly observed concrete URL is independent evidence, not a blind retry.
      existing.__failed = false;
      existing.__thumbLoaded = false;
      existing.__thumbLoading = false;
      existing.__thumbCtx = null;
      existing.__thumbToken = 0;
      existing.__thumbRetry = 0;
      existing.__thumbImg = null;
      state.items.errorCount = Math.max(0,state.items.errorCount-1);
      invalidateVisibilityStats('imgur-alias-recovery');
      existing.dupCount = (existing.dupCount || 1)+1;
      mergeDupCluster(existing,item);
      registerSeen(key);
      return existing;
    }
    if (!keepOriginal && (preferOriginal || (!keepExplicit && (preferExplicit || betterByScore || betterByQuality)))) {
      const prevAllHidden = !!existing.__allHidden;
      const prevAutoHidden = !!existing.__autoHidden;
      const prevAutoHiddenReason = existing.__autoHiddenReason || '';
      const prevDup = existing.dupCount || 1;
      mergeBest(existing, item);
      existing.dupCount = prevDup + 1;
      if (existing.card) {
        updateCard(existing);
        updateDupBadge(existing);
      }
      if (
        prevAllHidden !== !!existing.__allHidden ||
        prevAutoHidden !== !!existing.__autoHidden ||
        prevAutoHiddenReason !== (existing.__autoHiddenReason || '')
      ) {
        scheduleApplyFilterSort(prevAllHidden !== !!existing.__allHidden ? 'origHidden' : 'autoHidden');
      }
      registerSeen(key);
      return existing;
    }
    const prevAllHidden = !!existing.__allHidden;
    const prevAutoHidden = !!existing.__autoHidden;
    const prevAutoHiddenReason = existing.__autoHiddenReason || '';
    let embedTypeChanged = false;
    let previewTextChanged = false;
    const mergedPreviews = uniqueThumbUrls((existing.previewUrls || []).concat(item.previewUrls || [])).slice(0,4);
    if (!existing.poster && item.poster) { existing.poster = item.poster; previewTextChanged = true; }
    if (!existing.__capturedPoster && item.__capturedPoster) { existing.__capturedPoster = item.__capturedPoster; previewTextChanged = true; }
    if (mergedPreviews.join('\n') !== (existing.previewUrls || []).join('\n')) { existing.previewUrls = mergedPreviews; previewTextChanged = true; }
    if (!existing.embedType && item.embedType) {
      existing.embedType = item.embedType;
      embedTypeChanged = true;
    }
    if (!existing.previewText && item.previewText) {
      existing.previewText = item.previewText;
      previewTextChanged = true;
    }
    existing.dupCount = (existing.dupCount || 1) + 1;
    existing.__allHidden = prevAllHidden && !!item.__allHidden;
    existing.__origHidden = existing.__allHidden;
    existing.__autoHidden = prevAutoHidden && !!item.__autoHidden;
    existing.__autoHiddenReason = existing.__autoHidden ? pickAutoHiddenReason(prevAutoHiddenReason, item.__autoHiddenReason || '') : '';
    mergeDupCluster(existing, item);
    if (existing.card) updateDupBadge(existing);
    if ((embedTypeChanged || previewTextChanged) && existing.card) updateCard(existing);
    if (
      prevAllHidden !== !!existing.__allHidden ||
      prevAutoHidden !== !!existing.__autoHidden ||
      prevAutoHiddenReason !== (existing.__autoHiddenReason || '')
    ) {
      if (existing.card) updateCard(existing);
      scheduleApplyFilterSort(prevAllHidden !== !!existing.__allHidden ? 'origHidden' : 'autoHidden');
    }
    registerSeen(key);
    return null;
  }
  registerSeen(key);
  if (item.__isNoise) state.items.noiseCount += 1;
  item.index = state.items.list.length;
  state.groupBest.set(key, item);
  if (item.kind === 'img') for (const u of item.renditionAliases || []) {
    const alias=canonicalMediaKey(u,'img');
    if (alias && !state.groupBest.has(alias)) state.groupBest.set(alias,item);
  }
  state.items.list.push(item);
  state.items.orderDirty=true;
  state.items.byId.set(item.id, item);
  return item;
}

function addItems(list) {
  reconcileLiveImageVisibility();
  if (!list || !list.length) return;
  let changed = false;
  list.forEach(function(item) {
    const adopted = adoptItem(item);
    if (adopted && !adopted.card) {
      changed = true;
      queueRender(adopted);
    }
  });
  if (changed) invalidateVisibilityStats('add-items');
}

/* [09] crawl */
function sameSite(url) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, location.href);
    if (u.origin === location.origin) return true;
    const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase();
    const allow = (state.cfg && state.cfg.sameSiteAllowHosts) || [];
    for (let i = 0; i < allow.length; i += 1) {
      if (host === String(allow[i] || '').toLowerCase()) return true;
    }
    return false;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function isSameOriginUrl(url, base) {
  const abs = normalizeCrawlCandidateUrl(url, base || location.href);
  if (!abs) return false;
  try {
    const u = new URL(abs);
    return u.origin === location.origin;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function normalizeCrawlCandidateUrl(url, base) {
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, base || location.href);
    if (
      location.protocol === 'https:' &&
      u.protocol === 'http:' &&
      u.hostname.toLowerCase() === location.hostname.toLowerCase() &&
      (!u.port || u.port === '80')
    ) {
      u.protocol = 'https:';
      u.port = '';
    }
    return u.toString();
  } catch (e) {
    return '';
  }
}

function normalizeCrawlUrl(url) {
  const normalized = normalizeCrawlCandidateUrl(url, location.href);
  if (!normalized) return '';
  return String(normalized).split('#')[0].trim();
}

function crawlQueueLength() {
  if (!state.crawl) return 0;
  const q = state.crawl.queue || [];
  const head = state.crawl.queueHead || 0;
  return Math.max(0, q.length - head);
}

function compactCrawlQueue() {
  if (!state.crawl) return;
  const q = state.crawl.queue || [];
  const head = state.crawl.queueHead || 0;
  if (!q.length || head >= q.length) {
    state.crawl.queue = [];
    state.crawl.queueHead = 0;
    return;
  }
  if (head > 32 && head * 2 >= q.length) {
    state.crawl.queue = q.slice(head);
    state.crawl.queueHead = 0;
  }
}

function popCrawlQueue() {
  if (!state.crawl) return '';
  const q = state.crawl.queue || [];
  const head = state.crawl.queueHead || 0;
  if (head >= q.length) return '';
  state.crawl.queueHead = head + 1;
  const next = q[head] || '';
  state.crawl.queuePriority.delete(next);
  compactCrawlQueue();
  return next;
}

function enqueueCrawlLinks(urls, priority, parent) {
  if (!urls || !urls.length) return;
  const maxQ = state.cfg.crawlMaxQueue || 200;
  const initial = normalizeCrawlUrl(location.href);
  const promoted = [];
  const q = state.crawl.queue;
  const head = state.crawl.queueHead || 0;
  const seen = new Set();
  let ordinal=0;
  for (const raw of urls) {
    const orderIndex=ordinal++;
    const clean = normalizeCrawlUrl(raw);
    if (!clean || clean === initial || seen.has(clean)) continue;
    seen.add(clean);
    if (state.crawl.visited.has(clean)) continue;
    if (state.crawl.queueDedup.has(clean)) {
      if (priority) {
        const pos = q.indexOf(clean, head);
        if (pos >= head) {
          promoted.push(q.splice(pos, 1)[0]);
          state.crawl.queuePriority.add(clean);
        }
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (crawlQueueLength() + promoted.length >= maxQ) {
      if (!priority) break;
      let evict = q.length - 1;
      while (evict >= head && state.crawl.queuePriority.has(q[evict])) evict--;
      if (evict < head) continue; // never evict a pending priority link
      const removed = q.splice(evict, 1)[0];
      if (!state.crawl.visited.has(removed)) { state.crawl.queueDedup.delete(removed); state.crawl.pageOrder.delete(removed); }
      perfCount('crawl.evict.normal');
    }
    state.crawl.queueDedup.add(clean);
    recordCrawlPageOrder(clean,parent,priority,orderIndex);
    if (priority) {
      promoted.push(clean);
      state.crawl.queuePriority.add(clean);
    } else q.push(clean);
    perfCount('crawl.enqueue');
  }
  if (promoted.length) q.splice(head, 0, ...promoted);
  perfGauge('crawl.queue', crawlQueueLength());
}

function collectCrawlLinksFromDocument(doc, baseUrl, details) {
  const linkScanStartedAt = perfStart('crawl.linkScan');
  perfCount('crawl.linkScan.document');
  const cfg = state.cfg;
  const out = [];
  const body = [];
  const nexts = [];
  const bodyKeys = new Set();
  const page = baseUrl || location.href;
  const base = getMediaDocumentBase(doc, page);
  const currentKey = normalizeCrawlUrl(page);
  const initialKey = normalizeCrawlUrl(location.href);
  const preferSameOrigin = !!cfg.crawlPreferSameOriginOnly;
  function allowUrl(u) {
    if (!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol)) return false;
    if (isImageResourceLink(u.href) || isDirectImageUrl(u.href) || isVideoUrl(u.href)) return false;
    const key = normalizeCrawlUrl(u.href);
    if (!key || key === currentKey || key === initialKey) return false;
    if (preferSameOrigin) return isSameOriginUrl(u.toString(), base);
    return sameSite(u.toString());
  }
  function pushCandidate(list, href) {
    if (!href) return;
    let u;
    try { u = new URL(href, base); } catch (e) { return; }
    if (!allowUrl(u)) return;
    list.push(u.toString());
  }
  const priLinks = Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll(
    'link[rel~=\"next\"][href], a[rel~=\"next\"][href], a[rel~=\"prev\"][href], #entryPageNav a[href], li.next > a[href], li.prev > a[href], a.next[href], a.prev[href]'
  ));
  priLinks.forEach(function(a) {
    pushCandidate(nexts, a.getAttribute('href'));
  });
  if (details) details.priorityLinks = nexts.slice();
  const links = Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll('a[href]'));
  links.forEach(function(a) {
    const href = a.getAttribute('href');
    if (!href || href.startsWith('#')) return;
    const inBody = inBodyScope(a) && !inAvoidScope(a);
    const list = inBody ? body : out;
    const before = list.length;
    pushCandidate(list, href);
    if (inBody && list.length > before && isSameOriginUrl(list[list.length - 1], base)) bodyKeys.add(normalizeCrawlUrl(list[list.length - 1]));
  });
  const merged = nexts.concat(body, out);
  const uniq = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  merged.forEach(function(raw) {
    const key = normalizeCrawlUrl(raw) || raw;
    if (seen.has(key)) return;
    seen.add(key);
    uniq.push(raw);
  });
  perfCount('crawl.linkScan.links', uniq.length);
  perfEnd('crawl.linkScan', linkScanStartedAt);
  if (details) { details.links = uniq.slice(); details.bodySameOriginCount = bodyKeys.size; }
  return uniq;
}

function collectCrawlLinks(doc, baseUrl, details) {
  const sourceDoc = doc || document;
  const sourceBase = baseUrl || location.href;
  const list = collectCrawlLinksFromDocument(sourceDoc, sourceBase, details);
  if (location.hostname.indexOf('weekly.ascii.jp') !== -1) {
    const rescue = new URL('img.html', location.href).toString();
    if (list.indexOf(rescue) === -1) list.unshift(rescue);
  }
  if (details) details.links = list.slice();
  return list;
}

function collectCrawlBootstrap(doc, baseUrl) {
  const sourceDoc = doc || document;
  const sourceBase = baseUrl || location.href;
  const details = {};
  const links = collectCrawlLinks(sourceDoc, sourceBase, details);
  perfCount('crawl.bootstrap.collect');
  return {
    links: links.slice(),
    priorityLinks: (details.priorityLinks || []).slice(),
    bodySameOriginCount: details.bodySameOriginCount || 0,
    pagerPlan: buildPagerPlan(sourceBase, sourceDoc)
  };
}

function buildPagerPlan(baseUrl, doc) {
  if (!isLivedoorArticle(baseUrl)) return null;
  const cap = state.cfg.crawlArticleMaxPages || 30;
  let baseKey = '';
  try {
    const u = new URL(baseUrl, location.href);
    u.search = '';
    u.hash = '';
    baseKey = u.toString();
  } catch (e) {
    return null;
  }
  let max = 1;
  let param = '';
  const links = Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll('a[href]'));
  links.forEach(function(a) {
    const href = a.getAttribute('href');
    if (!href) return;
    let u;
    try { u = new URL(href, baseUrl); } catch (e) { return; }
    if ((u.origin + u.pathname) !== baseKey) return;
    const p = u.searchParams.get('p');
    const page = u.searchParams.get('page');
    const raw = p || page;
    if (!raw) return;
    const n = parseInt(raw, 10);
    if (!n || n < 1) return;
    if (!param) param = page ? 'page' : 'p';
    if (n > max) max = n;
  });
  const numericMax = max;
  const pagerNodes = doc.querySelectorAll('.page-current, .article-inner-pager, .pager, .pagination, [class*="pager"], [class*="paging"], [id*="pager"]');
  for (const el of pagerNodes) {
    if (numericMax >= 2 && !el.matches('.page-current, .article-inner-pager')) {
      const linkedHere = Array.from(el.querySelectorAll('a[href]')).some(function(a) {
        try {
          const u = new URL(a.getAttribute('href'), baseUrl);
          return u.origin + u.pathname === baseKey && (u.searchParams.has('p') || u.searchParams.has('page'));
        } catch (e) { return false; }
      });
      if (!linkedHere) continue;
    }
    const marker = el.matches('.page-current') ? el : el.querySelector('.page-current');
    const text = String(marker ? marker.textContent : Array.from(el.childNodes).map(function(node) {
      return node.nodeType === 1 && node.matches('a[href],button') ? ' ' : node.textContent;
    }).join(' ') || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
    const m = /(?:^|[^\d])(\d{1,3})\s*\/\s*(\d{1,3})(?!\d)/.exec(text);
    if (!m) continue;
    const current = Number(m[1]), total = Number(m[2]);
    if (current >= 1 && current <= total && total > max) max = total;
  }
  if (max > 1 && !param) param = 'p';
  if (max > cap) max = cap;
  if (max < 2) return null;
  if (!param) param = 'p';
  const urls = [];
  for (let n = 2; n <= max; n += 1) {
    urls.push(baseKey + '?' + param + '=' + n);
  }
  return { urls: urls, baseKey: baseKey, param: param, max: max };
}

function isMediaDocument(doc) {
  try {
    const d = doc || document;
    const ct = (d.contentType || '').toLowerCase();
    if (ct.indexOf('image/') === 0 || ct.indexOf('video/') === 0) return true;
    const body = d.body;
    if (!body) return false;
    const kids = Array.from(body.children).filter(function(el) {
      return el.tagName !== 'SCRIPT' && el.tagName !== 'STYLE' && el !== state.ui.root;
    });
    if (kids.length === 1) {
      const tag = kids[0].tagName;
      if (tag === 'IMG' || tag === 'VIDEO') return true;
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  return false;
}

function countImageCandidates() {
  let count = 0;
  state.items.list.forEach(function(it) {
    if (it.kind !== 'img') return;
    if (state.cfg.noiseHide && it.__isNoise) return;
    if (it.__hashDup) return;
    if (it.__failed) return;
    count += 1;
  });
  return count;
}

function countMediaCandidates() {
  let count = 0;
  state.items.list.forEach(function(it) {
    if (it.kind !== 'img' && it.kind !== 'video') return;
    if (it.kind === 'img') {
      if (state.cfg.noiseHide && it.__isNoise) return;
      if (it.__hashDup) return;
      if (it.__failed) return;
    }
    count += 1;
  });
  return count;
}

function isHtmlLikeContentType(value) {
  const ct = String(value || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!ct) return true;
  if (ct.indexOf('text/html') !== -1) return true;
  if (ct.indexOf('application/xhtml+xml') !== -1) return true;
  return false;
}

function canStreamResponse(res) {
  return !!(res && res.body && typeof res.body.getReader === 'function' && typeof TextDecoder === 'function');
}

async function readResponseTextLimited(res, maxChars, maxBytes) {
  const limit = Math.max(1, maxChars || 300000);
  const byteLimit = Math.max(0, maxBytes || 0);
  if (!canStreamResponse(res)) {
    const text = await res.text();
    return String(text || '').slice(0, limit);
  }
  const reader = res.body.getReader();
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  let text = '';
  let totalBytes = 0;
  let cappedByBytes = false;
  try {
    while (text.length < limit) {
      const chunk = await reader.read();
      if (!chunk || chunk.done) break;
      const value = chunk.value;
      if (value) {
        totalBytes += Number(value.byteLength || value.length || 0);
      }
      if (byteLimit && totalBytes > byteLimit) {
        cappedByBytes = true;
        try { await reader.cancel('fmp-byte-cap'); } catch (e) {}
        break;
      }
      text += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
      if (text.length >= limit) {
        try { await reader.cancel('fmp-char-cap'); } catch (e) {}
        break;
      }
    }
    text += decoder.decode();
  } catch (e) {
    try { await reader.cancel(); } catch (e2) {}
  } finally {
    try { reader.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (cappedByBytes) {
    perfCount('crawl.byteCap');
    if (state.log && state.log.push) {
      state.log.push('crawl', 'stream byte cap bytes=' + totalBytes + ' limit=' + byteLimit);
    }
  }
  return text.slice(0, limit);
}

// Detached documents have no computed CSS. Apply only explicit complementary
// media rules to paired sibling IMG variants. Do not guess from viewport names
// alone, mount foreign DOM, load scripts, or bypass browser blockers.
const RESPONSIVE_PAIRS=[['pc','sp'],['pc-only','sp-only'],['desktop','mobile'],['desktop-only','mobile-only']];
const RESPONSIVE_CLASSES=new Set(RESPONSIVE_PAIRS.flat().map(n=>'.'+n));
const RESPONSIVE_CSS_MAX=8,RESPONSIVE_CSS_BYTES=524288,RESPONSIVE_CSS_TOTAL=1572864;
function responsiveSiblingPairs(doc,base) {
  const out=[];
  for(const names of RESPONSIVE_PAIRS){
    const [a,b]=names;
    const candidates=doc.querySelectorAll('img.'+a);
    for(const first of candidates){
      if(out.length>=256)return out;
      if(first.classList.contains(b) || !first.parentElement)continue;
      const siblings=Array.from(first.parentElement.children).filter(e=>e.tagName==='IMG');
      const peers=siblings.filter(e=>e.classList.contains(b)&&!e.classList.contains(a));
      if(peers.length!==1 || siblings.filter(e=>e.classList.contains(a)).length!==1)continue;
      const second=peers[0];
      // Require matching rendition filenames or matching nonempty alt text
      // plus a shared two-image slot. Class names alone are not identity.
      const file=e=>{
        try{
          const u=new URL(e.getAttribute('data-original')||e.getAttribute('data-src')||e.getAttribute('src')||'',base);
          if(!/^https?:$/.test(u.protocol))return '';
          return u.origin+u.pathname.replace(/(?:^|\/)(?:sp|pc|mobile|desktop)[_-](?=[^/]+$)/i,'/').replace(/[_-](?:sp|pc|mobile|desktop)(?=\.[a-z0-9]+$)/i,'')+u.search;
        }catch(e){return '';}
      };
      const x=file(first),y=file(second);
      const alt=(first.getAttribute('alt')||'').trim();
      if(!(x && x===y) && !(siblings.length===2 && alt && alt===(second.getAttribute('alt')||'').trim()))continue;
      out.push({names,first,second});
    }
  }
  return out;
}

function readResponsiveRules(rules,out,media) {
  for(const rule of rules){
    // Only CSSMediaRule and simple class selectors with important display
    // declarations are supported. Unknown nested contexts are not evaluated.
    if(rule.type===4 && rule.cssRules){
      const q=rule.conditionText||rule.media.mediaText;
      if(media.length<3)readResponsiveRules(rule.cssRules,out,media.concat(q));
    }else if(rule.type===1 && rule.style){
      const sel=(rule.selectorText||'').trim();
      if(!RESPONSIVE_CLASSES.has(sel))continue;
      const display=rule.style.getPropertyValue('display').trim().toLowerCase();
      if(!display)continue;
      out.push({name:sel.slice(1),display,important:rule.style.getPropertyPriority('display')==='important',media});
    }
  }
}
function parseResponsiveCss(text) {
  if(!text || text.length>=RESPONSIVE_CSS_BYTES || typeof CSSStyleSheet!=='function')return [];
  try{
    const sheet=new CSSStyleSheet();
    sheet.replaceSync(text);
    const out=[];readResponsiveRules(sheet.cssRules,out,[]);return out;
  }catch(e){return [];}
}
async function responsiveRulesForUrl(url,signal) {
  const map=state.crawl.responsiveCss;
  if(map.has(url))return map.get(url);
  if(map.size>=RESPONSIVE_CSS_MAX)return [];
  const work=(async()=>{
    // Prefer an already-loaded stylesheet; otherwise use one bounded,
    // same-origin CSS read. Cache results including failures for this run.
    for(const sheet of document.styleSheets){
      if(sheet.href!==url || sheet.disabled)continue;
      try{const out=[];readResponsiveRules(sheet.cssRules,out,[]);return out;}catch(e){}
    }
    try{
      if(state.crawl.responsiveCssBytes>=RESPONSIVE_CSS_TOTAL)return [];
      const res=await fetch(url,{signal,credentials:'same-origin',redirect:'error'});
      if(!res.ok || !isSameOriginUrl(res.url||url) || !/^text\/css(?:;|$)/i.test(res.headers.get('content-type')||''))return [];
      const len=Number(res.headers.get('content-length')||0);
      if(len>RESPONSIVE_CSS_BYTES || len>RESPONSIVE_CSS_TOTAL-state.crawl.responsiveCssBytes || !canStreamResponse(res)){
        try{await res.body?.cancel();}catch(e){}
        return [];
      }
      const reader=res.body.getReader(),decoder=new TextDecoder();
      let text='',bytes=0;
      try{
        for(;;){
          const {done,value}=await reader.read();if(done)break;
          bytes+=value.byteLength;
          state.crawl.responsiveCssBytes+=value.byteLength;
          if(bytes>=RESPONSIVE_CSS_BYTES || state.crawl.responsiveCssBytes>=RESPONSIVE_CSS_TOTAL){await reader.cancel();return [];}
          text+=decoder.decode(value,{stream:true});
        }
        text+=decoder.decode();
      }finally{try{reader.releaseLock();}catch(e){}}
      if(signal && signal.aborted)return [];
      return parseResponsiveCss(text);
    }catch(e){return [];}
  })();
  map.set(url,work);
  return work;
}
function responsiveHiddenSide(pair,rules,cache=new Map()) {
  const side=name=>{
    if(cache.has(name))return cache.get(name);
    const rs=rules.filter(r=>r.name===name);
    const hidden=rs.filter(r=>r.important && r.display==='none' && r.media.length);
    const matches=r=>r.media.every(q=>{try{return matchMedia(q).matches;}catch(e){return false;}});
    const active=rs.filter(matches);
    // Require an explicit responsive hide rule for BOTH sides, and no
    // active conflicting visible declaration. Never infer an absent rule.
    const result={declared:hidden.length>0,hidden:hidden.some(matches),
      conflict:active.some(r=>r.display!=='none'),unconditional:rs.some(r=>r.display==='none'&&!r.media.length)};
    cache.set(name,result);
    return result;
  };
  const a=side(pair.names[0]),b=side(pair.names[1]);
  if(!a.declared||!b.declared||a.conflict||b.conflict||a.unconditional||b.unconditional||a.hidden===b.hidden)return null;
  return a.hidden?pair.first:pair.second;
}
async function getDetachedResponsiveHidden(doc,page,signal) {
  const pairs=responsiveSiblingPairs(doc,getMediaDocumentBase(doc,page));
  if(!pairs.length)return null;
  const rules=[];
  for(const style of Array.from(doc.querySelectorAll('style')).slice(0,8)){
    if(style.media && !matchMedia(style.media).matches)continue;
    rules.push(...parseResponsiveCss(style.textContent));
  }
  const base=getMediaDocumentBase(doc,page);
  // Prioritize conventional shared/responsive stylesheets, never unrelated
  // font or third-party files; no new crawl pages or unbounded CSS discovery.
  const urls=[];
  for(const link of doc.querySelectorAll('link[rel~="stylesheet"][href]')){
    if(link.hasAttribute('disabled') || (link.media && !matchMedia(link.media).matches))continue;
    const url=resolveMediaReference(link.getAttribute('href'),base);
    if(!url || !isSameOriginUrl(url))continue;
    try{
      if(!/(?:^|\/)(?:common|style|responsive|layout|main|index|base)(?:[-_.][^/]*)?\.css$/i.test(new URL(url).pathname))continue;
    }catch(e){continue;}
    if(!urls.includes(url))urls.push(url);
  }
  // At most four CSS resources per qualifying document, eight per run.
  // Existing crawl cancellation and timeout also cover these reads.
  const sideCache=new Map();
  for(const url of urls.slice(0,4)){
    if(signal && signal.aborted)return null;
    rules.push(...await responsiveRulesForUrl(url,signal));
    sideCache.clear(); // rules/viewport may change across the await
    if(pairs.every(p=>responsiveHiddenSide(p,rules,sideCache)))break;
  }
  const hidden=new WeakSet();let count=0;
  for(const pair of pairs){
    const el=responsiveHiddenSide(pair,rules,sideCache);
    if(el){hidden.add(el);count++;}
  }
  if(count){perfCount('crawl.responsive.hidden',count);state.log.push('crawl','responsive hidden='+count+' '+page);}
  return count?hidden:null;
}

async function crawlFetch(url) {
  if (state.abort.aborted || state.crawl.stop) return;
  if (state.cfg.crawlPreferSameOriginOnly && !isSameOriginUrl(url)) return;
  const controller = makeController();
  const crawlStopped = function() {
    return state.abort.aborted || state.crawl.stop || !!(controller.signal && controller.signal.aborted);
  };
  const token = ++state.crawl.token;
  const timer = setTimeout(function() {
    try { controller.abort(); } catch (e) {}
  }, state.cfg.crawlTimeoutMs);
  state.crawl.inFlight += 1;
  const crawlRequestStartedAt = perfStart('crawl.request');
  perfGauge('crawl.inFlight', state.crawl.inFlight);
  updateProgress('net', token);
  try {
    const res = await fetch(url, { signal: controller.signal, credentials: 'same-origin' });
    if (crawlStopped()) return;
    if (!res.ok) return;
    const responseUrl = res.url || url;
    if (state.cfg.crawlPreferSameOriginOnly ? !isSameOriginUrl(responseUrl) : !sameSite(responseUrl)) {
      state.log.push('crawl', 'skip redirected out of scope ' + url);
      return;
    }
    if (state.crawl.mode === 'pager' && state.crawl.pagerBase) {
      const response = new URL(responseUrl);
      if (response.origin + response.pathname !== state.crawl.pagerBase) {
        state.log.push('crawl', 'skip redirect outside article ' + url);
        return;
      }
    }
    if (responseUrl !== url) {
      const alias=normalizeCrawlUrl(responseUrl);
      state.crawl.queueDedup.add(alias);
      if (!state.crawl.pageOrder.has(alias)) state.crawl.pageOrder.set(alias,pageOrderRank(url));
    }
    const contentType = (res.headers && typeof res.headers.get === 'function')
      ? (res.headers.get('content-type') || '')
      : '';
    if (!isHtmlLikeContentType(contentType)) {
      state.log.push('crawl', 'skip non-html ' + url + ' ct=' + (contentType || 'none'));
      return;
    }
    const contentLengthText = (res.headers && typeof res.headers.get === 'function') ? (res.headers.get('content-length') || '') : '';
    const contentLength = parseInt(contentLengthText || '0', 10) || 0;
    const maxBytes = state.cfg.crawlMaxHtmlBytes || 0;
    const streamable = canStreamResponse(res);
    if (maxBytes && contentLength > maxBytes && !streamable) {
      state.log.push('crawl', 'skip huge html ' + url + ' bytes=' + contentLength);
      return;
    }
    const html = await readResponseTextLimited(res, state.cfg.maxHtmlChars, maxBytes);
    if (crawlStopped()) return;
    const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(html, 'text/html');
    const responsiveHidden = await getDetachedResponsiveHidden(doc,responseUrl,controller.signal);
    if (crawlStopped()) return;
    const items = extractFromDocument(doc, 'crawl', responseUrl, responsiveHidden);
    if (crawlStopped()) return;
    if (!items.length) {
      state.log.push('crawl', '0 items (strict body-only) ' + url);
    }
    addItems(items);
    if (crawlStopped()) return;
    if (state.crawl.mode === 'pager') {
      const plan = buildPagerPlan(responseUrl, doc);
      if (plan && plan.baseKey === state.crawl.pagerBase && plan.urls && plan.urls.length) {
        enqueueCrawlLinks(plan.urls, true, responseUrl);
        state.crawl.limit = Math.max(state.crawl.limit,
          Math.min(state.cfg.crawlArticleMaxPages || 30, state.crawl.visited.size + crawlQueueLength() + 2));
      }
    } else {
      const details = {};
      const more = collectCrawlLinksFromDocument(doc, responseUrl, details).slice(0, state.cfg.crawlMaxNewLinksPerDoc);
      const priorityKeys = new Set((details.priorityLinks || []).map(normalizeCrawlUrl));
      enqueueCrawlLinks(more.filter(function(u) { return priorityKeys.has(normalizeCrawlUrl(u)); }), true, responseUrl);
      enqueueCrawlLinks(more.filter(function(u) { return !priorityKeys.has(normalizeCrawlUrl(u)); }), false, responseUrl);
    }
    updateProgress('crawl', token);
  } catch (e) {
    if (controller.signal && controller.signal.aborted) perfCount('crawl.request.abort');
    else perfCount('crawl.request.error');
    state.log.push('crawl', 'fetch error ' + url);
  } finally {
    clearTimeout(timer);
    state.abort.controllers.delete(controller);
    state.crawl.inFlight -= 1;
    perfGauge('crawl.inFlight', state.crawl.inFlight);
    perfGauge('crawl.queue', crawlQueueLength());
    perfEnd('crawl.request', crawlRequestStartedAt);
  }
}

function extractUrlsFromText(html, baseUrl) {
  const items = [];
  const re = /(https?:\/\/[^\s'"\\>]+?\.(png|jpe?g|gif|webp|avif|bmp|mp4|webm|mov|m4v))(\?|#|\b)/ig;
  let m;
  while ((m = re.exec(html))) {
    items.push(makeItem(isVideoUrl(m[1]) ? 'video' : 'img', m[1], {
      width: 0,
      height: 0,
      scope: '',
      source: 'crawl',
      openUrl: m[1]
    }, baseUrl));
  }
  return items;
}

async function startCrawl() {
  if (state.crawl.started) return;
  state.crawl.started = true;
  perfCount('crawl.start');
  state.crawl.queue = [];
  state.crawl.queueHead = 0;
  state.crawl.queueDedup.clear();
  state.crawl.queuePriority.clear();
  state.crawl.pageOrder.clear();
  state.crawl.mode = '';
  state.crawl.pagerBase = '';
  state.crawl.pagerParam = '';
  const bootstrap = state.cfg.crawlBootstrapReuse && state.crawl.bootstrap
    ? state.crawl.bootstrap
    : null;
  if (bootstrap) perfCount('crawl.bootstrap.reuse');
  const plan = bootstrap ? bootstrap.pagerPlan : buildPagerPlan(location.href, document);
  if (plan && plan.urls && plan.urls.length) {
    state.crawl.mode = 'pager';
    state.crawl.pagerBase = plan.baseKey;
    state.crawl.pagerParam = plan.param;
    enqueueCrawlLinks(plan.urls, true);
  } else {
    const details = {};
    const links = bootstrap ? bootstrap.links : collectCrawlLinks(document, location.href, details);
    const priority = bootstrap ? bootstrap.priorityLinks : details.priorityLinks;
    enqueueCrawlLinks(priority || [], true);
    enqueueCrawlLinks(links);
  }
  state.crawl.limit = state.cfg.crawlBaseLimit + (state.crawl.allowUserBoost ? state.cfg.crawlBoost : 0);
  if (state.crawl.mode === 'pager') {
    state.crawl.limit = Math.max(state.crawl.limit, crawlQueueLength() + 2);
  }
  perfGauge('crawl.queue', crawlQueueLength());
  state.log.push('crawl', 'start mode=' + (state.crawl.mode || 'normal') + ' queue=' + crawlQueueLength() + ' limit=' + state.crawl.limit + ' visited=' + state.crawl.visited.size + ' inFlight=' + state.crawl.inFlight + ' errors=' + state.items.errorCount);
  if (state.crawl.timer) clearTimeout(state.crawl.timer);
  state.crawl.timer = setTimeout(function() { state.crawl.timer = 0; pumpCrawl(); }, 0);
}

async function pumpCrawl() {
  if (state.abort.aborted || state.crawl.stop) {
    state.crawl.done = true;
    maybeFinalize();
    return;
  }
  while (state.crawl.inFlight < state.cfg.crawlConcurrent && crawlQueueLength()) {
    if (state.crawl.visited.size >= state.crawl.limit) break;
    const url = popCrawlQueue();
    if (!url) continue;
    if (state.crawl.visited.has(url)) continue;
    state.crawl.visited.add(url);
    crawlFetch(url).then(pumpCrawl);
  }
  if (!state.crawl.boostedAuto && state.crawl.visited.size >= state.cfg.crawlBaseLimit) {
    const mediaCount = countMediaCandidates();
    if (mediaCount < state.cfg.htmlMinItems) {
      const nextLimit = Math.min(state.cfg.crawlBaseLimit + state.cfg.crawlBoost, state.cfg.crawlBoostLimit || state.cfg.crawlBaseLimit);
      if (nextLimit > state.crawl.limit) {
        state.crawl.limit = nextLimit;
        state.crawl.boostedAuto = true;
        state.log.push('crawl', 'auto-boost limit=' + state.crawl.limit + ' media=' + mediaCount);
        if (!state.crawl.inFlight && crawlQueueLength()) return pumpCrawl();
      }
    }
  }
  if (state.crawl.inFlight === 0 && (crawlQueueLength() === 0 || state.crawl.visited.size >= state.crawl.limit)) {
    state.crawl.done = true;
    if (state.ui.stopBtn) state.ui.stopBtn.classList.remove('danger');
    updateProgress('idle', 'done');
    state.log.push('crawl', 'done visited=' + state.crawl.visited.size + ' inFlight=' + state.crawl.inFlight + ' limit=' + state.crawl.limit + ' errors=' + state.items.errorCount);
    maybeFinalize();
  }
}

function boostCrawl(reason) {
  if (state.crawl.allowUserBoost) return;
  state.crawl.allowUserBoost = true;
  state.crawl.limit = Math.max(state.crawl.limit, state.cfg.crawlBaseLimit + state.cfg.crawlBoost);
  state.log.push('boost', reason || 'user');
  if (state.crawl.started && !state.crawl.stop && !state.abort.aborted && crawlQueueLength() && state.crawl.visited.size < state.crawl.limit) {
    state.crawl.done = false;
    pumpCrawl();
  }
}

    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .embed { width: 100%; border: 0; height: ${state.cfg.embedHeight}px; }

    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb { padding-top: calc(env(safe-area-inset-top) + 44px); padding-bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 76px); padding-inline: max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-left)) max(12px, env(safe-area-inset-right)); }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-frame { border-radius: 18px; }

    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-frame :is(img,video,canvas) { border-radius: 14px; }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-controls,
    :where(.fmp-css-scope).wrap:is(.is-iphone,.is-ipad) .lb-controls {
      display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(44px,1fr));
      left: max(10px, env(safe-area-inset-left)); right: max(10px, env(safe-area-inset-right));
      bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 8px); max-width: 420px; margin-inline: auto;
      gap: 4px; padding: 5px; border-radius: 22px;
      background: var(--fmp-control-bg, #111); border: 1px solid var(--fmp-border-soft, #444);
    }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope).wrap:is(.is-iphone,.is-ipad) .lb-controls { margin-left: 0; margin-right: auto; max-width: 330px; }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb .lb-controls.zoom-ready { grid-template-columns: repeat(6, minmax(44px,1fr)); }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-controls .lb-btn {
      padding: 4px 2px; min-width: 44px; min-height: 44px; border-radius: 16px;
      background: var(--fmp-card-bg, #0b0b0b); color: var(--fmp-text, #eee);
      font-size: 12px; white-space: nowrap;
    }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-controls .lb-btn[aria-label="Lightboxを閉じる"] { background: var(--fmp-accent, #fff); color: var(--fmp-text-on-accent, #111); }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-controls .lb-btn[aria-pressed="true"],
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-controls .lb-btn[aria-expanded="true"] { background: var(--fmp-accent, #fff); color: var(--fmp-text-on-accent, #111); }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-zoom-btn { position: static; }
    @media (max-width: 319px) {
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb .lb-controls, :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb .lb-controls.zoom-ready { grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(44px,1fr)); }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb { padding-bottom: calc(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) + 124px); }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-frame,
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-frame :is(img,video,canvas,iframe) { max-height: calc(100vh - 176px - env(safe-area-inset-top) - env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); }
    }
    :where(.fmp-css-scope) .lb-zoom-btn[hidden] { display: none !important; }
    @media (min-width: 900px) {
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="compact"] { grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(130px, 100%), 1fr)); }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="large"] { grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(260px, 100%), 1fr)); }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="compact"] .thumb :is(img,video,.ph) { height: 130px !important; }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="large"] .thumb :is(img,video,.ph) { height: 260px !important; }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="compact"] .card { min-height: 170px !important; }
      :where(.fmp-css-scope) .grid[data-density="large"] .card { min-height: 300px !important; }
    }
  `;
  const wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'wrap fmp-css-scope';
  if (state.env.isIPhone) wrap.classList.add('is-iphone');
  if (state.env.isIPad) wrap.classList.add('is-ipad');
  if (state.cfg.dmThemeTokensEnabled && state.cfg.dmClassStylesEnabled) wrap.classList.add('dm-class-styles');
  if (state.cfg.dmEmbedSurfaceEnabled) wrap.classList.add('dm-embed-surface');
  const bg = document.createElement('div');
  bg.className = 'bg';
  bg.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'none', 'important');
  bg.style.setProperty('z-index', '0', 'important');
  const panel = document.createElement('div');
  panel.className = 'panel';
  panel.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');
  panel.style.setProperty('z-index', '1', 'important');

  const header = document.createElement('div');
  header.className = 'header';
  const title = document.createElement('div');
  title.className = 'title';
  title.textContent = 'IMG View rev' + SCRIPT_REV;
  const btns = document.createElement('div');
  btns.className = 'btns';
  const closeBtn = mkBtn('閉じる', 'btn');
  const stopBtn = mkBtn('巡回停止', 'btn danger');
  const logBtn = mkBtn('ログコピー', 'btn');
  btns.appendChild(closeBtn);
  btns.appendChild(stopBtn);
  btns.appendChild(logBtn);
  header.appendChild(title);
  header.appendChild(btns);

  const filterBar = document.createElement('div');
  filterBar.className = 'chips';
  filterBar.setAttribute('role', 'group');
  filterBar.setAttribute('aria-label', 'メディアフィルター');
  const filterButtons = [];
  const labels = {ALL:'すべて', BODY:'本文', IMG:'画像', VIDEO:'動画', EXTVIDEO:'外部動画'};
  ['ALL', 'BODY', 'IMG', 'VIDEO', 'X', 'INSTA', 'IMGUR', 'YT', 'TT', 'EXTVIDEO'].forEach(function(key) {
    const label = labels[key] || key;
    const chip = mkBtn(label, 'chip');
    chip.setAttribute('data-filter', key);
    chip.dataset.baseLabel = label;
    chip.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
    filterBar.appendChild(chip);
    filterButtons.push(chip);
  });
  const densityChip = mkBtn('一覧：標準', 'chip');
  densityChip.setAttribute('data-action', 'density');
  densityChip.setAttribute('aria-label', '一覧の表示密度：標準。押すと変更');
  filterBar.insertBefore(densityChip, filterButtons[4]);
  const hiddenChip = mkBtn('非表示も表示', 'chip');
  hiddenChip.setAttribute('data-action', 'toggle-hidden');
  hiddenChip.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  hiddenChip.setAttribute('aria-label', '非表示画像の表示切替');
  filterBar.appendChild(hiddenChip);
  const noiseChip = mkBtn('ノイズも表示', 'chip');
  noiseChip.setAttribute('data-action', 'toggle-noise');
  noiseChip.setAttribute('aria-label', 'ノイズ画像の表示切替');
  noiseChip.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  filterBar.appendChild(noiseChip);
  const boostChip = mkBtn('巡回+', 'chip');
  boostChip.setAttribute('data-action', 'boost-crawl');
  boostChip.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  boostChip.setAttribute('aria-label', '巡回範囲を拡張');
  filterBar.appendChild(boostChip);
  filterBar.insertBefore(hiddenChip, filterButtons[4]);
  filterBar.insertBefore(noiseChip, filterButtons[4]);
  filterBar.insertBefore(boostChip, filterButtons[4]);

  const info = document.createElement('div');
  info.className = 'info';
  info.textContent = 'loading...';

  const progress = document.createElement('div');
  progress.className = 'progress';
  progress.textContent = 'idle';

  const grid = document.createElement('div');
  grid.className = 'grid';

  panel.appendChild(header);
  panel.appendChild(filterBar);
  panel.appendChild(info);
  panel.appendChild(progress);
  panel.appendChild(grid);

  wrap.appendChild(bg);
  wrap.appendChild(panel);
  mount.appendChild(style);
  mount.appendChild(wrap);

  state.ui.root = root;
  state.ui.shadow = shadow;
  state.ui.wrap = wrap;
  state.ui.header = header;
  state.ui.filterBar = filterBar;
  state.ui.filterButtons = filterButtons;
  state.ui.hiddenChip = hiddenChip;
  state.ui.noiseChip = noiseChip;
  state.ui.boostChip = boostChip;
  state.ui.stopBtn = stopBtn;
  state.ui.logBtn = logBtn;
  state.ui.grid = grid;
  state.ui.densityChip = densityChip;
  state.ui.info = info;
  state.ui.progress = progress;

  function bindUiTap(el, handler) {
    if (!el || typeof handler !== 'function') return;
    // The filter rail delegates several buttons. Suppress duplicate
    // pointer/touch/click events per actual button, not across the rail.
    const gestures = new WeakMap();
    const onTap = function(ev) {
      const raw = ev.target && ev.target.nodeType === 1 ? ev.target : ev.target && ev.target.parentElement;
      const target = el === filterBar ? raw && raw.closest('button') : el;
      if (!target || (el === filterBar && !el.contains(target))) return;
      let g = gestures.get(target);
      if (!g) { g = {touchAt:0, nonClickAt:0, nonClickType:''}; gestures.set(target,g); }
      const now = Date.now();
      if (ev.type === 'click' && ev.detail !== 0 && now - g.touchAt < 450) {
        try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
        return;
      }
      if (ev.type !== 'click') {
        if (ev.type !== g.nonClickType && now - g.nonClickAt < 120) {
          try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
          return;
        }
        g.nonClickAt = now;
        g.nonClickType = ev.type;
        g.touchAt = now;
        try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
      }
      handler(ev);
    };
    el.addEventListener('click', onTap);
    el.addEventListener('pointerup', onTap);
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onTap, { passive: false });
  }

  bindUiTap(closeBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    cleanupUi();
    finalize('ok', { reason: 'closed' });
  });
  bindUiTap(stopBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    abortAll('user');
    state.crawl.stop = true;
    state.crawl.queue.length = 0;
    state.crawl.queueHead = 0;
    state.crawl.queueDedup.clear();
    state.crawl.queuePriority.clear();
    if (state.crawl.timer) {
      clearTimeout(state.crawl.timer);
      state.crawl.timer = 0;
    }
    state.log.push('crawl', 'stopped visited=' + state.crawl.visited.size + ' inFlight=' + state.crawl.inFlight + ' limit=' + state.crawl.limit + ' errors=' + state.items.errorCount);
    updateProgress('idle', 'stopped');
  });
  bindUiTap(logBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    if (state.log.copy) state.log.copy();
  });

  let filterRailTouchX = 0;
  let filterRailStartScrollLeft = 0;
  let filterRailMoved = false;
  filterBar.addEventListener('touchstart', function(ev) {
    const t = ev.touches && ev.touches[0];
    filterRailTouchX = t ? t.clientX : 0;
    filterRailStartScrollLeft = filterBar.scrollLeft || 0;
    filterRailMoved = false;
  }, { passive: true });
  filterBar.addEventListener('touchmove', function(ev) {
    const t = ev.touches && ev.touches[0];
    if (!t) return;
    if (Math.abs(t.clientX - filterRailTouchX) > 8 ||
        Math.abs((filterBar.scrollLeft || 0) - filterRailStartScrollLeft) > 4) {
      filterRailMoved = true;
    }
  }, { passive: true });
  filterBar.addEventListener('touchcancel', function() {
    filterRailMoved = false;
  }, { passive: true });

  bindUiTap(filterBar, function(ev) {
    if (filterRailMoved) {
      filterRailMoved = false;
      return;
    }
    const target = ev.target && ev.target.nodeType === 1 ? ev.target : (ev.target && ev.target.parentElement ? ev.target.parentElement : null);
    if (!target || !target.closest) return;
    const densityBtn = target.closest('[data-action="density"]');
    if (densityBtn) {
      const modes = ['normal','compact','large'];
      const next = modes[(modes.indexOf(state.ui.density) + 1) % modes.length];
      setGridDensity(next);
      return;
    }
    const hiddenBtn = target.closest('[data-action="toggle-hidden"]');
    if (hiddenBtn) {
      state.cfg.hiddenHide = !state.cfg.hiddenHide;
      setInfoTemp(state.cfg.hiddenHide ? '非表示画像OFF' : '非表示画像ON', 1200);
      applyFilterSort();
      return;
    }
    const noiseBtn = target.closest('[data-action="toggle-noise"]');
    if (noiseBtn) {
      state.cfg.noiseHide = !state.cfg.noiseHide;
      state.ui.lastAllTap = 0;
      setInfoTemp(state.cfg.noiseHide ? 'ノイズ画像を非表示' : 'ノイズ画像も表示', 1200);
      applyFilterSort();
      return;
    }
    const actionBtn = target.closest('[data-action="boost-crawl"]');
    if (actionBtn) {
      if (state.crawl.allowUserBoost) {
        setInfoTemp('巡回拡張済', 900);
        return;
      }
      boostCrawl('chip');
      setInfoTemp('巡回拡張ON', 900);
      updateChipUI();
      return;
    }
    const btn = target.closest('[data-filter]');
    if (!btn) return;
    const key = btn.getAttribute('data-filter');
    if (key === 'ALL') {
      const now = Date.now();
      if (state.ui.lastAllTap && now - state.ui.lastAllTap < 500) {
        state.cfg.noiseHide = !state.cfg.noiseHide;
        setInfoTemp(state.cfg.noiseHide ? 'ノイズ表示OFF' : 'ノイズ表示ON', 1200);
        state.ui.lastAllTap = 0;
      } else state.ui.lastAllTap = now;
    } else state.ui.lastAllTap = 0;
    setFilter(key);
  });

  updateChipUI();
  initThumbLoader();
}

function setGridDensity(mode) {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.ui.grid || !state.ui.densityChip) return;
  const labels = {normal:'標準',compact:'小',large:'大'};
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(labels, mode) || state.ui.density === mode) return;
  state.ui.density = mode;
  setAttrIfChanged(state.ui.grid, 'data-density', mode);
  setTextIfChanged(state.ui.densityChip, '一覧：' + labels[mode]);
  setAttrIfChanged(state.ui.densityChip, 'aria-label', '一覧の表示密度：' + labels[mode] + '。押すと変更');
  // IO handles layout changes. Only the non-IO fallback needs a rescan.
  if (!state.thumb.observer && typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function') state.thumb.scanNow();
}

function mountShell(attempt) {
  const root = state.ui.root;
  if (!root || root.isConnected) return;
  const host = document.body || document.documentElement;
  try {
    if (host) host.appendChild(root);
  } catch (e) {}
  if (root.isConnected) perfCount('shell.mount');
  if (!root.isConnected && (attempt || 0) < 12) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() { mountShell((attempt || 0) + 1); });
  }
}

function lockPage(state) {
  if (state.ui.locked) return;
  try { state.ui.prevOverflow = document.documentElement.style.overflow || ''; } catch (e) {}
  try { state.ui.prevBodyOverflow = document.body ? (document.body.style.overflow || '') : ''; } catch (e) {}
  try { document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden'; } catch (e) {}
  try { if (document.body) document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; } catch (e) {}
  try { if (document.documentElement) document.documentElement.dataset.fmpLocked = '1'; } catch (e) {}
  try { if (document.body) document.body.dataset.fmpLocked = '1'; } catch (e) {}
  if (state.ui.root) state.ui.root.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  state.ui.locked = true;
}

function unlockPage(state) {
  const htmlMarked = document.documentElement && document.documentElement.dataset && document.documentElement.dataset.fmpLocked === '1';
  const bodyMarked = document.body && document.body.dataset && document.body.dataset.fmpLocked === '1';
  if (!state.ui.locked && !htmlMarked && !bodyMarked) {
    if (state.ui.root) state.ui.root.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    return;
  }
  if (htmlMarked) {
    try { document.documentElement.style.overflow = state.ui.prevOverflow || ''; } catch (e) {}
    try { delete document.documentElement.dataset.fmpLocked; } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (bodyMarked) {
    try { if (document.body) document.body.style.overflow = state.ui.prevBodyOverflow || ''; } catch (e) {}
    try { if (document.body) delete document.body.dataset.fmpLocked; } catch (e) {}
  }
  if (state.ui.root) state.ui.root.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  state.ui.locked = false;
}

function isUiVisible(state) {
  const root = state.ui.root;
  const wrap = state.ui.wrap;
  const grid = state.ui.grid;
  if (!root || !root.isConnected) return false;
  if (!wrap || !wrap.isConnected) return false;
  if (!grid || !grid.isConnected) return false;
  try {
    const rect = grid.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.height < 60) return false;
    const cs = getComputedStyle(wrap);
    if (cs.display === 'none') return false;
    if (cs.visibility === 'hidden') return false;
    if (cs.opacity === '0') return false;
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function restoreUiState() {
  if (!state.ui.root || !state.ui.root.isConnected) return;
  if (state.ui.locked) {
    try { if (document.documentElement) { document.documentElement.style.overflow = 'hidden'; document.documentElement.dataset.fmpLocked = '1'; } } catch (e) {}
    try { if (document.body) { document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'; document.body.dataset.fmpLocked = '1'; } } catch (e) {}
    state.ui.root.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  } else {
    state.ui.root.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  }
  if (state.lb.open && state.lb.overlay) {
    state.lb.overlay.classList.add('open');
  }
}

function bindVisibilityHandlers() {
  if (state.ui.boundVis) return;
  state.ui.boundVis = true;
  if (!state.ui.onVisibilityChange) {
    state.ui.onVisibilityChange = function() {
      if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') restoreUiState();
    };
  }
  if (!state.ui.onPageShow) {
    state.ui.onPageShow = function() {
      restoreUiState();
    };
  }
  if (!state.ui.onFocus) {
    state.ui.onFocus = function() {
      restoreUiState();
    };
  }
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', state.ui.onVisibilityChange);
  window.addEventListener('pageshow', state.ui.onPageShow);
  window.addEventListener('focus', state.ui.onFocus);
}

function showBootBadge(message) {
  const wrap = state.ui.wrap;
  if (!wrap) return;
  const useClassStyles = !!(state.cfg.dmThemeTokensEnabled && state.cfg.dmClassStylesEnabled);
  const badge = document.createElement('div');
  badge.textContent = message;
  if (useClassStyles) {
    badge.className = 'dm-boot-badge';
  } else {
    badge.style.position = 'fixed';
    badge.style.top = '12px';
    badge.style.right = '12px';
    badge.style.zIndex = '2147483647';
    badge.style.background = '#111';
    badge.style.color = '#eee';
    badge.style.border = '1px solid #444';
    badge.style.borderRadius = '10px';
    badge.style.padding = '6px 8px';
    badge.style.fontSize = '11px';
  }
  wrap.appendChild(badge);
  setTimeout(function() {
    if (badge.parentNode) badge.parentNode.removeChild(badge);
  }, 1800);
}

function showBootBadgeGlobal(message) {
  const host = document.body || document.documentElement;
  if (!host) return;
  const badge = document.createElement('div');
  badge.textContent = message;
  badge.style.position = 'fixed';
  badge.style.top = '12px';
  badge.style.right = '12px';
  badge.style.zIndex = '2147483647';
  badge.style.background = '#111';
  badge.style.color = '#eee';
  badge.style.border = '1px solid #444';
  badge.style.borderRadius = '10px';
  badge.style.padding = '6px 8px';
  badge.style.fontSize = '11px';
  host.appendChild(badge);
  setTimeout(function() {
    if (badge.parentNode) badge.parentNode.removeChild(badge);
  }, 1800);
}

/* [11] cards */
function mkBtn(text, cls) {
  const btn = document.createElement('button');
  btn.type = 'button';
  btn.textContent = text;
  btn.className = cls;
  btn.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
  btn.style.touchAction = 'manipulation';
  return btn;
}

function showThumbOverlay(card, text) {
  if (!card) return null;
  const thumb = card.querySelector('.thumb');
  if (!thumb) return null;
  let overlay = thumb.querySelector('.ph-load');
  if (!overlay) {
    overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'ph-load';
    thumb.appendChild(overlay);
  }
  overlay.textContent = text || 'LOADING';
  if (overlay.__hideTimer) {
    clearTimeout(overlay.__hideTimer);
    overlay.__hideTimer = 0;
  }
  overlay.style.display = 'flex';
  overlay.style.opacity = '0';
  return overlay;
}

function clearOverlayShowTimer(item) {
  if (!item) return;
  if (item.__overlayShowTimer) {
    clearTimeout(item.__overlayShowTimer);
    item.__overlayShowTimer = 0;
  }
}

function scheduleOverlayShow(item, card, delayMs) {
  if (!item || !card) return;
  clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
  const wait = typeof delayMs === 'number' ? delayMs : 120;
  item.__overlayShowTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    item.__overlayShowTimer = 0;
    if (!item.__thumbLoading) return;
    if (!card || !card.isConnected) return;

    const showNow = function() {
      if (!item.__thumbLoading) return;
      if (!card || !card.isConnected) return;
      const overlay = showThumbOverlay(card, 'LOADING');
      if (!overlay || !overlay.isConnected) return;
      if (overlay.style.display === 'none') return;
      overlay.style.opacity = '1';
    };

    if (state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
      thumbQueueUi(showNow);
      return;
    }
    showNow();
  }, wait);
}

function hideThumbOverlay(card) {
  if (!card) return;
  const overlay = card.querySelector('.ph-load');
  if (!overlay) return;
  if (overlay.__hideTimer) {
    clearTimeout(overlay.__hideTimer);
    overlay.__hideTimer = 0;
  }
  const removeOverlay = function() {
    try {
      if (overlay && overlay.parentNode) overlay.parentNode.removeChild(overlay);
      else if (overlay) overlay.style.display = 'none';
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  if (overlay.style.opacity === '0') {
    removeOverlay();
    return;
  }
  overlay.style.opacity = '0';
  overlay.__hideTimer = setTimeout(function() {
    overlay.__hideTimer = 0;
    removeOverlay();
  }, 160);
}

function thumbQueueUi(fn) {
  try {
    if (state.ui.disposed || !state.thumb) return;
    if (!state.thumb.uiQueue) state.thumb.uiQueue = [];
    if (typeof state.thumb.uiQueueHead !== 'number') state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
    state.thumb.uiQueue.push(fn);
    if (!state.thumb.pausedByScroll) thumbScheduleUiFlush();
  } catch (e) {}
}

function thumbScheduleUiFlush() {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.thumb) return;
  if (state.thumb.uiFlushScheduled) return;
  state.thumb.uiFlushScheduled = true;
  state.thumb.uiFlushRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    state.thumb.uiFlushRaf = 0;
    thumbFlushUiQueue();
  });
}

function thumbFlushUiQueue() {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.thumb) return;
  if (state.thumb.uiFlushRaf) cancelAnimationFrame(state.thumb.uiFlushRaf);
  state.thumb.uiFlushRaf = 0;
  state.thumb.uiFlushScheduled = false;
  if (state.thumb.pausedByScroll) return;
  const q = state.thumb.uiQueue;
  if (!q || !q.length) {
    state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
    return;
  }
  let head = state.thumb.uiQueueHead || 0;
  if (head >= q.length) {
    q.length = 0;
    state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
    return;
  }

  const batch = state.cfg.thumbUiBatchPerFrame ||
    ((state.env && state.env.isIPhone) ? 6 : 12);

  let n = 0;
  while (n < batch && head < q.length) {
    const fn = q[head];
    q[head++] = null;
    try { if (fn) fn(); } catch (e) {}
    n++;
  }
  state.thumb.uiQueueHead = head;
  if (head >= q.length) {
    q.length = 0;
    state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
  } else if (head > 64 && head * 2 >= q.length) {
    state.thumb.uiQueue = q.slice(head);
    state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
  }
  const remain = state.thumb.uiQueue && ((state.thumb.uiQueue.length - (state.thumb.uiQueueHead || 0)) > 0);
  if (remain) thumbScheduleUiFlush();
}

function applyTempGpuClass(img) {
  if (!img || !img.classList) return;
  if (!state.env || !state.env.isIOS) return;
  if (img.__fmpGpuActive) return;
  img.__fmpGpuActive = true;
  try {
    if (img.__fmpGpuRaf1) cancelAnimationFrame(img.__fmpGpuRaf1);
    if (img.__fmpGpuRaf2) cancelAnimationFrame(img.__fmpGpuRaf2);
  } catch (e) {}
  img.classList.add('fmp-gpu');
  img.__fmpGpuRaf1 = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    img.__fmpGpuRaf2 = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      try {
        if (img && img.classList) img.classList.remove('fmp-gpu');
      } catch (e) {}
      img.__fmpGpuActive = false;
      img.__fmpGpuRaf1 = 0;
      img.__fmpGpuRaf2 = 0;
    });
  });
}

function queueRepaint(img) {
  if (!img || !img.isConnected || !state.thumb) return;
  if (!state.thumb.repaintSet) state.thumb.repaintSet = new Set();
  state.thumb.repaintSet.add(img);
  if (state.thumb.repaintRaf) return;
  state.thumb.repaintRaf = requestAnimationFrame(flushRepaintQueue);
}

function flushRepaintQueue() {
  if (!state.thumb) return;
  state.thumb.repaintRaf = 0;
  const set = state.thumb.repaintSet;
  if (!set || !set.size) return;

  const maxPerFrame = state.env && state.env.isIPhone ? 4 : (state.env && state.env.isIPad ? 6 : 8);
  const list = [];
  set.forEach(function(img) {
    if (list.length >= maxPerFrame) return;
    list.push(img);
  });
  list.forEach(function(img) {
    try { set.delete(img); } catch (e) {}
  });

  if (!state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
    try {
      const host = document.body || document.documentElement;
      if (host) void host.offsetHeight;
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  const restore = [];
  list.forEach(function(img) {
    if (!img || !img.isConnected) return;
    applyTempGpuClass(img);
    forceRepaintImage(img, restore);
  });
  if (restore.length) {
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      restore.forEach(function(fn) {
        try { if (fn) fn(); } catch (e) {}
      });
    });
  }
  if (set.size && !state.thumb.repaintRaf) {
    state.thumb.repaintRaf = requestAnimationFrame(flushRepaintQueue);
  }
}

function thumbApplyFit(img) {
  try {
    if (!img || !img.isConnected) return;
    const w = img.naturalWidth || 0;
    const h = img.naturalHeight || 0;
    if (!w || !h) return;
    const box = (state.env && state.env.thumbBoxPx) ? state.env.thumbBoxPx : ((state.env && state.env.isWide) ? 180 : 140);
    const cw = Math.round(box || 0);
    const ch = Math.round(box || 0);
    if (!cw || !ch) return;

    if (w <= cw && h <= ch) {
      img.style.objectFit = 'none';
      img.style.objectPosition = 'center';
    } else {
      img.style.objectFit = 'contain';
      img.style.objectPosition = 'center';
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

const VISUAL_W=64,VISUAL_H=40,VISUAL_MAX=256;
function visualEligible(item) {
  return item && !item.__failed && !isSourceSuppressed(item) &&
    !(state.cfg.hiddenHide && (item.__allHidden || item.__autoHidden)) &&
    !(state.cfg.noiseHide && item.__isNoise);
}

function visualSample(item,img) {
  const w=img.naturalWidth,h=img.naturalHeight;
  if(w<80 || h<64 || w*h<10000)return null;
  const actual=getDisplayUrl(item);
  let u;
  try{u=new URL(actual,location.href);}catch(e){return null;}
  if(u.origin!==location.origin || !/\.jpe?g$/i.test(u.pathname))return null;
  const q=new URLSearchParams(u.search);
  for(const key of ['w','h','width','height','dpr','__ivb_retry'])q.delete(key);
  try{
    const canvas=state.items.visualCanvas || (state.items.visualCanvas=document.createElement('canvas'));
    canvas.width=VISUAL_W;canvas.height=VISUAL_H;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
    if(!ctx)return null;
    ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';
    ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,VISUAL_W,VISUAL_H);
    const pixels=ctx.getImageData(0,0,VISUAL_W,VISUAL_H).data;
    let sum=0,sq=0;
    for(let i=0;i<pixels.length;i+=4){
      if(pixels[i+3]!==255)return null;
      const l=(pixels[i]*3+pixels[i+1]*6+pixels[i+2])/10;
      sum+=l;sq+=l*l;
    }
    const n=VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H,mean=sum/n;
    if(sq/n-mean*mean<324)return null;
    return {item,url:actual,w,h,pixels,query:q.toString(),host:u.host,page:item.__orderPage,scope:item.scope,provider:getMediaProviderType(item)};
  }catch(e){return null;}
}

function visualPixelsMatch(a,b,crop) {
  let ax=1,bx=1,ay=1,by=1;
  if(crop){
    const ar=a.w/a.h,br=b.w/b.h;
    if(crop===1){if(ar>br)ax=br/ar;else bx=ar/br;}
    else {if(ar>br)by=br/ar;else ay=ar/br;}
  }
  const sample=(p,x,y,c)=>{
    const ix=Math.max(0,Math.min(VISUAL_W-1,x)),iy=Math.max(0,Math.min(VISUAL_H-1,y));
    const x0=Math.floor(ix),x1=Math.min(VISUAL_W-1,x0+1),y0=Math.floor(iy),y1=Math.min(VISUAL_H-1,y0+1);
    const dx=ix-x0,dy=iy-y0;
    return (p[(y0*VISUAL_W+x0)*4+c]*(1-dx)+p[(y0*VISUAL_W+x1)*4+c]*dx)*(1-dy)+
      (p[(y1*VISUAL_W+x0)*4+c]*(1-dx)+p[(y1*VISUAL_W+x1)*4+c]*dx)*dy;
  };
  let total=0,bad=0;
  const tiles=new Float32Array(16);
  const coarse=new Float32Array((VISUAL_W/2)*(VISUAL_H/2)*3);
  for(let y=0;y<VISUAL_H;y++)for(let x=0;x<VISUAL_W;x++){
    let d=0;
    for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
      const i=(y*VISUAL_W+x)*4+c;
      const v=crop?sample(a.pixels,(x-(VISUAL_W-1)/2)*ax+(VISUAL_W-1)/2,(y-(VISUAL_H-1)/2)*ay+(VISUAL_H-1)/2,c):a.pixels[i];
      const z=crop?sample(b.pixels,(x-(VISUAL_W-1)/2)*bx+(VISUAL_W-1)/2,(y-(VISUAL_H-1)/2)*by+(VISUAL_H-1)/2,c):b.pixels[i];
      d+=Math.abs(v-z)/3;
      coarse[(Math.floor(y/2)*(VISUAL_W/2)+Math.floor(x/2))*3+c]+=(v-z)/4;
    }
    total+=d;if(d>18)bad++;
    tiles[Math.floor(y/10)*4+Math.floor(x/16)]+=d;
    if(total>6*VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H || bad>0.075*VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H)return false;
  }
  if(total/(VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H)>6 || !tiles.every(v=>v/160<=14))return false;
  let residual=0,large=0;
  const local=new Float32Array(16);
  for(let y=0;y<VISUAL_H/2;y++)for(let x=0;x<VISUAL_W/2;x++){
    const i=(y*(VISUAL_W/2)+x)*3;
    const d=(Math.abs(coarse[i])+Math.abs(coarse[i+1])+Math.abs(coarse[i+2]))/3;
    residual+=d;if(d>15)large++;
    local[Math.floor(y/5)*4+Math.floor(x/8)]+=d;
  }
  return residual/(VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H/4)<=4.2 && large<=0.055*VISUAL_W*VISUAL_H/4 && local.every(v=>v/40<=9);
}

function visualRenditionMatch(a,b) {
  if(a.page!==b.page || a.host!==b.host || a.scope!==b.scope || a.provider!==b.provider || a.query!==b.query || a.url===b.url)return false;
  const ratio=Math.max(a.w*a.h,b.w*b.h)/Math.min(a.w*a.h,b.w*b.h);
  const aspect=Math.max(a.w/a.h,b.w/b.h)/Math.min(a.w/a.h,b.w/b.h);
  if(aspect>1.28)return false;
  if(ratio<1.20){
    // Equal-size independent URLs require exact decoded sample equality.
    return a.w===b.w && a.h===b.h && a.pixels.every((v,i)=>v===b.pixels[i]);
  }
  if(aspect>1.035 && /(?:^|&)(?:crop|rect|fit|fp-x|fp-y)=/i.test(a.query))return false;
  return visualPixelsMatch(a,b,false) || (aspect>1.035 && (visualPixelsMatch(a,b,1) || visualPixelsMatch(a,b,2)));
}

function refreshVisualGroup(group) {
  const eligible=group.filter(e=>visualEligible(e.item));
  const earliest=eligible.reduce((v,e)=>!v || compareMediaOrder(e.item,v.item)<0?e:v,null);
  const winner=eligible.reduce((v,e)=>!v || e.w*e.h>v.w*v.h || (e.w*e.h===v.w*v.h && compareMediaOrder(e.item,v.item)<0)?e:v,null);
  for(const e of group){
    const it=e.item,duplicate=!!(winner && e!==winner && eligible.includes(e));
    const order=winner===e && earliest && earliest!==e?earliest.item:null;
    if(!!it.__hashDup!==duplicate || (it.__visualOrder || null)!==order){
      it.__hashDup=duplicate;it.__visualOrder=order;
      state.items.orderDirty=true;
      if(duplicate && it.card)it.card.classList.add('is-hidden');
      invalidateVisibilityStats('visual-rendition');
      scheduleApplyFilterSort('visual-rendition');
    }
  }
}

function forgetVisualFingerprint(item) {
  const group=item.__visualGroup;
  if(!group)return;
  const i=group.findIndex(e=>e.item===item);
