  if(i>=0){group.splice(i,1);state.items.visualCount--;}
  item.__visualGroup=null;item.__hashDup=false;item.__visualOrder=null;
  if(!group.length){
    const j=state.items.visualGroups.indexOf(group);
    if(j>=0)state.items.visualGroups.splice(j,1);
  }else refreshVisualGroup(group);
  state.items.orderDirty=true;
  invalidateVisibilityStats('visual-source-change');
  scheduleApplyFilterSort('visual-source-change');
}

function registerVisualFingerprint(item,img) {
  if(!visualEligible(item) || item.__visualGroup || state.items.visualCount>=VISUAL_MAX)return;
  const entry=visualSample(item,img);
  if(!entry)return;
  let group=null,compared=0;
  for(const g of state.items.visualGroups){
    if(g.length>=8 || g[0].page!==entry.page || g[0].host!==entry.host)continue;
    if(++compared>64)break;
    if(g.every(e=>visualRenditionMatch(e,entry))){group=g;break;}
  }
  if(!group){group=[];state.items.visualGroups.push(group);}
  group.push(entry);item.__visualGroup=group;state.items.visualCount++;
  if(group.length>1)refreshVisualGroup(group);
}

function applyThumbMetrics(item, img, card) {
  try {
    perfMeasureSourceEl(item);
    const w = img.naturalWidth || 0;
    const h = img.naturalHeight || 0;
    if (w && h) {
      if (!item.width || !item.height) {
        item.width = w;
        item.height = h;
      }
      const prevAutoHidden = !!item.__autoHidden;
      const prevAutoHiddenReason = item.__autoHiddenReason || '';
      if (item.kind === 'img' && (item.__sourceEl || item.__thumbSourceFacts)) {
        const thumbMeta = { width: w, height: h, pageUrl:item.pageUrl, mediaBase:item.mediaBase };
        const sourceFacts = item.__thumbSourceFacts || getThumbSourceFacts(item.__sourceEl, thumbMeta);
        item.__thumbScore = scoreThumbnailLike(item.__sourceEl, thumbMeta, item.openUrl || item.url, sourceFacts);
        const autoHiddenReason = getAutoHiddenReason(item.__sourceEl, thumbMeta, item.openUrl || item.url, item.__thumbScore, sourceFacts);
        if ((item.dupCount || 1) <= 1) {
          item.__autoHidden = !!autoHiddenReason;
          item.__autoHiddenReason = autoHiddenReason;
        } else if (item.__autoHidden) {
          item.__autoHidden = !!autoHiddenReason;
          item.__autoHiddenReason = item.__autoHidden ? pickAutoHiddenReason(prevAutoHiddenReason, autoHiddenReason) : '';
        }
      }
      item.score = scoreItem(item);
      const area = w * h;
      const aspect = Math.max(w / h, h / w);
      const smallThumb = (state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxW > 0 && state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxH > 0 &&
        w <= state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxW && h <= state.cfg.thumbSmallMaxH);
      let noiseReason = '';
      if (smallThumb) noiseReason = 'smallPx';
      else if (w < state.cfg.noiseMinW || h < state.cfg.noiseMinH) noiseReason = 'smallDim';
      else if (area < state.cfg.noiseMinArea) noiseReason = 'smallArea';
      else if (aspect > state.cfg.noiseMaxAspect) noiseReason = 'extremeAspect';
      const isNoise = smallThumb || (w < state.cfg.noiseMinW) ||
        (h < state.cfg.noiseMinH) ||
        (area < state.cfg.noiseMinArea) ||
        (aspect > state.cfg.noiseMaxAspect);
      const wasNoise = !!item.__isNoise;
      if (isNoise !== wasNoise) {
        item.__isNoise = isNoise;
        state.items.noiseCount = Math.max(0, state.items.noiseCount + (isNoise ? 1 : -1));
        invalidateVisibilityStats('thumb-noise');
        scheduleApplyFilterSort('thumb-noise');
      }
      item.__noiseReason = isNoise ? (noiseReason || 'noise') : '';
      if (card) {
        const dim = card.querySelector('.meta div');
        if (dim) dim.textContent = formatItemMetaText(item);
      }
      if (
        prevAutoHidden !== !!item.__autoHidden ||
        prevAutoHiddenReason !== (item.__autoHiddenReason || '')
      ) {
        if (card) updateCard(item);
        scheduleApplyFilterSort('autoHidden');
      }
      if (isNoise && state.cfg.noiseHide && card) {
        card.classList.add('is-hidden');
      }
      if (state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
        if (state.ui) state.ui.__infoDirty = true;
      } else {
        updateInfo();
      }

      releaseSourceEl(item, 'thumb-metrics');
      // スクロール中はrect計測を避け、後で分割適用
      if (state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
        if (img && img.dataset) {
          if (img.dataset.fmpFitQueued === '1') return;
          img.dataset.fmpFitQueued = '1';
        }
        thumbQueueUi(function() {
          try {
            if (img && img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpFitQueued = '0';
          } catch (e) {}
          thumbApplyFit(img);
        });
        return;
      }

      thumbApplyFit(img);
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function isRectInGridView(rect, gridRect) {
  if (!rect || !gridRect) return false;
  return rect.bottom >= gridRect.top && rect.top <= gridRect.bottom;
}

function isCardInGridView(card, gridRect) {
  if (!card || typeof card.getBoundingClientRect !== 'function') return false;
  let cardRect = null;
  try {
    cardRect = card.getBoundingClientRect();
  } catch (e) {
    cardRect = null;
  }
  if (!cardRect) return false;
  let g = gridRect || null;
  if (!g && state.ui && state.ui.grid && typeof state.ui.grid.getBoundingClientRect === 'function') {
    try {
      g = state.ui.grid.getBoundingClientRect();
    } catch (e) {
      g = null;
    }
  }
  if (!g) return false;
  return isRectInGridView(cardRect, g);
}

function isEntryInGridView(entry, gridRect) {
  if (!entry || !entry.boundingClientRect || !gridRect) return false;
  return isRectInGridView(entry.boundingClientRect, gridRect);
}

function resolveIoGridRect(entry) {
  if (entry && entry.rootBounds) {
    if (state.thumb) state.thumb.lastIoRootRect = entry.rootBounds;
    return entry.rootBounds;
  }
  if (state.thumb && state.thumb.lastIoRootRect) return state.thumb.lastIoRootRect;
  return null;
}

function revealThumb(card, img) {
  // スクロール中はUI反映を遅延（ただし表示中カードは即時反映）
  let scrolling = false;
  let inViewNow = false;
  if (state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
    scrolling = true;
    let resolvedFromIO = false;
    if (state.thumb.observer && card && card.dataset && card.dataset.id) {
      const item = state.items && state.items.byId ? state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id) : null;
      if (item) {
        inViewNow = !!item.__thumbInView;
        resolvedFromIO = true;
      }
    }
    if (resolvedFromIO && !inViewNow) {
      try {
        const grid = state.ui ? state.ui.grid : null;
        if (grid && typeof grid.getBoundingClientRect === 'function') {
          const gridRect = grid.getBoundingClientRect();
          if (isCardInGridView(card, gridRect)) {
            inViewNow = true;
            const item = state.items && state.items.byId ? state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id) : null;
            if (item) item.__thumbInView = 1;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (!resolvedFromIO) {
      let gridRect = null;
      if (state.ui && state.ui.grid && typeof state.ui.grid.getBoundingClientRect === 'function') {
        try {
          gridRect = state.ui.grid.getBoundingClientRect();
        } catch (e) {
          gridRect = null;
        }
      }
      inViewNow = isCardInGridView(card, gridRect);
    }
  }

  if (scrolling && !inViewNow) {
    if (img && img.dataset) {
      if (img.dataset.fmpRevealQueued === '1') return;
      img.dataset.fmpRevealQueued = '1';
    }
    thumbQueueUi(function() {
      try {
        if (img && img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpRevealQueued = '0';
      } catch (e) {}
      if (img && img.isConnected) img.style.opacity = '1';
      if (card && card.isConnected) hideThumbOverlay(card);

      if (img && img.isConnected) {
        queueRepaint(img);
        if (state.env && state.env.isIOS && state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
          const nudged = img.dataset ? img.dataset.fmpNudged : img.getAttribute('data-fmp-nudged');
          if (nudged !== '1') {
            if (img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpNudged = '1';
            else img.setAttribute('data-fmp-nudged', '1');
            setTimeout(function() {
              queueRepaint(img);
            }, 30);
          }
        }
      }
    });
    return;
  }

  // 通常（非スクロール中）または表示中カードは即反映
  if (img) img.style.opacity = '1';
  hideThumbOverlay(card);
  if (img) {
    queueRepaint(img);
    if (state.env && state.env.isIOS && state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
      const nudged = img.dataset ? img.dataset.fmpNudged : img.getAttribute('data-fmp-nudged');
      if (nudged !== '1') {
        if (img.dataset) img.dataset.fmpNudged = '1';
        else img.setAttribute('data-fmp-nudged', '1');
        setTimeout(function() {
          queueRepaint(img);
        }, 30);
      }
    }
  }
}

function getThumbMaxConcurrent() {
  if (state.env.isIPhone) {
    let max = state.cfg.thumbConcurrentIphone || 3;
    const count = state.items && state.items.list ? state.items.list.length : 0;
    if (count >= 320) max = Math.min(max, 1);
    else if (count >= 180) max = Math.min(max, 2);
    return max;
  }
  if (state.env.isIPad) return state.cfg.thumbConcurrentIpad || 4;
  return Math.max(state.cfg.thumbConcurrentIpad || 4, 6);
}

function getThumbMaxConcurrentWhileScroll() {
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPhone) return 3;
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPad) return 3;
  return 4;
}

function shouldSkipThumbDecode() {
  const count = state.items && state.items.list ? state.items.list.length : 0;
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPhone) return count >= 240;
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPad) return count >= 360;
  return false;
}

function getAdaptiveThumbRootMarginPx() {
  const base = state.cfg.thumbRootMarginPx || 0;
  const count = state.items && state.items.list ? state.items.list.length : 0;
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPhone) {
    if (count >= 320) return 420;
    if (count >= 180) return 450;
    return base;
  }
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPad) {
    if (count >= 320) return 520;
    return base;
  }
  return base;
}

function getThumbIoRootMarginPx() {
  const base = state.cfg.thumbIoRootMarginPx || 0;
  const adaptive = getAdaptiveThumbRootMarginPx();
  if (!base) return 0;
  if (!adaptive) return base;
  return Math.min(base, adaptive);
}

function initThumbLoader() {
  if (!state.thumb || !state.ui.grid) return;
  if (state.thumb.__inited) return;
  state.thumb.__inited = true;
  state.thumb.max = getThumbMaxConcurrent();
  state.thumb.pausedByScroll = false;
  state.thumb.scrollPauseUntil = 0;

  // UI遅延キュー（スクロール中のガクつき対策）
  if (!state.thumb.uiQueue) state.thumb.uiQueue = [];
  if (typeof state.thumb.uiQueueHead !== 'number') state.thumb.uiQueueHead = 0;
  state.thumb.uiFlushScheduled = false;

  const root = state.ui.grid;
  let scheduled = false;
  const scan = function() {
    scheduled = false;
    const grid = state.ui.grid;
    if (!grid) return;
    const rect = grid.getBoundingClientRect();
    const margin = getAdaptiveThumbRootMarginPx();
    const top = rect.top - margin;
    const bottom = rect.bottom + margin;
    for (const card of grid.querySelectorAll('.card')) {
      const item = state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id);
      if (!item || item.__failed || item.__thumbLoaded || item.__thumbLoading ||
          (item.kind !== 'img' && !item.__thumbPreview)) continue;
      const r = card.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.bottom >= top && r.top <= bottom) thumbEnqueue(item);
    }
  };
  const schedule = function() {
    if (scheduled) return;
    scheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(scan);
  };
  let kickScheduled = false;
  const kickScanNow = function() {
    if (kickScheduled) return;
    kickScheduled = true;
    requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      kickScheduled = false;
      const obs = state.thumb ? state.thumb.observer : null;
      if (!obs) return;
      let entries = [];
      try {
        entries = obs.takeRecords ? obs.takeRecords() : [];
      } catch (e) {
        entries = [];
      }
      if (!entries || !entries.length) return;
      entries.forEach(function(entry) {
        if (!entry) return;
        const card = entry.target;
        if (!card || !card.dataset) return;
        const item = state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id);
        if (!item) return;
        if (!entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio <= 0) {
          item.__thumbInView = 0;
          return;
        }
        const gridRect = resolveIoGridRect(entry);
        const inView = gridRect ? isEntryInGridView(entry, gridRect) : !!entry.isIntersecting;
        item.__thumbInView = inView ? 1 : 0;
        thumbEnqueue(item, inView);
      });
    });
  };
  state.thumb.scanNow = schedule;

  if (typeof IntersectionObserver === 'function') {
    try {
      const ioMargin = getThumbIoRootMarginPx();
      const ioRootMargin = ioMargin ? (ioMargin + 'px 0px ' + ioMargin + 'px 0px') : '0px 0px';
      state.thumb.observer = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
        entries.forEach(function(entry) {
          if (!entry) return;
          const card = entry.target;
          if (!card || !card.dataset) return;
          const item = state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id);
          if (!item) return;
          if (!entry.isIntersecting && entry.intersectionRatio <= 0) {
            item.__thumbInView = 0;
            return;
          }
          const gridRect = resolveIoGridRect(entry);
          const inView = gridRect ? isEntryInGridView(entry, gridRect) : !!entry.isIntersecting;
          item.__thumbInView = inView ? 1 : 0;
          thumbEnqueue(item, inView);
        });
      }, {
        root: root,
        rootMargin: ioRootMargin,
        threshold: 0
      });
      Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.card')).forEach(function(card) {
        try { state.thumb.observer.observe(card); } catch (e) {}
      });
    } catch (e) {
      state.thumb.observer = null;
    }
  }
  if (state.thumb.observer) {
    state.thumb.scanNow = kickScanNow;
  }

  const resumeFromScroll = function() {
    if (!state.thumb) return;
    state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer = 0;
    const now = Date.now();
    const until = state.thumb.scrollPauseUntil || 0;
    if (until > now) {
      state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer = setTimeout(resumeFromScroll, until - now);
      return;
    }
    state.thumb.pausedByScroll = false;
    if (state.ui && state.ui.__infoDirty) {
      state.ui.__infoDirty = false;
      updateInfo();
    }

    // スクロール中に溜めたUI反映を分割で実行
    try { thumbFlushUiQueue(); } catch (e) {}

    // ロード再開
    thumbPump();
  };

  const markScrollPause = function() {
    if (!state.thumb) return;
    if (!state.thumb.pausedByScroll) state.thumb.scrollPriorityRescueUsed = false;
    state.thumb.pausedByScroll = true;
    state.thumb.scrollPauseUntil = Date.now() + (state.cfg.thumbScrollPauseMs || 0);
    if (state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer);
      state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer = 0;
    }
    state.thumb.scrollResumeTimer = setTimeout(resumeFromScroll, state.cfg.thumbScrollResumeMs || 0);
  };

  const scheduleScrollEndScan = function() {
    if (!state.thumb) return;

    // IntersectionObserverがあるなら、スクロール停止ごとの総当たりスキャンはやらない（ガクつき対策）
    if (state.thumb.observer) return;

    if (state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer) {
      clearTimeout(state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer);
      state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer = 0;
    }
    state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      state.thumb.scrollEndScanTimer = 0;
      schedule();
    }, state.cfg.thumbScrollEndScanMs || 0);
  };

  const onScroll = function() {
    markScrollPause();
    scheduleScrollEndScan();
  };

  if (state.thumb.onScroll) {
    try { root.removeEventListener('scroll', state.thumb.onScroll); } catch (e) {}
  }
  state.thumb.onScroll = onScroll;
  root.addEventListener('scroll', state.thumb.onScroll, { passive: true });

  if (state.thumb.onResize) {
    try { window.removeEventListener('resize', state.thumb.onResize); } catch (e) {}
  }
  state.thumb.onResize = function() {
    resetLightboxZoomOnResize();
    if (state.thumb && typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function') state.thumb.scanNow();
  };
  window.addEventListener('resize', state.thumb.onResize);
  if (state.thumb.observer) {
    if (typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function') state.thumb.scanNow();
  } else {
    schedule();
  }
}

function observeThumbCard(card) {
  if (!card || !state.thumb) return;
  if (state.thumb.observer) {
    try { state.thumb.observer.observe(card); } catch (e) {}
  } else if (state.thumb && typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function') {
    state.thumb.scanNow();
  }
}

function thumbQueueLength() {
  if (!state.thumb) return 0;
  const pri = Math.max(0, (state.thumb.queuePri.length || 0) - (state.thumb.queuePriHead || 0));
  const normal = Math.max(0, (state.thumb.queue.length || 0) - (state.thumb.queueHead || 0));
  return pri + normal;
}

function thumbPriorityQueueLength() {
  if (!state.thumb) return 0;
  return Math.max(0, (state.thumb.queuePri.length || 0) - (state.thumb.queuePriHead || 0));
}

function compactThumbQueue(name) {
  if (!state.thumb) return;
  const queueKey = name === 'pri' ? 'queuePri' : 'queue';
  const headKey = name === 'pri' ? 'queuePriHead' : 'queueHead';
  const q = state.thumb[queueKey];
  const head = state.thumb[headKey] || 0;
  if (!q || head >= q.length) {
    if (q) q.length = 0;
    state.thumb[headKey] = 0;
    return;
  }
  if (head > 64 && head * 2 >= q.length) {
    state.thumb[queueKey] = q.slice(head);
    state.thumb[headKey] = 0;
  }
}

function pushThumbQueue(item, priority) {
  if (!state.thumb || !item) return;
  if (priority) {
    state.thumb.queuePri.push(item);
    item.__thumbQueuedPri = 1;
    perfCount('thumb.enqueue.priority');
  } else {
    state.thumb.queue.push(item);
    item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
    perfCount('thumb.enqueue.normal');
  }
  perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
}

function popThumbQueue() {
  if (!state.thumb) return null;
  let item = null;
  while (state.thumb.queuePriHead < state.thumb.queuePri.length) {
    item = state.thumb.queuePri[state.thumb.queuePriHead++];
    if (item) break;
    item = null;
  }
  compactThumbQueue('pri');
  if (item) {
    perfCount('thumb.pop.priority');
    perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
    return item;
  }
  while (state.thumb.queueHead < state.thumb.queue.length) {
    item = state.thumb.queue[state.thumb.queueHead++];
    if (item) break;
    item = null;
  }
  compactThumbQueue('normal');
  if (item) perfCount('thumb.pop.normal');
  perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
  return item;
}

function popPriorityThumbQueue() {
  if (!state.thumb) return null;
  let item = null;
  while (state.thumb.queuePriHead < state.thumb.queuePri.length) {
    item = state.thumb.queuePri[state.thumb.queuePriHead++];
    if (item) break;
    item = null;
  }
  compactThumbQueue('pri');
  if (item) perfCount('thumb.pop.priority');
  perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
  return item;
}

function promoteQueuedThumbItem(item) {
  if (!state.thumb || !item) return;
  if (item.__thumbQueuedPri === 1) return;
  const q = state.thumb.queue;
  const pos = q.indexOf(item, state.thumb.queueHead || 0);
  if (pos >= 0) q.splice(pos, 1);
  perfCount('thumb.promote');
  pushThumbQueue(item, true);
}

function requeueThumbItem(item) {
  if (!state.thumb || !item) return;
  perfCount(item.__thumbQueuedPri === 1 ? 'thumb.requeue.priority' : 'thumb.requeue.normal');
  pushThumbQueue(item, item.__thumbQueuedPri === 1);
}

function thumbSchedulePump() {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.thumb) return;
  if (state.thumb.pumpScheduled) return;
  state.thumb.pumpScheduled = true;
  state.thumb.pumpRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    state.thumb.pumpRaf = 0;
    state.thumb.pumpScheduled = false;
    thumbPump();
  });
}

function thumbEnqueue(item, priority) {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !item) return;
  if (item.__failed || item.__thumbLoaded || item.__thumbLoading || isSourceSuppressed(item) || item.__hashDup) return;
  const canLoad = (item.kind === 'img') || !!item.__thumbPreview;
  if (!canLoad) return;
  if (!item.card) return;
  let img = item.__thumbImg;
  if (!img || !img.isConnected) {
    img = item.card.querySelector('img[data-src]');
    if (img) item.__thumbImg = img;
  }
  if (!img) return;
  if (!state.thumb) return;
  const pri = !!priority;
  if (state.thumb.queued.has(item.id)) {
    if (pri) promoteQueuedThumbItem(item);
    thumbSchedulePump();
    return;
  }
  state.thumb.queued.add(item.id);
  pushThumbQueue(item, pri);
  if (item.__thumbInView) {
    perfCount('thumb.visible.enqueue');
  }
  markThumbProgress();
  thumbSchedulePump();
}

function thumbPump() {
  if (state.ui.disposed || !state.thumb || state.thumb.paused) return;
  const prevMax = state.thumb.max || 0;
  const baseMax = getThumbMaxConcurrent() || 0;
  let max = baseMax;
  if (state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
    const scrollMax = getThumbMaxConcurrentWhileScroll();
    max = scrollMax || max;
  }
  state.thumb.max = max;
  if (state.env && state.env.isIPhone) {
    const base = state.cfg.thumbConcurrentIphone || 3;
    if (max && max < base && max !== prevMax) {
      if (state.log && state.log.push) {
        state.log.push('thumb', 'throttle max=' + max + ' items=' + state.items.list.length);
      }
    }
  }
  if (!max) return;

  if (!state.thumb.pausedByScroll) {
    while (state.thumb.inFlight < max && thumbQueueLength()) {
      const item = popThumbQueue();
      if (!item) continue;
      state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
      item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
      if (item.__failed || item.__thumbLoaded || item.__thumbLoading) continue;
      startThumbLoad(item);
    }
    return;
  }

  const hasIO = !!state.thumb.observer;
  let gridRect = null;
  if (!hasIO) {
    const grid = state.ui ? state.ui.grid : null;
    if (!grid || typeof grid.getBoundingClientRect !== 'function') return;
    gridRect = grid.getBoundingClientRect();
  }
  const probeLimit = 36;
  if (hasIO && state.cfg.thumbScrollPriorityOnly) {
    let requeued = 0;
    const requeueLimit = Math.max(6, Math.min(probeLimit, (max || 1) * 6));
    for (let i = 0; i < probeLimit && state.thumb.inFlight < max && thumbPriorityQueueLength(); i += 1) {
      const item = popPriorityThumbQueue();
      if (!item) continue;
      if (item.__failed || item.__thumbLoaded || item.__thumbLoading) {
        state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
        item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
        continue;
      }
      if (!item.card) {
        state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
        item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
        continue;
      }
      if (!item.__thumbInView) {
        requeueThumbItem(item);
        requeued += 1;
        if (requeued >= requeueLimit) break;
        continue;
      }
      state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
      item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
      startThumbLoad(item);
    }
    if (
      state.thumb.inFlight < max &&
      !thumbPriorityQueueLength() &&
      thumbQueueLength() &&
      !state.thumb.scrollPriorityRescueUsed &&
      typeof state.thumb.scanNow === 'function'
    ) {
      state.thumb.scrollPriorityRescueUsed = true;
      perfCount('thumb.scrollPriorityRescue');
      state.thumb.scanNow();
    }
    return;
  }
  let requeued = 0;
  const requeueLimit = Math.max(6, Math.min(probeLimit, (max || 1) * 6));
  for (let i = 0; i < probeLimit && state.thumb.inFlight < max && thumbQueueLength(); i++) {
    const item = popThumbQueue();
    if (!item) continue;
    if (item.__failed || item.__thumbLoaded || item.__thumbLoading) {
      state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
      item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
      continue;
    }
    if (!item.card) {
      state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
      item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
      continue;
    }
    if (hasIO) {
      if (!item.__thumbInView) {
        requeueThumbItem(item);
        requeued += 1;
        if (requeued >= requeueLimit) break;
        continue;
      }
    } else if (!isCardInGridView(item.card, gridRect)) {
      requeueThumbItem(item);
      requeued += 1;
      if (requeued >= requeueLimit) break;
      continue;
    }
    state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
    item.__thumbQueuedPri = 0;
    startThumbLoad(item);
  }
}

function addRetryBuster(url, n) {
  const retry = Number(n) || 0;
  if (!url || retry < 1) return url;
  try {
    const u = new URL(url, location.href);
    if (!(u.protocol === 'http:' || u.protocol === 'https:')) return url;
    u.searchParams.set('__ivb_retry', String(retry));
    return u.toString();
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    const raw = String(url);
    const hashPos = raw.indexOf('#');
    const hash = hashPos >= 0 ? raw.slice(hashPos) : '';
    const base = hashPos >= 0 ? raw.slice(0, hashPos) : raw;
    const sep = base.indexOf('?') >= 0 ? '&' : '?';
    return base + sep + '__ivb_retry=' + encodeURIComponent(String(retry)) + hash;
  } catch (e) {}
  return url;
}

function resolveLoadedSrc(raw) {
  if (!raw) return '';
  try {
    return new URL(String(raw), location.href).toString();
  } catch (e) {
    return String(raw);
  }
}

function isSameLoadedSrc(currentSrc, targetSrc) {
  if (!currentSrc || !targetSrc) return false;
  return resolveLoadedSrc(currentSrc) === resolveLoadedSrc(targetSrc);
}

function getThumbCandidateUrls(item, src) {
  const out = [];
  const seen = new Set();
  const add = function(u) {
    const s = String(u || '').trim();
    if (!s || seen.has(s) || (item && item.kind==='img' && isExcludedLiveImage(s))) return;
    seen.add(s);
    out.push(s);
  };
  add(src);
  if (item && item.kind === 'img') {
    add(item.fallbackRequestUrl);
    if (item.pictureFallbackUrls) item.pictureFallbackUrls.forEach(add);
    if (item.imgurFallbackUrls) item.imgurFallbackUrls.forEach(add);
  }
  if (item && Array.isArray(item.__thumbCandidates)) {
    item.__thumbCandidates.forEach(add);
  }
  return out;
}

function startThumbLoad(item) {
  if (!item || !item.card || isSourceSuppressed(item) || item.__hashDup) return;
  const card = item.card;
  let img = item.__thumbImg;
  if (!img || !img.isConnected) {
    img = card.querySelector('img');
    if (img) item.__thumbImg = img;
  }
  if (!img) return;
  const previewOnly = item.kind !== 'img';
  const src = img.dataset && img.dataset.src ? img.dataset.src : getDisplayUrl(item);
  if (!src) return;
  const candidateUrls = getThumbCandidateUrls(item, src);
  if (!candidateUrls.length) return;
  const requestAtStart = item.requestUrl;
  const token = (item.__thumbToken = (item.__thumbToken || 0) + 1);
  const ctx = { token: token, aborted: false, abort: null };
  item.__thumbCtx = ctx;
  item.__thumbLoading = true;
  state.thumb.inFlight += 1;
  perfCount('thumb.load.start');
  perfMarkOnce('thumb.firstStart');
  perfGauge('thumb.inFlight', state.thumb.inFlight);
  perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
  if (item.__thumbInView) {
    perfCount('thumb.visible.start');
    perfMarkOnce('thumb.firstVisibleStart');
  }
  markThumbProgress();
  const getOverlayDelay = function() {
    const shownSrc = (img.currentSrc || img.src || '');
    const visible = img.style.opacity !== '0' && shownSrc && !isSameLoadedSrc(shownSrc, BLANK_GIF);
    return visible ? 300 : 180;
  };
  const shownSrc = (img.currentSrc || img.src || '');
  const keepVisibleOnReload = !!(
    item.__thumbLoaded &&
    isSameLoadedSrc(shownSrc, src) &&
    img.style.opacity !== '0' &&
    !isSameLoadedSrc(shownSrc, BLANK_GIF)
  );
  scheduleOverlayShow(item, card, getOverlayDelay());
  if (!keepVisibleOnReload) img.style.opacity = '0';

  let retries = item.__thumbRetry || 0;
  let candidateIndex = 0;
  let done = false;
  let timer = 0;

  const isCurrent = function() {
    return item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.token === token;
  };

  const finish = function() {
    if (done) return;
    const current = isCurrent();
    done = true;
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    if (current || !item.__thumbCtx) {
      item.__thumbLoading = false;
    }
    state.thumb.queued.delete(item.id);
    state.thumb.inFlight = Math.max(0, state.thumb.inFlight - 1);
    perfGauge('thumb.inFlight', state.thumb.inFlight);
    perfGauge('thumb.queue', thumbQueueLength());
    markThumbProgress();
    if (current && item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.token === token) {
      item.__thumbCtx = null;
    }
    thumbPump();
  };

  ctx.abort = function(reason) {
    if (done) return;
    perfCount('thumb.load.abort');
    ctx.aborted = true;
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    if (isCurrent()) {
      img.onload = null;
      img.onerror = null;
      hideThumbOverlay(card);
    }
    finish();
  };

  const finalizeSuccess = function() {
    if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
      finish();
      return;
    }
    const pendingRefresh = !!item.__thumbPendingRefresh;
    item.__thumbPendingRefresh = 0;
    item.__thumbLoaded = true;
    if (previewOnly) item.__previewSucceeded = true;
    perfCount('thumb.load.success');
    if (item.__thumbInView) perfCount('thumb.visible.loaded');
    releaseSourceEl(item, 'thumb-success');
    finish();
    if (pendingRefresh && item.card && !item.__failed) {
      updateCard(item);
    }
    if (item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.token === token) item.__thumbCtx = null;
  };

  const failFinal = function() {
    if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
      finish();
      return;
    }
    if (previewOnly) {
      clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
      item.__thumbLoaded = true;
      hideThumbOverlay(card);
      item.__previewSucceeded = false;
      const pendingRefresh = !!item.__thumbPendingRefresh;
      item.__thumbPendingRefresh = 0;
      perfCount('thumb.load.previewError');
      if (item.__thumbInView) perfCount('thumb.visible.error');
      releaseSourceEl(item, 'thumb-preview-error');
      finish();
      if (pendingRefresh && item.card) updateCard(item);
      if (item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.token === token) item.__thumbCtx = null;
      return;
    }
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    if (item.imgurFallbackUrls && item.requestUrl === requestAtStart) {
      const extra = item.imgurFallbackUrls.filter(u => !candidateUrls.includes(u) && !isExcludedLiveImage(u));
      if (extra.length) {
        candidateUrls.push(...extra.slice(0,3));
        if (scheduleNextCandidate()) return;
      }
    }
    state.items.errorCount += 1;
    perfCount('thumb.load.error');
    if (item.__thumbInView) perfCount('thumb.visible.error');
    item.__failed = true;
    forgetVisualFingerprint(item);
    invalidateVisibilityStats('thumb-fail');
    try {
      if (state.thumb && state.thumb.observer) {
        try { state.thumb.observer.unobserve(card); } catch (e) {}
      }
      if (card.parentNode) card.parentNode.removeChild(card);
    } catch (e) {}
    item.card = null;
    applyErrorVis();
    releaseSourceEl(item, 'thumb-error');
    finish();
    if (item.__thumbCtx && item.__thumbCtx.token === token) item.__thumbCtx = null;
  };

  const scheduleRetry = function() {
    if (done) return;
    if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
      finish();
      return;
    }
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    retries += 1;
    item.__thumbRetry = retries;
    img.onload = null;
    img.onerror = null;
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (done) return;
      attempt();
    }, state.cfg.thumbRetryDelayMs || 0);
  };

  const scheduleNextCandidate = function() {
    if (done) return true;
    if ((candidateIndex + 1) >= candidateUrls.length) return false;
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    candidateIndex += 1;
    retries = 0;
    item.__thumbRetry = 0;
    img.onload = null;
    img.onerror = null;
    if (img.dataset) img.dataset.src = candidateUrls[candidateIndex];
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (done) return;
      attempt();
    }, state.cfg.thumbRetryDelayMs || 0);
    return true;
  };

  const attempt = function() {
    if (done) return;
    if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
      finish();
      return;
    }
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    item.__thumbStartedAt = Date.now();
    perfCount('thumb.attempt');
    const baseSrc = candidateUrls[candidateIndex] || src;
    const targetSrc = retries > 0 ? addRetryBuster(baseSrc, retries) : baseSrc;
    if (img.dataset) img.dataset.src = baseSrc;
    scheduleOverlayShow(item, card, getOverlayDelay());
    img.onload = function() {
      if (done) return;
      if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
        finish();
        return;
      }
      if (!isSameLoadedSrc((img.currentSrc || img.src), targetSrc)) return;
      if (previewOnly && /https:\/\/i\.ytimg\.com\/vi\/[^/]+\/(?:maxresdefault|sddefault|hqdefault|mqdefault)\.jpg/i.test(baseSrc) &&
          img.naturalWidth <= 120 && img.naturalHeight <= 90 && scheduleNextCandidate()) return;
      const show = function() {
        if (done) return;
        if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
          finish();
          return;
        }
        clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
        if (!previewOnly && item.requestUrl === requestAtStart) {
          if (candidateIndex > 0 || (candidateUrls[candidateIndex] && candidateUrls[candidateIndex] !== src)) {
            item.__resolvedRequestFor = requestAtStart;
            item.__resolvedRequestUrl = candidateUrls[candidateIndex];
            item.width = img.naturalWidth || 0;
            item.height = img.naturalHeight || 0;
          }
          applyThumbMetrics(item, img, card);
          registerVisualFingerprint(item,img);
        }
        revealThumb(card, img);
        perfCount('thumb.reveal');
        perfMarkOnce('thumb.firstReveal');
        if (item.__thumbInView) perfMarkOnce('thumb.firstVisibleReveal');
        finalizeSuccess();
      };
      const settleAfterReady = function() {
        if (done) return;
        if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
          finish();
          return;
        }
        if (!isSameLoadedSrc((img.currentSrc || img.src), targetSrc)) return;
        if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
        const nw = img.naturalWidth || 0;
        const nh = img.naturalHeight || 0;
        if (!nw || !nh) {
          markThumbProgress();
          if (state.log && state.log.push) state.log.push('thumb', 'dim0 retry');
          if (scheduleNextCandidate()) return;
          if (retries < (state.cfg.thumbRetryMax || 0)) {
            scheduleRetry();
          } else {
            failFinal();
          }
          return;
        }
        show();
      };
      if (shouldSkipThumbDecode() || (state.thumb && state.thumb.pausedByScroll)) {
        requestAnimationFrame(function() {
          settleAfterReady();
        });
      } else {
        decodeWithTimeout(img, state.cfg.decodeTimeoutMsThumb || 0).then(function() {
          settleAfterReady();
        });
      }
    };
    img.onerror = function() {
      if (done) return;
      if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
        finish();
        return;
      }
      markThumbProgress();
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      if (scheduleNextCandidate()) return;
      if (retries < (state.cfg.thumbRetryMax || 0)) {
        scheduleRetry();
      } else {
        failFinal();
      }
    };
    timer = setTimeout(function() {
      if (done) return;
      if (!item.__thumbCtx || item.__thumbCtx.token !== token) {
        finish();
        return;
      }
      markThumbProgress();
      if (scheduleNextCandidate()) return;
      if (retries < (state.cfg.thumbRetryMax || 0)) {
        scheduleRetry();
      } else {
        failFinal();
      }
    }, state.cfg.thumbTimeoutMs || 0);
    try { img.src = targetSrc; } catch (e) { img.src = targetSrc; }
  };

  attempt();
}

function getMediaProviderType(item) {
  if (!item) return '';
  let t = (item.embedType || '').toUpperCase();
  if (!t) {
    try {
      const host = new URL(item.requestUrl || item.url, location.href).hostname.toLowerCase();
      if (host === 'i.imgur.com') t = 'IMGUR';
      else if (host === 'pbs.twimg.com' || host === 'video.twimg.com') t = 'X';
      else if (host === 'i.ytimg.com') t = 'YT';
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return t;
}

function isImgurFilterItem(item) {
  return getMediaProviderType(item) === 'IMGUR';
}

function getMediaBadgeInfo(item) {
  if (!item) return null;
  let t = getMediaProviderType(item);
  if (!t && item.kind === 'video') t = 'VIDEO';
  if (!t && item.kind === 'embed') t = 'OTHER';
  const names = {VIDEO:'動画',YT:'YouTube',IMGUR:'Imgur',X:'X',TT:'TikTok',INSTA:'Instagram',VIMEO:'Vimeo',DM:'Dailymotion',TWITCH:'Twitch',NICO:'ニコニコ動画',BILI:'Bilibili',FB:'Facebook',OTHER:'埋込'};
  if (!t) return null;
  return {text:(names[t] || t) + (item.kind === 'video' && t !== 'VIDEO' ? ' ▶' : ''),full:t};
}

function setMediaCardType(card, item) {
  const badge = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
  if (badge) setAttrIfChanged(card, 'data-media-type', badge.full);
  else if (card.hasAttribute('data-media-type')) card.removeAttribute('data-media-type');
}

function renderEmbedPlaceholder(ph, item) {
  if (!ph) return;
  const useClassStyles = !!(state.cfg.dmThemeTokensEnabled && state.cfg.dmClassStylesEnabled);
  ph.textContent = '';
  while (ph.firstChild) ph.removeChild(ph.firstChild);
  ph.classList.remove('dm-embed-preview');
  if (!useClassStyles) {
    ph.style.flexDirection = '';
    ph.style.gap = '';
    ph.style.padding = '';
    ph.style.textAlign = '';
  }
  ph.title = 'TAP FOR LB ' + item.embedType;
  const previewLines = getEmbedPreviewLines(item);
  if (previewLines && previewLines.length) {
    if (useClassStyles) {
      ph.classList.add('dm-embed-preview');
    } else {
      ph.style.flexDirection = 'column';
      ph.style.gap = '4px';
      ph.style.padding = '10px 8px';
      ph.style.textAlign = 'center';
    }
    previewLines.forEach(function(line, idx) {
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.textContent = line;
      if (useClassStyles) {
        row.className = 'dm-preview-row ' + (idx === 0 ? 'dm-preview-primary' : 'dm-preview-secondary');
        if (idx === 0) row.style.color = '#d0d0d0';
      } else {
        row.style.maxWidth = '100%';
        row.style.overflowWrap = 'anywhere';
        row.style.lineHeight = '1.3';
        row.style.color = idx === 0 ? '#d0d0d0' : '#9a9a9a';
        row.style.fontSize = idx === 0 ? '12px' : '10px';
        row.style.fontWeight = idx === 0 ? '700' : '500';
      }
      ph.appendChild(row);
    });
    return;
  }
  const badge = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
  ph.textContent = badge ? badge.text + ' ・ タップして開く' : 'タップして開く';
}

function createCard(item) {
  const card = document.createElement('div');
  card.className = 'card';
  card.dataset.id = item.id;
  card.dataset.kind = item.kind || '';
  setMediaCardType(card, item);
  card.dataset.oh = item.__allHidden ? '1' : '0';
  card.dataset.ah = item.__autoHidden ? '1' : '0';
  if (item.embedType) {
    card.dataset.etype = ((item.embedType || '').toUpperCase()) || 'OTHER';
  }
  const th = (state.env && state.env.isWide) ? 180 : 140;
  card.style.minHeight = (th + 40) + 'px';

  const thumb = document.createElement('button');
  thumb.className = 'thumb';
  if (item.kind === 'video' || item.kind === 'embed') thumb.classList.add('is-playable');
  const hlabel = document.createElement('div');
  hlabel.className = 'hlabel';
  hlabel.textContent = '非表示';
  thumb.appendChild(hlabel);

  item.__thumbPreview = false;
  let needsThumbOverlay = false;
  const useClassStyles = !!(state.cfg.dmThemeTokensEnabled && state.cfg.dmClassStylesEnabled);
  const appendThumbImage = function(src, overlayMode) {
    if (!src) return null;
    const img = document.createElement('img');
    img.decoding = 'async';
    img.src = BLANK_GIF;
    img.dataset.src = src;
    img.alt = '';
    img.style.width = '100%';
    img.style.height = th + 'px';
    if (!useClassStyles) {
      img.style.display = 'block';
      img.style.objectFit = 'contain';
      img.style.objectPosition = 'center';
      img.style.background = '#000';
    }
    img.style.opacity = '0';
    if (overlayMode) {
      img.style.position = 'absolute';
      img.style.left = '0';
      img.style.top = '0';
    }
    thumb.appendChild(img);
    item.__thumbImg = img;
    needsThumbOverlay = true;
    return img;
  };

  if (item.kind === 'img') {
    const displayUrl = getDisplayUrl(item);
    appendThumbImage(displayUrl, false);
    const badgeInfo = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
    if (badgeInfo) {
      const badge = document.createElement('div');
      badge.className = 'badge';
      badge.textContent = badgeInfo.text;
      badge.title = badgeInfo.full;
      badge.setAttribute('aria-label', badgeInfo.full);
      thumb.appendChild(badge);
    }
  } else if (item.kind === 'video') {
    const ph = document.createElement('div');
    ph.className = 'ph';
    ph.textContent = '動画 ・ タップして再生';
    ph.style.height = th + 'px';
    ph.style.width = '100%';
    thumb.appendChild(ph);
    const badge = document.createElement('div');
    badge.className = 'badge';
    const badgeInfo = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
    badge.textContent = badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.text : '▶';
    badge.title = badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.full : 'VIDEO';
    badge.setAttribute('aria-label', badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.full : 'VIDEO');
    thumb.appendChild(badge);
    const urls = getCardPreviewUrls(item);
    item.__thumbCandidates = urls;
    item.__previewKey = urls.join('\n');
    if (urls.length) {
      appendThumbImage(urls[0], true);
      item.__thumbPreview = true;
    }
  } else if (item.kind === 'embed') {
    const ph = document.createElement('div');
    ph.className = 'ph';
    ph.style.height = th + 'px';
    ph.style.width = '100%';
    renderEmbedPlaceholder(ph, item);
    thumb.appendChild(ph);
    const badge = document.createElement('div');
    badge.className = 'badge';
    const badgeInfo = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
    badge.textContent = badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.text : '?';
    badge.title = badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.full : (((item.embedType || '').toUpperCase()) || 'OTHER');
    badge.setAttribute('aria-label', badgeInfo ? badgeInfo.full : (((item.embedType || '').toUpperCase()) || 'OTHER'));
    thumb.appendChild(badge);
    const embedThumbUrls = getCardPreviewUrls(item);
    item.__thumbCandidates = embedThumbUrls.slice();
    item.__previewKey = embedThumbUrls.join('\n');
    if (embedThumbUrls.length) {
      appendThumbImage(embedThumbUrls[0], true);
      item.__thumbPreview = true;
    }
  }

  const meta = document.createElement('div');
  meta.className = 'meta';
  const page = document.createElement('span');
  page.className = 'chip-page';
  page.textContent = item.pageLabel || 'P1';
  const dim = document.createElement('div');
  dim.textContent = formatItemMetaText(item);
  if (item.kind === 'embed') {
    const inlineBtn = document.createElement('button');
    inlineBtn.className = 'inline';
    inlineBtn.textContent = 'IN';
    inlineBtn.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      buildEmbedInCard(card, item);
      boostCrawl('embed');
    });
    meta.appendChild(inlineBtn);
  }
  const open = document.createElement('a');
  open.className = 'open';
  open.textContent = '↗';
  open.href = allowOpen(item.openUrl) ? item.openUrl : item.url;
  open.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopPropagation();
    openExternal(open.href, 'card');
  });
  meta.appendChild(page);
  meta.appendChild(dim);
  meta.appendChild(open);

  let thumbLastTouchLikeTs = 0;
  let thumbIgnoreClickUntil = 0;
  let thumbTouchActive = false;
  let thumbTouchId = -1;
  let thumbTouchMoved = false;
  let thumbStartX = 0;
  let thumbStartY = 0;
  let thumbStartGridScrollTop = 0;
  let thumbStartWindowScrollY = 0;
  const thumbTapMoveThresholdPx = 8;
  const thumbScrollMoveThresholdPx = 4;
  const readGridScrollTop = function() {
    const gridEl = state.ui && state.ui.grid;
    return gridEl ? (gridEl.scrollTop || 0) : 0;
  };
  const readWindowScrollY = function() {
    return window.pageYOffset || document.documentElement.scrollTop || 0;
  };
  const findTouchById = function(list, id) {
    if (!list || !Number.isFinite(id)) return null;
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      const t = list[i];
      if (t && t.identifier === id) return t;
    }
    return null;
  };
  const markTouchMovedByPoint = function(x, y) {
    if (!thumbTouchActive) return;
    if (!Number.isFinite(x) || !Number.isFinite(y)) return;
    if (Math.abs(x - thumbStartX) > thumbTapMoveThresholdPx || Math.abs(y - thumbStartY) > thumbTapMoveThresholdPx) {
      thumbTouchMoved = true;
    }
  };
  const markTouchMovedByScroll = function() {
    if (!thumbTouchActive) return false;
    if (Math.abs(readGridScrollTop() - thumbStartGridScrollTop) > thumbScrollMoveThresholdPx) return true;
    if (Math.abs(readWindowScrollY() - thumbStartWindowScrollY) > thumbScrollMoveThresholdPx) return true;
    return false;
  };
  const clearThumbTouchTrack = function() {
    window.removeEventListener('touchmove', onThumbTouchMoveCapture, true);
    window.removeEventListener('touchend', onThumbTouchEndCapture, true);
    window.removeEventListener('touchcancel', onThumbTouchCancelCapture, true);
    thumbTouchActive = false;
    thumbTouchId = -1;
    thumbTouchMoved = false;
    thumbStartX = 0;
    thumbStartY = 0;
    thumbStartGridScrollTop = 0;
    thumbStartWindowScrollY = 0;
  };
  const onThumbTouchMoveCapture = function(ev) {
    if (!thumbTouchActive) return;
    const t = findTouchById(ev.touches, thumbTouchId) || findTouchById(ev.changedTouches, thumbTouchId);
    if (!t) return;
    markTouchMovedByPoint(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    if (markTouchMovedByScroll()) thumbTouchMoved = true;
  };
  const onThumbTouchEndCapture = function(ev) {
    if (!thumbTouchActive) return;
    const t = findTouchById(ev.changedTouches, thumbTouchId);
    if (!t) return;
    markTouchMovedByPoint(t.clientX, t.clientY);
    if (markTouchMovedByScroll()) thumbTouchMoved = true;
    const moved = thumbTouchMoved;
    const now = Date.now();
    thumbLastTouchLikeTs = now;
    thumbIgnoreClickUntil = now + 520;
    clearThumbTouchTrack();
    if (moved) return;
    try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
    openLightboxById(item.id);
  };
  const onThumbTouchCancelCapture = function() {
    if (!thumbTouchActive) return;
    thumbIgnoreClickUntil = Date.now() + 520;
    clearThumbTouchTrack();
  };
  thumb.addEventListener('touchstart', function(ev) {
    const t = ev.changedTouches && ev.changedTouches[0];
    if (!t) return;
    clearThumbTouchTrack();
    thumbTouchActive = true;
    thumbTouchId = t.identifier;
    thumbTouchMoved = false;
    thumbStartX = t.clientX;
    thumbStartY = t.clientY;
    thumbStartGridScrollTop = readGridScrollTop();
    thumbStartWindowScrollY = readWindowScrollY();
    window.addEventListener('touchmove', onThumbTouchMoveCapture, { capture: true, passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('touchend', onThumbTouchEndCapture, { capture: true, passive: false });
    window.addEventListener('touchcancel', onThumbTouchCancelCapture, { capture: true, passive: true });
  }, { passive: true });
  thumb.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
    const now = Date.now();
    if (now < thumbIgnoreClickUntil || (now - thumbLastTouchLikeTs) < 450) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    ev.preventDefault();
    openLightboxById(item.id);
  });

  card.appendChild(thumb);
  card.appendChild(meta);
  const dup = document.createElement('div');
  dup.className = 'dup-badge';
  card.appendChild(dup);
  item.card = card;
  updateDupBadge(item);
  item.__renderUrl = (item.kind === 'img') ? getDisplayUrl(item) : item.url;
  if (item.kind !== 'img') releaseSourceEl(item, 'card');
  return card;
}

function getCardPreviewUrls(item) {
  if (item.kind === 'video') {
    const direct = getImgurDirectMedia(item.requestUrl);
    return uniqueThumbUrls([item.poster].concat(item.previewUrls || [], item.__capturedPoster || [],
      direct && direct.video ? getImgurThumbUrls(item) : []));
  }
  return getEmbedThumbUrls(item);
}

function refreshCardPreview(item, thumb) {
  if (!thumb || item.kind === 'img' || thumb.querySelector('iframe') || state.ui.disposed) return;
  const urls = getCardPreviewUrls(item);
  const key = urls.join('\n');
  if (!urls.length || item.__previewKey === key) return;
  item.__previewKey = key;
  item.__thumbCandidates = urls;
  // A successful preview remains valid when new metadata arrives.
  if (item.__thumbLoaded && item.__previewSucceeded) return;
  if (item.__thumbLoading) { item.__previewKey = ''; item.__thumbPendingRefresh = 1; return; }
  let img = item.__thumbImg;
  if (!img || !img.isConnected) {
    img = document.createElement('img');
    img.decoding = 'async'; img.alt = '';
    img.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:' + (state.env.isWide ? 180 : 140) + 'px;opacity:0';
    thumb.appendChild(img);
    item.__thumbImg = img;
  }
  img.src = BLANK_GIF;
  img.dataset.src = urls[0];
  img.style.opacity = '0';
  item.__thumbPreview = true;
  item.__thumbLoaded = false;
  thumbEnqueue(item);
}

function updateCard(item) {
  if (!item.card) return;
  item.card.dataset.kind = item.kind || '';
  setMediaCardType(item.card, item);
  item.card.dataset.oh = item.__allHidden ? '1' : '0';
  item.card.dataset.ah = item.__autoHidden ? '1' : '0';
  if (item.embedType) {
    item.card.dataset.etype = ((item.embedType || '').toUpperCase()) || 'OTHER';
  } else {
    item.card.removeAttribute('data-etype');
  }
  const thumbBtn = item.card.querySelector('.thumb');
  if (thumbBtn) {
    const playable = (item.kind === 'video' || item.kind === 'embed') && !thumbBtn.querySelector('iframe');
    thumbBtn.classList.toggle('is-playable', playable);
    if (item.kind === 'embed' && !thumbBtn.querySelector('iframe')) {
      const ph = thumbBtn.querySelector('.ph');
      if (ph) renderEmbedPlaceholder(ph, item);
    }
  }
  refreshCardPreview(item, thumbBtn);
  let mediaBadge = item.card.querySelector('.thumb .badge');
  if (!mediaBadge) {
    const badgeInfo = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
    if (badgeInfo && thumbBtn) {
      mediaBadge = document.createElement('div');
      mediaBadge.className = 'badge';
      mediaBadge.textContent = badgeInfo.text;
      mediaBadge.title = badgeInfo.full;
      mediaBadge.setAttribute('aria-label', badgeInfo.full);
      thumbBtn.appendChild(mediaBadge);
    }
  }
  if (mediaBadge) {
    const badgeInfo = getMediaBadgeInfo(item);
    if (badgeInfo) {
      mediaBadge.textContent = badgeInfo.text;
      mediaBadge.title = badgeInfo.full;
      mediaBadge.setAttribute('aria-label', badgeInfo.full);
    }
  }
  let img = item.__thumbImg;
  if (!img || !img.isConnected) {
    img = item.card.querySelector('img');
    if (img) item.__thumbImg = img;
  }
  const displayUrl = getDisplayUrl(item);
  const shownSrc = img ? (img.currentSrc || img.src || '') : '';
  const requestedSrc = (img && img.dataset) ? (img.dataset.src || '') : '';
  const exactSource = !!item.requestUrl;
  const sameSource = exactSource ? isSameLoadedSrc : isSameImageVariantUrl;
  const sameShownAsDisplay = !!(shownSrc && sameSource(shownSrc, displayUrl));
  const sameRequestedAsDisplay = !!(requestedSrc && sameSource(requestedSrc, displayUrl));
  const urlChanged = !!(item.__renderUrl && !sameSource(item.__renderUrl, displayUrl));
  const loadingWithSwitch = !!(item.__thumbLoading && !sameRequestedAsDisplay && urlChanged);
  if (loadingWithSwitch) item.__thumbPendingRefresh = 1;
  const keepCurrentLoad = !!(item.__thumbLoading && (sameRequestedAsDisplay || loadingWithSwitch));
  const needReload = !!(img && !keepCurrentLoad && !sameShownAsDisplay &&
    (urlChanged || !item.__thumbLoaded || isSameLoadedSrc(shownSrc, BLANK_GIF)));
  if (img && item.kind === 'img') {
    img.dataset.src = displayUrl;
  }
  if (img && item.kind === 'img' && needReload) {
    try {
      if (item.__thumbLoading && item.__thumbCtx && typeof item.__thumbCtx.abort === 'function') {
        item.__thumbCtx.abort('updateCard');
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    item.__thumbCtx = null;
    img.src = BLANK_GIF;
    img.style.opacity = '0';
    item.__thumbLoaded = false;
    item.__thumbLoading = false;
    item.__thumbRetry = 0;
    clearOverlayShowTimer(item);
    hideThumbOverlay(item.card);
    thumbEnqueue(item);
  }
  const page = item.card.querySelector('.chip-page');
  if (page) page.textContent = item.pageLabel || 'P1';
  const dim = item.card.querySelector('.meta div');
  if (dim) dim.textContent = formatItemMetaText(item);
  const open = item.card.querySelector('a.open');
  if (open) open.href = allowOpen(item.openUrl) ? item.openUrl : item.url;
  updateDupBadge(item);
  item.__renderUrl = displayUrl;
}

function updateDupBadge(item) {
  if (!item.card) return;
  let badge = item.card.querySelector('.dup-badge');
  if (!badge) {
    badge = document.createElement('div');
    badge.className = 'dup-badge';
    item.card.appendChild(badge);
  }
  if (!badge.__dupClickBound) {
    badge.__dupClickBound = true;
    badge.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      const info = badge.dataset.info || badge.title || '';
      if (!info) return;
      if (navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === 'function') {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(info).then(function() {
          if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DUP DETAIL', 700);
        }).catch(function() {
          try { prompt('DUP DETAIL (copy)', info); } catch (e) {}
          if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DUP DETAIL', 700);
        });
      } else {
        try { prompt('DUP DETAIL (copy)', info); } catch (e) {}
        if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DUP DETAIL', 700);
      }
    });
  }
  if (item.dupCount >= 2) {
    addDupClusterUrl(item, item.url || item.abs || '');
    const hostCounts = getDupClusterHostCounts(item);
    const hostCount = Object.keys(hostCounts).length;
    badge.textContent = hostCount >= 2 ? ('×' + item.dupCount + ' H' + hostCount) : ('×' + item.dupCount);
    const detail = buildDupClusterDetail(item, 8);
    badge.title = detail;
    badge.dataset.info = detail;
    badge.style.display = 'block';
  } else {
    badge.title = '';
    badge.dataset.info = '';
    badge.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

/* [12] lightbox */
function formatLightboxInfo(item) {
  if (!item) return '';
  let host = item.host || '';
  if (!host) {
    try { host = new URL(item.openUrl || item.url || item.abs || '', location.href).hostname || ''; } catch (e) {}
  }
  const kind = item.kind === 'embed'
    ? ((item.embedType || 'EMBED').toUpperCase())
    : String(item.kind || '').toUpperCase();
  const lines = [];
  if (item.pageLabel) lines.push(item.pageLabel);
  if (kind) lines.push(kind);
  if (item.width && item.height) lines.push(item.width + ' × ' + item.height);
  if (host) lines.push(host);
  if ((item.dupCount || 1) > 1) lines.push('dup ×' + item.dupCount);
  return lines.join('\n');
}

function updateLightboxChrome() {
  const item = state.lb.list[state.lb.index];
  if (state.lb.counter) {
    const current = state.lb.list.length ? (state.lb.index + 1) : 0;
    const text = current + ' / ' + state.lb.list.length;
    setTextIfChanged(state.lb.counter, text);
    setAttrIfChanged(state.lb.counter, 'aria-label', 'メディア ' + current + ' / ' + state.lb.list.length);
  }
  if (state.lb.infoPanel) {
    if (state.lb.infoOpen) setTextIfChanged(state.lb.infoPanel, formatLightboxInfo(item));
    if (state.lb.infoPanel.hidden === state.lb.infoOpen) state.lb.infoPanel.hidden = !state.lb.infoOpen;
  }
  if (state.lb.infoBtn) {
    setAttrIfChanged(state.lb.infoBtn, 'aria-expanded', state.lb.infoOpen ? 'true' : 'false');
    setAttrIfChanged(state.lb.infoBtn, 'aria-label', state.lb.infoOpen ? '詳細を閉じる' : '詳細を表示');
  }
}

function getLightboxFocusable() {
  const lb = state.lb && state.lb.overlay;
  if (!lb || !lb.querySelectorAll) return [];
  return Array.from(lb.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]), a[href], iframe, video[controls], [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'))
    .filter(function(el) {
      if (!el || !el.isConnected) return false;
      try {
        const cs = getComputedStyle(el);
        return cs.display !== 'none' && cs.visibility !== 'hidden' && el.getClientRects().length > 0;
      } catch (e) {
        return true;
      }
    });
}

function createLightbox() {
  const lb = document.createElement('div');
  lb.className = 'lb';
  lb.style.setProperty('z-index', '2', 'important');
  lb.style.setProperty('pointer-events', 'auto', 'important');
  lb.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
  lb.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
  lb.setAttribute('aria-label', 'メディアビューア');
  lb.tabIndex = -1;

  function bindLbTap(el, handler) {
    if (!el || typeof handler !== 'function') return;
    let lastTouchLikeTs = 0;
    let lastNonClickTs = 0;
    let lastNonClickType = '';
    const onTap = function(ev) {
      const now = Date.now();
      if (ev.type === 'click' && ev.detail !== 0 && (now - lastTouchLikeTs) < 450) {
        try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
        return;
      }
      if (ev.type !== 'click') {
        if (ev.type !== lastNonClickType && (now - lastNonClickTs) < 120) {
          try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
          return;
        }
        lastNonClickTs = now;
        lastNonClickType = ev.type;
        lastTouchLikeTs = now;
        try { ev.preventDefault(); } catch (e) {}
      }
      handler(ev);
    };
    el.addEventListener('click', onTap);
    el.addEventListener('pointerup', onTap);
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onTap, { passive: false });
  }

  const frame = document.createElement('div');
  frame.className = 'lb-frame';

  const badge = document.createElement('div');
  badge.className = 'lb-badge';
  badge.textContent = '';
  badge.style.display = 'none';
  if (!badge.__lbClickBound) {
    badge.__lbClickBound = true;
    badge.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      const info = badge.dataset.info || badge.title || '';
      if (!info) return;
      if (navigator.clipboard && typeof navigator.clipboard.writeText === 'function') {
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(info).then(function() {
          if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DETAIL', 700);
        }).catch(function() {
          try { prompt('VIDEO FAIL info (copy)', info); } catch (e) {}
          if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DETAIL', 700);
        });
      } else {
        try { prompt('VIDEO FAIL info (copy)', info); } catch (e) {}
        if (typeof setInfoTemp === 'function') setInfoTemp('DETAIL', 700);
      }
    });
  }
  frame.appendChild(badge);

  const counter = document.createElement('div');
  counter.className = 'lb-counter';
  counter.setAttribute('role', 'status');
  counter.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
  counter.setAttribute('aria-atomic', 'true');
  counter.textContent = '0 / 0';

  const infoPanel = document.createElement('div');
  infoPanel.className = 'lb-info-panel';
  infoPanel.id = '__fmp_lb_info__';
  infoPanel.hidden = true;

  const controls = document.createElement('div');
  controls.className = 'lb-controls';
  controls.setAttribute('role', 'group');
  controls.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Lightbox操作');

  const prevBtn = mkBtn('‹ 前', 'lb-btn');
  const nextBtn = mkBtn('次 ›', 'lb-btn');
  const infoBtn = mkBtn('ⓘ', 'lb-btn');
  const openBtn = mkBtn('↗', 'lb-btn');
  const closeBtn = mkBtn('閉じる', 'lb-btn');
  const zoomBtn = mkBtn('拡大', 'lb-btn lb-zoom-btn');
  zoomBtn.hidden = true;
  zoomBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '画像を2倍に拡大');
  zoomBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');
  prevBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '前のメディア');
  nextBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '次のメディア');
  infoBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '詳細を表示');
  infoBtn.setAttribute('aria-controls', infoPanel.id);
  infoBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  openBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', '元ページを開く');
  closeBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Lightboxを閉じる');

  controls.appendChild(prevBtn);
  controls.appendChild(nextBtn);
  controls.appendChild(closeBtn);
  controls.appendChild(zoomBtn);
  controls.appendChild(infoBtn);
  controls.appendChild(openBtn);

  lb.appendChild(counter);
  lb.appendChild(infoPanel);
  lb.appendChild(frame);
  lb.appendChild(controls);
  state.ui.wrap.appendChild(lb);

  bindLbTap(zoomBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopPropagation();
    toggleLightboxZoom();
  });
  bindLbTap(prevBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    moveLightbox(-1);
  });
  bindLbTap(nextBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    moveLightbox(1);
  });
  bindLbTap(infoBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopPropagation();
    state.lb.infoOpen = !state.lb.infoOpen;
    updateLightboxChrome();
  });
  bindLbTap(closeBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    closeLightbox();
  });
  bindLbTap(openBtn, function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopPropagation();
    const it = state.lb.list[state.lb.index];
    if (it) openExternal(it.openUrl, 'lightbox');
  });
  bindLbTap(lb, function(ev) {
    if (ev.target === lb) closeLightbox();
  });

  bindLightboxZoomGestures(frame);

  const onKeyDown = function(ev) {
    if (!state.lb.open) return;
    const target = ev.target && ev.target.nodeType === 1 ? ev.target : null;
    const editable = !!(target && (
      target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
      target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
      target.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
      target.isContentEditable
    ));

    if (ev.key === 'Escape') {
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
      closeLightbox();
      return;
    }
    if (!editable && ev.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
      ev.preventDefault();
      moveLightbox(-1);
      return;
    }
    if (!editable && ev.key === 'ArrowRight') {
      ev.preventDefault();
      moveLightbox(1);
      return;
    }
    if (!editable && activeLightboxZoom() && (ev.key === '+' || ev.key === '=' || ev.key === '0' || ev.key === '-')) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      if (ev.key === '0' || ev.key === '-') {
        const z = activeLightboxZoom();
        setLightboxZoom(z, 1, 0, 0);
      } else toggleLightboxZoom();
      return;
    }
    if (ev.key !== 'Tab') return;

    const focusable = getLightboxFocusable();
    if (!focusable.length) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      try { lb.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { try { lb.focus(); } catch (e2) {} }
      return;
    }
    const rootActive = state.ui.shadow && state.ui.shadow.activeElement
      ? state.ui.shadow.activeElement
      : document.activeElement;
    const first = focusable[0];
    const last = focusable[focusable.length - 1];
    if (ev.shiftKey && (rootActive === first || rootActive === lb)) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      try { last.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { try { last.focus(); } catch (e2) {} }
    } else if (!ev.shiftKey && rootActive === last) {
      ev.preventDefault();
      try { first.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { try { first.focus(); } catch (e2) {} }
    }
  };
  lb.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown, true);

  state.lb.overlay = lb;
  state.lb.frame = frame;
  state.lb.badge = badge;
  state.lb.counter = counter;
  state.lb.infoPanel = infoPanel;
  state.lb.infoBtn = infoBtn;
  state.lb.openBtn = openBtn;
  state.lb.closeBtn = closeBtn;
  state.lb.zoomBtn = zoomBtn;
  state.lb.onKeyDown = onKeyDown;
}

function resetLightboxZoom() {
  const z = state.lb.zoom;
  if (z) {
    if (z.raf) cancelAnimationFrame(z.raf);
    if (z.layer) z.layer.style.removeProperty('transform');
    state.lb.zoom = null;
  }
  const btn = state.lb.zoomBtn;
  if (btn) {
    if (btn.parentElement) btn.parentElement.classList.remove('zoom-ready');
    btn.hidden = true;
    setTextIfChanged(btn, '拡大');
    setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-label', '画像を2倍に拡大');
    setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-pressed', 'false');
  }
}

function createLightboxZoom(layer, img, token) {
  state.lb.zoom = {layer:layer, img:img, token:token, scale:1, x:0, y:0, raf:0, gesture:null, tap:null, ready:false};
}

function currentLightboxZoom() {
  const z = state.lb.zoom;
  return z && state.lb.open && !state.ui.disposed &&
    isLightboxTokenCurrent(z.token) && z.layer.isConnected ? z : null;
}

function activeLightboxZoom() {
  const z = currentLightboxZoom();
  return z && z.ready ? z : null;
}

function setLightboxZoom(z, scale, x, y) {
  if (activeLightboxZoom() !== z) return;
  scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, scale));
  const w = z.width || z.layer.offsetWidth;
  const h = z.height || z.layer.offsetHeight;
  if (!w || !h) return;
  const maxX = w * (scale - 1) / 2, maxY = h * (scale - 1) / 2;
  z.scale = scale;
  z.x = Math.max(-maxX, Math.min(maxX, x));
  z.y = Math.max(-maxY, Math.min(maxY, y));
  if (!z.raf) z.raf = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    z.raf = 0;
    if (activeLightboxZoom() !== z) return;
    if (z.scale === 1) z.layer.style.removeProperty('transform');
    else z.layer.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + z.x + 'px,' + z.y + 'px,0) scale(' + z.scale + ')';
    const btn = state.lb.zoomBtn;
    setTextIfChanged(btn, z.scale > 1 ? '等倍' : '拡大');
    setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-label', z.scale > 1 ? '画像を等倍に戻す' : '画像を2倍に拡大');
    setAttrIfChanged(btn, 'aria-pressed', String(z.scale > 1));
  });
}

function measureLightboxZoom(z) {
  const r = state.lb.frame.getBoundingClientRect();
  z.width = z.layer.offsetWidth;
  z.height = z.layer.offsetHeight;
  z.cx = r.left + r.width / 2;
  z.cy = r.top + r.height / 2;
}

function toggleLightboxZoom(clientX, clientY) {
  const z = activeLightboxZoom();
  if (!z) return;
  measureLightboxZoom(z);
  const scale = z.scale > 1 ? 1 : 2;
  const x = Number.isFinite(clientX) ? clientX - z.cx : 0;
  const y = Number.isFinite(clientY) ? clientY - z.cy : 0;
  z.gesture = null;
  z.tap = null;
  setLightboxZoom(z, scale, -x * (scale - 1), -y * (scale - 1));
}

function resetLightboxZoomOnResize() {
  const z = activeLightboxZoom();
  if (!z) return;
  z.gesture = null;
  z.tap = null;
  measureLightboxZoom(z);
  setLightboxZoom(z, 1, 0, 0);
}

function bindLightboxZoomGestures(frame) {
  const targetAllowed = function(ev) {
    const t = ev.target;
    return !t || !t.closest || !t.closest('.lb-badge');
  };
  const distance = function(a,b) { return Math.hypot(a.clientX-b.clientX,a.clientY-b.clientY); };
  const beginPinch = function(z, touches) {
    const a=touches[0],b=touches[1];
    z.tap = null;
    z.gesture = {kind:'pinch', distance:Math.max(1,distance(a,b)),scale:z.scale,x:z.x,y:z.y,
      mx:(a.clientX+b.clientX)/2-z.cx,my:(a.clientY+b.clientY)/2-z.cy};
  };
  frame.addEventListener('touchstart', function(ev) {
    const z = currentLightboxZoom();
    if (!z || !targetAllowed(ev)) return;
    if (z.ready) measureLightboxZoom(z);
    if (ev.touches.length === 2) {
      if (!z.ready) { z.gesture=null; z.tap=null; return; }
      beginPinch(z,ev.touches);
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    } else if (ev.touches.length === 1) {
      const t=ev.touches[0];
      z.gesture={kind:'single',id:t.identifier,x:t.clientX,y:t.clientY,ox:z.x,oy:z.y,scale:z.scale,at:Date.now(),moved:false};
    } else { z.gesture=null; z.tap=null; }
  }, {passive:false});
  frame.addEventListener('touchmove', function(ev) {
    const z=currentLightboxZoom();
    if (!z || !z.gesture) return;
    const g=z.gesture;
    if (ev.touches.length > 2) { z.gesture=null; z.tap=null; return; }
    if (ev.touches.length === 2) {
      if (!z.ready) { z.gesture=null; z.tap=null; return; }
      if (g.kind !== 'pinch') beginPinch(z,ev.touches);
      const pin=z.gesture,a=ev.touches[0],b=ev.touches[1];
      const scale=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,pin.scale*distance(a,b)/pin.distance));
      const ratio=scale/pin.scale;
      const mx=(a.clientX+b.clientX)/2-z.cx,my=(a.clientY+b.clientY)/2-z.cy;
      setLightboxZoom(z,scale,mx-(pin.mx-pin.x)*ratio,my-(pin.my-pin.y)*ratio);
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    } else if (ev.touches.length === 1 && g.kind === 'single') {
      const t=ev.touches[0],dx=t.clientX-g.x,dy=t.clientY-g.y;
      if (Math.hypot(dx,dy)>10) { g.moved=true; z.tap=null; }
      if (g.scale>1) {
        setLightboxZoom(z,g.scale,g.ox+dx,g.oy+dy);
        if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
      }
    }
  }, {passive:false});
  frame.addEventListener('touchend', function(ev) {
    const z=currentLightboxZoom();
    if (!z || !z.gesture) return;
    const g=z.gesture;
    if (g.kind==='pinch') {
      // Do not turn the remaining finger after a pinch into a swipe.
      if (!ev.touches.length) z.gesture=null;
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
      return;
    }
    z.gesture=null;
    const t=Array.from(ev.changedTouches||[]).find(function(v){return v.identifier===g.id;});
    if (!t || ev.touches.length) return;
    const dx=t.clientX-g.x,dy=t.clientY-g.y,dt=Date.now()-g.at;
    if (g.scale===1 && z.scale===1 && dt<=900 && Math.abs(dx)>=48 && Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)*1.25) {
      z.tap=null;
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
      perfCount('lb.swipe');
      moveLightbox(dx<0?1:-1);
      return;
    }
    if (z.ready && !g.moved && dt<=300 && Math.hypot(dx,dy)<=10) {
      const last=z.tap,now=Date.now();
      if (last && now-last.at<=320 && Math.hypot(t.clientX-last.x,t.clientY-last.y)<=24) {
        toggleLightboxZoom(t.clientX,t.clientY);
      } else z.tap={at:now,x:t.clientX,y:t.clientY};
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    } else z.tap=null;
  }, {passive:false});
  frame.addEventListener('touchcancel', function() {
    const z=state.lb.zoom;
    if (z) { z.gesture=null; z.tap=null; }
  }, {passive:true});
  frame.addEventListener('dblclick', function(ev) {
    if (!targetAllowed(ev) || !activeLightboxZoom()) return;
    ev.preventDefault();
    toggleLightboxZoom(ev.clientX,ev.clientY);
  });
}

function buildLightboxList() {
  syncMediaOrder();
  const list = [];
  for (const card of state.ui.grid.querySelectorAll('.card')) {
    if (card.classList.contains('is-hidden') || card.classList.contains('is-error')) continue;
    list.push(state.items.byId.get(card.dataset.id));
  }
  state.lb.list = list;
}

function openLightboxById(id) {
  buildLightboxList();
  const idx = state.lb.list.findIndex(function(it) { return it && it.id === id; });
  if (idx < 0) return;
  const card = state.ui.grid ? state.ui.grid.querySelector('.card[data-id="' + id + '"]') : null;
  state.lb.invoker = card ? (card.querySelector('.thumb') || card) : null;
  state.lb.index = idx;
  state.lb.infoOpen = false;
  if (state.thumb) state.thumb.paused = true;
  renderLightboxItem();
  state.lb.overlay.classList.add('open');
  state.lb.open = true;
  requestAnimationFrame(function() {
    if (state.ui.disposed || !state.lb.open) return;
    const target = state.lb.closeBtn || state.lb.overlay;
    try { target.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) { try { target.focus(); } catch (e2) {} }
  });
}

function moveLightbox(dir) {
  if (!state.lb.list.length) return;
  let next = state.lb.index + dir;
  if (next < 0) next = state.lb.list.length - 1;
  if (next >= state.lb.list.length) next = 0;
  state.lb.index = next;
  renderLightboxItem();
}

function clearLightboxFrame() {
  const frame = state.lb.frame;
  while (frame.childNodes.length > 1) {
    frame.removeChild(frame.lastChild);
  }
}

function resetLightboxLoad(reason) {
  const ctx = state.lb ? state.lb.__ctx : null;
  if (ctx && typeof ctx.abort === 'function') {
    try { ctx.abort(reason || 'reset'); } catch (e) {}
  }
  try {
    cleanupLightboxPreloads(reason || 'reset');
  } catch (e) {}
  if (state.lb) {
    state.lb.__ctx = null;
    state.lb.__loading = false;
    state.lb.__token = (state.lb.__token || 0) + 1;
    return state.lb.__token;
  }
  return 0;
}

function isLightboxTokenCurrent(token) {
  return !!(state.lb && token && state.lb.__token === token);
}

function cleanupLightboxPreloads(reason) {
  try {
    const list = state.lb && state.lb.__preloadCtxs ? state.lb.__preloadCtxs : [];
    if (list && list.length) {
      list.forEach(function(ctx) {
        if (!ctx) return;
        ctx.aborted = true;
        try {
          if (ctx.img) {
            ctx.img.onload = null;
            ctx.img.onerror = null;
            try { ctx.img.removeAttribute('src'); } catch (e2) {}
          }
        } catch (e) {}
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (state.lb) {
      state.lb.__preloadCtxs = [];
      state.lb.__preloadKey = '';
    }
  } catch (e) {}
}

function getLightboxNeighborIndex(dir) {
  const list = state.lb && state.lb.list ? state.lb.list : [];
  if (!list.length) return -1;
  if (state.lb.index < 0) return -1;
  if (list.length < 2) return -1;
  let idx = state.lb.index + dir;
  if (idx < 0) idx = list.length - 1;
  if (idx >= list.length) idx = 0;
  if (idx === state.lb.index) return -1;
  return idx;
}

function getLightboxPreloadUrl(item) {
  if (!item) return '';
  if (item.kind !== 'img') return '';
  const url = getDisplayUrl(item) || item.openUrl || item.url || '';
  if (!url) return '';
  if (isSameLoadedSrc(url, BLANK_GIF)) return '';
  return url;
}

function startLightboxImagePreload(item, dir, token) {
  const url = getLightboxPreloadUrl(item);
  if (!url) return null;
  const img = new Image();
  const ctx = {
    img: img,
    url: url,
    dir: dir,
    token: token,
    done: false,
    aborted: false
  };

  img.decoding = 'async';
  img.fetchPriority = 'low';
  img.onload = function() {
    ctx.done = true;
  };
  img.onerror = function() {
    ctx.done = true;
  };

  try { img.src = url; } catch (e) { img.src = url; }
  return ctx;
}

function preloadLightboxNeighbors(token) {
  if (!state.lb) return;
  const list = state.lb.list || [];
  if (!list.length) return;
  if (state.lb.index < 0) return;
  const key = String(token || 0) + ':' + state.lb.index + ':' + list.length;
  if (state.lb.__preloadKey === key) return;

  cleanupLightboxPreloads('preload-refresh');
  state.lb.__preloadKey = key;
  state.lb.__preloadCtxs = [];

  const seen = new Set();
  [-1, 1].forEach(function(dir) {
    const idx = getLightboxNeighborIndex(dir);
    if (idx < 0) return;
    const item = list[idx];
    const url = getLightboxPreloadUrl(item);
    if (!url) return;
    if (seen.has(url)) return;
    seen.add(url);
    const ctx = startLightboxImagePreload(item, dir, token);
    if (ctx) state.lb.__preloadCtxs.push(ctx);
  });
}

function renderLightboxItem() {
  const item = state.lb.list[state.lb.index];
  if (!item) return;
  perfCount('lb.render');
  if (state.lb.overlay) {
    state.lb.overlay.dataset.kind = item.kind || '';
    if (item.embedType) {
      state.lb.overlay.dataset.etype = ((item.embedType || '').toUpperCase()) || 'OTHER';
    } else {
      state.lb.overlay.removeAttribute('data-etype');
    }
  }
  const prevId = state.lb.currentId;
  if (state.wd && prevId !== item.id) {
    state.wd.lbKickCount = 0;
  }
  state.lb.currentId = item.id;
  updateLightboxChrome();
  resetLightboxZoom();
  const lbToken = resetLightboxLoad('switch');
  clearLightboxFrame();
  state.lb.badge.style.display = 'none';
  if (item.kind === 'video') {
    const token = lbToken;
    const v = document.createElement('video');
    let host = 'unknown';
    try { host = new URL(item.url || '', location.href).hostname || 'unknown'; } catch (e) {}
    const describeMediaError = function(err) {
      if (!err) return 'unknown';
      if (typeof err.message === 'string' && err.message) return err.message;
      const code = typeof err.code === 'number' ? err.code : 0;
      if (code === 1) return 'aborted';
      if (code === 2) return 'network';
      if (code === 3) return 'decode';
      if (code === 4) return 'src-not-supported';
      return code ? ('code-' + code) : 'unknown';
    };
    const escapeHtml = function(text) {
      return String(text || '')
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
    };
    const fallbackVideoViaSrcdoc = function(url) {
      if (!url) return;
      const frame = state.lb.frame;
      if (!frame) return;
      if (v && v.parentNode === frame) {
        frame.removeChild(v);
      } else if (v) {
        v.style.display = 'none';
      }
      const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
      iframe.className = 'lb-embed lb-video-fallback';
      iframe.loading = 'eager';
      iframe.setAttribute('referrerpolicy', 'no-referrer');
      iframe.setAttribute('allow', 'autoplay; fullscreen; picture-in-picture');
      iframe.addEventListener('load', function() {
        item.__lbFallbackPending = false;
        markVideoSuccess();
      });
      const safeUrl = escapeHtml(url);
      iframe.srcdoc = [
        '<!doctype html><html><head>',
        '<meta charset="utf-8">',
        '<meta name="referrer" content="no-referrer">',
        '<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">',
        '<style>',
        state.cfg.dmEmbedSurfaceEnabled ? ':root{color-scheme:dark}' : '',
        'html,body{margin:0;height:100%;background:#000}video{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain}</style>',
        '</head><body>',
        '<video controls playsinline webkit-playsinline preload="metadata" src="', safeUrl, '"></video>',
        '<a href="', safeUrl, '" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" style="position:fixed;bottom:6px;right:8px;color:#9cf;font-size:11px;text-decoration:none;opacity:0.7">Open</a>',
        '</body></html>'
      ].join('');
      frame.appendChild(iframe);
      item.__lbFallbackPending = true;
      clearFallbackPendingTimer();
      fallbackPendingTimer = setTimeout(function() {
        fallbackPendingTimer = 0;
        item.__lbFallbackPending = false;
        if (!videoSuccess && videoFailCandidate) {
          videoFailCandidate.explicitFinal = true;
          confirmFinalVideoFail();
        }
      }, 1200);
    };
    let videoFailTimer = 0;
    let videoFailCandidate = null;
    let videoSuccess = false;
    let videoAborted = false;
    let noSourceTimer = 0;
    let fallbackPendingTimer = 0;
    const clearVideoFailTimer = function() {
      if (!videoFailTimer) return;
      clearTimeout(videoFailTimer);
      videoFailTimer = 0;
    };
    const clearNoSourceTimer = function() {
      if (!noSourceTimer) return;
      clearTimeout(noSourceTimer);
      noSourceTimer = 0;
    };
    const clearFallbackPendingTimer = function() {
      if (!fallbackPendingTimer) return;
      clearTimeout(fallbackPendingTimer);
      fallbackPendingTimer = 0;
    };
    const hideVideoFailBadge = function() {
      if (!state.lb.badge) return;
      state.lb.badge.style.display = 'none';
    };
    const markVideoSuccess = function() {
      if (!isLightboxTokenCurrent(token)) return;
      if (videoSuccess) return;
      videoSuccess = true;
      perfCount('lb.video.success');
      perfMarkOnce('lb.firstReveal');
      videoFailCandidate = null;
      clearVideoFailTimer();
      clearNoSourceTimer();
      clearFallbackPendingTimer();
      hideVideoFailBadge();
    };
    const showVideoFail = function(info) {
      if (!isLightboxTokenCurrent(token)) return;
      perfCount('lb.video.error');
      state.lb.badge.textContent = 'VIDEO FAIL\n' + info;
      state.lb.badge.title = info;
      state.lb.badge.setAttribute('aria-label', 'VIDEO FAIL ' + info);
      state.lb.badge.dataset.info = info;
